let loopStarted=false,bootRequested=false;
function finishBoot(){if(!loopStarted){bootRequested=true;return;}if(bootDone)return;bootDone=true;home();$('boot').classList.add('exit');setTimeout(()=>visibility('boot',false),save.motion?550:20);} $('boot-skip').onclick=finishBoot;
function initializeGame(){if(loopStarted)return;if(typeof draw!=='function')throw new Error('Renderer is not available');resize();draw(0);loopStarted=true;last=performance.now();requestAnimationFrame(frame);if(bootRequested)finishBoot();else Promise.resolve(document.fonts?document.fonts.ready:undefined).then(()=>setTimeout(finishBoot,save.motion?2400:150));}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',initializeGame,{once:true});else initializeGame();
// Pace both rendering and frame services. Physics remains fixed at 60 Hz.
// Carry the deadline remainder so 90/120 Hz callbacks cannot double the work.
let nextFrame=0;
function frame(t){requestAnimationFrame(frame);const interval=1000/(['drive','loading','unloading'].includes(mode)?60:30);if(t<nextFrame-2)return;nextFrame+=interval;if(nextFrame<t)nextFrame=t+interval;let dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(.05,(t-last)/1000||0));last=Math.max(last,t);clock+=dt;if(mode==='drive'&&sim){acc+=dt;while(acc+1e-9>=1/60){sim.previous=new Map();for(let b=sim.world.getBodyList();b;b=b.getNext()){let p=b.getPosition();sim.previous.set(b,{x:p.x,y:p.y,a:b.getAngle()});}sim.step(gas&&pedalController?pedalController.throttle():gas,brake);acc=Math.max(0,acc-1/60);if(sim.state!=='drive'){finish();break;}}for(let ev of sim.events.splice(0)){if(mode!=='drive')continue;roadsideEvent(ev);tankerEvent(ev);crossingEvent(ev);if(typeof recoveryEvent==='function')recoveryEvent(ev);if(ev.type==='pumpHit'){toast('УДАР · НАСОС −'+ev.damage+'%');if(save.sound)RoadAudio.hit(.6);}if(ev.type==='machineHit'){toast('УДАР · ГЕНЕРАТОР −'+ev.damage+'%');if(save.sound)RoadAudio.hit(.6);}if(ev.type==='sector'){toast(ev.physical?(ev.passed?'ЧИСТЫЙ УЧАСТОК +'+ev.reward:'УЧАСТОК ПРОЙДЕН · ПРЕМИЯ УПУЩЕНА'):(ev.passed?'ЧИСТЫЙ СПУСК +'+ev.reward:'СПУСК ПРОЙДЕН · ПРЕМИЯ УПУЩЕНА'));}if(ev.type==='winch'){toast('ЛЕБЁДКА · '+sim.winchCharges+' ОСТАЛОСЬ');}if(ev.type==='survey'){toast('КОНТРОЛЬНАЯ ТОЧКА ПРИНЯТА');}if(ev.type==='stopApproach'){sayDriver('Жёлтая площадка: остановись и держи тормоз.','focus',true);}if(ev.type==='stopComplete'){toast((ev.litres?'ПЕРЕКАЧАНО '+ev.litres+' Л · ':'ГРУЗ ПРИНЯТ · ')+(ev.index+1)+'/'+sim.stops.length);if(save.sound)RoadAudio.event('coin');driverMood='happy';driverMoodUntil=sim.time+2;}if(ev.type==='coin'&&save.sound)RoadAudio.event('coin');if(ev.type==='fuel'){toast('ТОПЛИВО +'+ev.amount+'%');if(save.sound)RoadAudio.event('fuel');driverMood='happy';driverMoodUntil=sim.time+2;}if(ev.type==='impact'){toast('УДАР ПО ПОДВЕСКЕ −'+ev.damage+'%');if(save.sound)RoadAudio.hit(1);sayDriver('Жёсткая посадка. Сбавь ход.','worried',true);}if(ev.type==='strap'){toast('РЕМЕНЬ СОРВАН');sayDriver('Ремень сорван. Береги груз.','worried',true);}if(ev.type==='lost'){toast(sim.route.cargo==='logs'?'БРЕВНО ПОТЕРЯНО':'ЯЩИК ПОТЕРЯН');if(save.sound)RoadAudio.event('lost');sayDriver(sim.route.cargo==='logs'?'Бревно потеряно. Сбавь ход.':'Ящик потерян. Сбавь ход.','worried',true);}}let p=pose(sim.car),speed=sim.car.getLinearVelocity().x;if(sim.ridge28){const view=CP.ridge28.camera(sim,W,H);scale+=(view.scale-scale)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*(view.scale<scale?9:2.2)));cam.x+=(view.x-cam.x)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*5));cam.y+=(view.y-cam.y)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*6));}else{const bridgeView=sim.nextLiftBridge(),bridgeFocus=!!bridgeView&&Math.abs(p.x-bridgeView.control)<18;const baseScale=sim.edition===25?Math.min(H/8,W/22):sim.edition===23?Math.min(H/10.3,W/29):sim.vehicle.axles?Math.min(H/10.3,W/29):Math.min(H/8.8,W/23),targetScale=Math.min(baseScale*(1-Math.min(.08,Math.max(0,Math.abs(speed)-3)*.009)),bridgeFocus?Math.min(H/13.5,W/34):Infinity);scale+=(targetScale-scale)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*2));cam.x+=(p.x+(sim.edition===25?Math.max(-1,Math.min(2.2,speed*.24)):sim.edition===23?-3.5:bridgeFocus?4.8:Math.max(-2,Math.min(4.2,speed*.48)))-cam.x)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*5));const ahead=CP.height(p.x+Math.max(0,speed)*.6,sim.roadId),ground=CP.height(p.x,sim.roadId);cam.y+=(Math.max(p.y,ground+1.0)+(sim.edition===25?.2:.8)+Math.max(-.6,Math.min(.9,(ahead-ground)*.13))-cam.y)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*3.4));}if(t-lastHud>95){hud();lastHud=t;}if(sim.time>7)$('hint').style.display='none';updateCoach(dt);RoadAudio.update(sim,save.sound&&mode==='drive',gas,brake);checkpointRun();}else RoadAudio.mute();if(toastTime>0){toastTime-=dt;if(toastTime<=0)$('toast').style.opacity=0;}updateDispatch(dt);RoadAudio.updateMusic(mode,save.sound&&save.music&&!document.hidden);draw(dt);if(sim&&['drive','loading','unloading'].includes(mode))drawRoadProfile();}
wallet();

'use strict';
(function(root){
 function create({routes,profiles,zones,landmarks,gaps,height}){
  const key='ridge:28',length=430;
  // The original 447-metre challenge remains an exact prefix. Each extension
  // has its own approach, landing room and level rest area.
  routes[key]={...routes['ridge:25'],name:'Гребень · большая серия',length};
  profiles[key]=[...profiles['ridge:25'].map(p=>p.slice()),[170,0],
   [183,3.2],[190,3.2],[197,.8],[202,.8],[208,-1.2],[213,-1.2],
   [228,4.6],[236,4.6],[244,1.4],[250,1.4],[258,0],[278,0],
   [296,6.0],[303,6.0],[315,.7],[321,.7],[329,3.3],[335,3.3],
   [344,-.6],[351,-.6],[369,5.7],[376,5.7],[385,2.0],[391,2.0],
   [399,-.4],[405,-.4],[416,0],[430,0]];
  gaps[key]={...gaps['ridge:25']};
  zones[key]=[{from:214,to:225,type:'gravel',friction:1.0,roughness:.06},{from:353,to:365,type:'gravel',friction:.98,roughness:.07}];landmarks[key]=[];
  const camps=[
   {x:82,value:100,next:250,name:'Первый привал',nextName:'За гребнем',tip:'Разгон до подъёма. Перед краем отпускай газ и лови посадку.'},
   {x:155,value:250,next:450,name:'У старого финиша',nextName:'Каменные полки',tip:'Два коротких спуска и подъём. Тормози до края, затем дай машине выкатиться.'},
   {x:270,value:450,next:750,name:'Перед перевалом',nextName:'Высокий перевал',tip:'Длинный подъём и несколько посадок подряд. Не разгоняйся на вершинах.'}
  ];
  const distance=length*3;
  function init(s){s.roadId=key;s.ridge28={edition:28,camp:0};return {...routes[key],boxes:s.ridge.load};}
  function valid(d){const r=d?.ridge28,q=d?.ridge25;if(!r||r.edition!==28||Object.keys(r).some(k=>!['edition','camp'].includes(k))||!Number.isInteger(r.camp)||r.camp<0||r.camp>3||!q||!d.groups||d.journey||d.expedition||d.waterRun||d.recoveryRun||d.course26||d.driving27)return false;
   if(r.camp===0)return q.stage===0&&!q.waiting;
   if(r.camp===1)return q.waiting?q.stage===0:q.stage===1;
   return q.stage===1;
  }
  function offer(s){return camps[s.ridge28.camp-1]||null;}
  function bankValue(s){return offer(s)?.value||0;}
  function proceed(s){if(!s.ridge.waiting||s.state!=='drive')return false;s.ridge.stage=1;s.ridge.waiting=false;s.fuel=100;s.condition=100;s.temperature=35;s.overheated=false;s.airDrop=0;s.airTicks=0;s.wasAir=false;s.ridge.airTime=0;s.ridge.peakFall=0;return true;}
  function deliver(s){if(!s.ridge.waiting||s.state!=='drive'||!s.remaining())return false;s.ridge.waiting=false;s.end('delivered');return true;}
  function score(s){if(s.state!=='win')return 0;const finished=s.car.getPosition().x>=length+6;return s.remaining()*(finished?750:bankValue(s))+s.ridge.landings;}
  function update(s,dt,wasAir){const r=s.ridge,p=s.car.getPosition(),v=s.car.getLinearVelocity(),air=s.groundCount===0;
   if(air){r.airTime=Math.min(10,r.airTime+dt);r.peakFall=Math.max(r.peakFall,-v.y);}else if(wasAir){const slope=Math.atan((height(p.x+1,key)-height(p.x-1,key))/2),a=Math.atan2(Math.sin(s.car.getAngle()-slope),Math.cos(s.car.getAngle()-slope));if(r.airTime>.20&&r.peakFall<7&&Math.abs(a)<.35&&s.remaining()&&p.x>r.awardX+6){r.awardX=p.x;const points=10*s.remaining();r.landings+=points;s.events.push({type:'ridgeLanding',points});}r.airTime=0;r.peakFall=0;}
   r.stallTime=Math.abs(v.x)<.15&&s.throttle>.2?Math.min(10,r.stallTime+dt):0;if(r.stallTime>=2&&r.stallTime-dt<2)s.events.push({type:'ridgeStall'});
   const a=Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(s.car.getAngle()),Math.cos(s.car.getAngle())));r.roofTime=a>2&&p.y<height(p.x,key)+2.3?r.roofTime+dt:0;if(r.roofTime>.35)s.end('flipped');
   const next=camps[s.ridge28.camp];if(s.state==='drive'&&next&&p.x>=next.x&&s.groundCount>=2&&a<.55){s.ridge28.camp++;r.waiting=true;s.events.push({type:'ridgeCamp'});}
  }
  function camera(s,w,h){
   const p=s.car.getPosition(),speed=Math.max(0,s.car.getLinearVelocity().x),look=Math.min(22,Math.max(12,9+speed*1.4));
   let low=Math.min(height(p.x,key),p.y-.7),high=p.y+1.4;
   for(const b of s.boxes)if(!b.lost)high=Math.max(high,b.body.getPosition().y+.4);
   for(let dx=2;dx<=look;dx+=2){const y=height(p.x+dx,key);low=Math.min(low,y);high=Math.max(high,y+.3);}
   const scale=Math.min(h/8,w/22,h*.52/Math.max(1,high-low));
   return {x:p.x+Math.min(5.4,2.2+speed*.3),y:(high+low)/2-h*.09/scale,scale};
  }
  return {key,length,distance,camps,init,valid,offer,bankValue,proceed,deliver,score,update,camera};
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=create;else root.Ridge28Model=create;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

'use strict';
// A preview of the real collision profile ahead of this rest area.
function RidgePreview28(s){
 const from=CP.ridge28.camps[s.ridge28.camp-1].x,to=CP.ridge28.camps[s.ridge28.camp]?.x||CP.ridge28.length+6;
 const samples=Array.from({length:101},(_,i)=>{const x=from+(to-from)*i/100;return {x,y:CP.groundHeight(x,s.roadId)};});
 const low=Math.min(...samples.map(p=>p.y)),high=Math.max(...samples.map(p=>p.y)),px=x=>8+(x-from)/(to-from)*404,py=y=>45-(y-low)/Math.max(3,high-low)*35;
 const line=samples.map((p,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+px(p.x).toFixed(1)+' '+py(p.y).toFixed(1)).join(' ');
 return '<svg viewBox="0 0 420 55" role="img" aria-label="Рельеф следующего участка"><path d="'+line+' L412 55 L8 55 Z" fill="#9ba28222"/><path d="'+line+'" fill="none" stroke="#d5bb83" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="8" cy="'+py(samples[0].y)+'" r="3" fill="#e6d9ad"/><path d="M412 3v12l-7-3 7-3" fill="#d5bb83" stroke="#d5bb83"/></svg><small>'+Math.round((to-from)*3)+' М ДО '+(s.ridge28.camp===3?'ФИНИША':'СЛЕДУЮЩЕГО ПРИВАЛА')+'</small>';
}

(function(){
 let owner=save,view=save.homeView25==='story'?'story':'ridge';

 const ridgeDistance=s=>s.ridge28?CP.ridge28.distance:CP.ridge25.distance;

'use strict';
(function(root){
 const fields=['time','fuel','fuelUsed','lastFuelX','winchCharges','winchTime','coins','distance','flip','stuckTime','groundCount','throttle','hardLandings','condition','temperature','overheated','peakTemperature','serviceHold','airDrop','airTicks','wasAir','brakeHeld','slip','slipTime','traceOverflow','traceAt','lastImpact','fluidLitres','fluidDelivered','fluidSlosh','peakSlosh','fluidLeak','parkingHold','waterDepth','intakeWet','inWater','peakWaterDepth'];
 const groups={items:['got'],boxes:['lost','delivered','out','stopIndex'],stops:['done','elapsed','boxIndex','warned'],services:['done','elapsed'],checkpoints:['done'],gates:['elapsed','requested','open'],liftBridges:['elapsed','wait','requested','done']};
 const finite=(v,limit=10000000)=>typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v)&&Math.abs(v)<=limit;
 const copy=(o,keys)=>Object.fromEntries(keys.filter(k=>o[k]!==undefined).map(k=>[k,o[k]]));
 function fingerprint(CP,id,car,levels,setup){let h=2166136261;const text=JSON.stringify([CP.routes[id],CP.profiles[id],CP.preparedVehicle(car,setup),levels]);for(let n=0;n<text.length;n++)h=Math.imul(h^text.charCodeAt(n),16777619);return h>>>0;}
 function bodies(s){const result=[];for(let b=s.world.getBodyList();b;b=b.getNext())if(!b.isStatic())result.push(b);return result;}
 function fixtures(b){const result=[];for(let f=b.getFixtureList();f;f=f.getNext())result.push(f);return result;}
 // Solver caches are version-pinned to the bundled Planck build. They preserve
 // suspension/contact impulses so a reload does not introduce a cold solve.
 const impulseFields=['m_impulse','m_motorImpulse','m_springImpulse','m_lowerImpulse','m_upperImpulse'];
 function jointList(s){const result=[];for(let j=s.world.getJointList();j;j=j.getNext())result.push(j);return result;}
 function fixtureKeys(s){const map=new Map();let n=0;for(let b=s.world.getBodyList();b;b=b.getNext(),n++)fixtures(b).forEach((f,i)=>map.set(f,n+'.'+i));return map;}
 function contactKey(c,map){return map.get(c.getFixtureA())+':'+c.m_indexA+'>'+map.get(c.getFixtureB())+':'+c.m_indexB;}
 function solverCapture(s){const map=fixtureKeys(s),contacts=[];for(let c=s.world.getContactList();c;c=c.getNext()){const m=c.getManifold();if(m.pointCount)contacts.push({key:contactKey(c,map),manifold:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(m))});}contacts.sort((a,b)=>a.key.localeCompare(b.key));return {joints:jointList(s).map(j=>({type:j.getType(),impulses:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(copy(j,impulseFields)))})),contacts};}
 function capture(s,CP){
  if(!s||s.state!=='drive'||s.paid||s.started===false)return null;
  if(!s.attemptId)s.attemptId=Date.now().toString(36)+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,12);
  const data={schema:1,engine:'river17',attempt:s.attemptId,id:s.id,car:s.vehicle.id,levels:s.levels.slice(),setup:s.vehicle.setup,signature:fingerprint(CP,s.id,s.vehicle.id,s.levels,s.vehicle.setup),state:copy(s,fields),drivetrain:{lowGear:!!s.lowGear,shiftCooldown:s.shiftCooldown||0,gearShifts:s.gearShifts||0},anchor:s.winchAnchor?{x:s.winchAnchor.x,y:s.winchAnchor.y}:null,trace:s.trace.map(p=>p.slice()),groups:{},bodies:[]};
  for(const [name,keys] of Object.entries(groups))data.groups[name]=s[name].map(x=>({...copy(x,keys),...(name==='boxes'?{strap:!!x.strap,strap2:!!x.strap2}:{})}));
  if(s.edition===20)data.journey={edition:20,sectors:s.sectors.map(o=>copy(o,['entered','done','passed','maxSpeed','initialCargo','initialHits']))};
  if(s.edition===21)data.expedition={edition:21,path:s.path,waiting:s.forkWaiting,powered:s.workshopPowered,machine:{...s.machine}};
  if(s.edition===22)data.waterRun={edition:22,support:s.workshopSupport,pump:{...s.pump}};
  if(s.edition===23)data.recoveryRun={edition:23,tow:{...s.tow}};
  if(s.course26)data.course26={edition:26};
  if(s.driving27)data.driving27=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(s.driving27));
  if(s.ridge28)data.ridge28={...s.ridge28};
  if(s.edition===25)data.ridge25={edition:25,...s.ridge};
  if(s.dispatchTerms)data.dispatchTerms={...s.dispatchTerms};
  data.solver=solverCapture(s);data.motors=[...s.joints,...s.gates.map(g=>g.joint),...s.liftBridges.map(b=>b.joint)].map(j=>[j.isMotorEnabled(),j.getMotorSpeed(),j.getMaxMotorTorque()]);
  data.bodies=bodies(s).map(b=>{const p=b.getPosition(),v=b.getLinearVelocity();return {x:p.x,y:p.y,a:b.getAngle(),vx:v.x,vy:v.y,w:b.getAngularVelocity(),active:b.isActive(),awake:b.isAwake(),fixtures:fixtures(b).map(f=>({density:f.getDensity(),sensor:f.isSensor()}))};});
  return validate(data,CP);
 }
 function validate(d,CP){
  try{
   if(!d||d.schema!==1||d.engine!=='river17'||typeof d.attempt!=='string'||! /^[a-z0-9-]{4,60}$/.test(d.attempt)||!Number.isInteger(d.id)||!CP.routes[d.id]||!CP.vehicles.some(m=>m.id===d.car)||!CP.SETUPS[d.setup]||!Array.isArray(d.levels)||d.levels.length!==3||d.levels.some(x=>!Number.isInteger(x)||x<0||x>5))return null;
   if(d.signature!==fingerprint(CP,d.id,d.car,d.levels,d.setup)||!d.state||!d.groups||!Array.isArray(d.bodies)||d.bodies.length>100||!Array.isArray(d.trace)||d.trace.length>800)return null;
   for(const k of fields)if(typeof d.state[k]!=='boolean'&&!finite(d.state[k]))return null;
   if(d.journey){const j=d.journey;if(j.edition!==20||!Array.isArray(j.sectors)||j.sectors.length!==(d.course26?CP.courses26.preview(d.id):CP.contractRoute(d.id,20)).sectors.length)return null;for(const o of j.sectors)if(!o||['entered','done','passed'].some(k=>typeof o[k]!=='boolean')||!finite(o.maxSpeed,500)||o.maxSpeed<0||!finite(o.initialCargo,5000)||o.initialCargo<0||!Number.isInteger(o.initialHits)||o.initialHits<0||o.initialHits>10000||o.passed&&(!o.done||!o.entered))return null;}
   if(d.expedition){const e=d.expedition,m=e.machine;if(d.journey||d.id!==0||e.edition!==21||!['pending','ridge','detour'].includes(e.path)||typeof e.waiting!=='boolean'||typeof e.powered!=='boolean'||e.waiting&&e.path!=='pending'||!m||!finite(m.integrity,100)||m.integrity<0||!finite(m.previousVy,500)||!finite(m.cooldown,1)||m.cooldown<0)return null;}
   if(d.waterRun){const w=d.waterRun,m=w.pump;if(d.journey||d.expedition||d.id!==3||w.edition!==22||typeof w.support!=='boolean'||!m||!finite(m.integrity,100)||m.integrity<0||!finite(m.previousVy,500)||!finite(m.cooldown,1)||m.cooldown<0)return null;}
   if(d.recoveryRun){const r=d.recoveryRun,t=r.tow;if(d.journey||d.expedition||d.waterRun||d.id!==4||r.edition!==23||!t||typeof t.attached!=='boolean'||typeof t.support!=='boolean'||t.delivered!==false||!finite(t.condition,100)||t.condition<0||!Number.isInteger(t.breaks)||t.breaks<0||t.breaks>10000||!finite(t.strain,100000)||t.strain<0||!finite(t.overload,2)||t.overload<0||!finite(t.cooldown,1)||t.cooldown<0||!finite(t.previousVx,500)||!finite(t.previousVy,500))return null;}
   if(d.ridge25&&!CP.ridge25.valid(d))return null;
   if(d.ridge28&&!CP.ridge28.valid(d))return null;
   if(d.course26&&!CP.courses26.valid(d))return null;
   if(d.driving27&&!CP.driving27.valid(d))return null;
   if(d.dispatchTerms&&(!d.journey||d.journey.edition!==20||!(typeof module==='object'?require('./dispatch-terms24.js'):root.DispatchTerms24).valid(d.dispatchTerms,d.id,20)))return null;
   if(d.drivetrain&&(typeof d.drivetrain.lowGear!=='boolean'||!finite(d.drivetrain.shiftCooldown)||d.drivetrain.shiftCooldown<0||d.drivetrain.shiftCooldown>1||!Number.isInteger(d.drivetrain.gearShifts)||d.drivetrain.gearShifts<0))return null;
   if(d.state.time<0||d.state.time>7200||d.state.fuel<0||d.state.fuel>100||d.state.condition<0||d.state.condition>100||d.state.winchCharges<0||d.state.winchCharges>3||d.state.coins<0||d.state.coins>10000)return null;
   if(d.anchor!==null&&(!d.anchor||!finite(d.anchor.x)||!finite(d.anchor.y)))return null;
   let last=-1;for(const p of d.trace){if(!Array.isArray(p)||p.length!==4||p.some(x=>!Number.isInteger(x)||!finite(x))||p[0]<=last||p[0]>d.state.time*60+1)return null;last=p[0];}
   for(const [name,keys] of Object.entries(groups)){if(!Array.isArray(d.groups[name])||d.groups[name].length>100)return null;for(const o of d.groups[name]){if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return null;for(const k of keys)if(o[k]!==undefined&&typeof o[k]!=='boolean'&&!finite(o[k]))return null;if(name==='boxes'&&(typeof o.strap!=='boolean'||typeof o.strap2!=='boolean'))return null;}}
   if(!Array.isArray(d.motors)||d.motors.length>12||d.motors.some(m=>!Array.isArray(m)||m.length!==3||typeof m[0]!=='boolean'||!finite(m[1])||!finite(m[2])||m[2]<0))return null;
   if(!d.solver||!Array.isArray(d.solver.joints)||d.solver.joints.length>100||!Array.isArray(d.solver.contacts)||d.solver.contacts.length>400)return null;
   const vector=(o,allowZ=false)=>o&&typeof o==='object'&&!Array.isArray(o)&&finite(o.x)&&finite(o.y)&&Object.keys(o).every(k=>['x','y',...(allowZ?['z']:[])].includes(k)&&finite(o[k]));
   for(const j of d.solver.joints)if(!j||typeof j.type!=='string'||!j.impulses||Object.keys(j.impulses).some(k=>!impulseFields.includes(k)||!finite(j.impulses[k])&&!vector(j.impulses[k],true)))return null;
   for(const c of d.solver.contacts){const m=c&&c.manifold;if(!c||typeof c.key!=='string'||!/^\d+\.\d+:\d+>\d+\.\d+:\d+$/.test(c.key)||!m||![1,2].includes(m.pointCount)||![-1,0,1,2].includes(m.type)||!vector(m.localNormal)||!vector(m.localPoint)||!Array.isArray(m.points)||m.points.length!==2)return null;for(const p of m.points)if(!p||!vector(p.localPoint)||!finite(p.normalImpulse)||!finite(p.tangentImpulse)||!p.id||Object.keys(p.id).some(k=>!['key','indexA','indexB','typeA','typeB'].includes(k)||!Number.isInteger(p.id[k])||!finite(p.id[k]))||!Number.isInteger(p.id.key))return null;}
   for(const b of d.bodies){if(!b||!['x','y','a','vx','vy','w'].every(k=>finite(b[k]))||Math.abs(b.vx)>500||Math.abs(b.vy)>500||typeof b.active!=='boolean'||typeof b.awake!=='boolean'||!Array.isArray(b.fixtures)||b.fixtures.length>20)return null;for(const f of b.fixtures)if(!f||!finite(f.density,500)||f.density<0||typeof f.sensor!=='boolean')return null;}
   if(JSON.stringify(d).length>160000)return null;
   return d;
  }catch(e){return null;}
 }
 function restore(data,CP){
  const d=validate(data,CP);if(!d)return null;
  try{
   const e=d.expedition,w=d.waterRun,r=d.recoveryRun,q=d.ridge25,s=new CP.Drive(d.id,d.levels.slice(),d.car,d.setup,q?25:r?23:w?22:e?21:d.journey?20:0,{...(q?{load:q.load,...(d.ridge28?{trail:28}:{})}:r?{attached:r.tow.attached,support:r.tow.support}:w?{support:w.support}:e?{path:e.path,powered:e.powered}:{}),...(d.course26?{course:26}:{}),...(d.driving27?{skill:27}:{})}),list=bodies(s);if(list.length!==d.bodies.length)return null;
   for(const name of Object.keys(groups)){if(s[name].length!==d.groups[name].length)return null;for(let i=0;i<s[name].length;i++){const o=s[name][i],saved=d.groups[name][i];for(const k of groups[name]){if(saved[k]!==undefined){if(o[k]!==undefined&&typeof o[k]!==typeof saved[k])return null;o[k]=saved[k];}}if(name==='boxes')for(const k of ['strap','strap2']){if(!saved[k]&&o[k]){s.world.destroyJoint(o[k]);o[k]=null;}}}}
   if(d.driving27)s.driving27=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(d.driving27));
   if(q){const {edition,...state}=q;Object.assign(s.ridge,state);}
   if(d.ridge28)s.ridge28={...d.ridge28};
   if(d.dispatchTerms)s.dispatchTerms={...d.dispatchTerms};
   for(const k of fields){if(typeof s[k]!==typeof d.state[k])return null;s[k]=d.state[k];}
   if(d.drivetrain)Object.assign(s,d.drivetrain);
   if(d.journey)for(let i=0;i<s.sectors.length;i++)Object.assign(s.sectors[i],d.journey.sectors[i]);
   if(e){s.forkWaiting=e.waiting;Object.assign(s.machine,e.machine);}
   if(w)Object.assign(s.pump,w.pump);
   if(r)Object.assign(s.tow,r.tow);
   s.attemptId=d.attempt;s.started=true;s.winchAnchor=d.anchor?{...d.anchor}:null;s.trace=d.trace.map(p=>p.slice());s.events=[];s.previous=null;
   for(let i=0;i<list.length;i++){const b=list[i],v=d.bodies[i],fs=fixtures(b);if(fs.length!==v.fixtures.length)return null;b.setActive(false);b.setTransform({x:v.x,y:v.y},v.a);b.m_sweep.a=b.m_sweep.a0=v.a;for(let n=0;n<fs.length;n++){fs[n].setDensity(v.fixtures[n].density);fs[n].setSensor(v.fixtures[n].sensor);}b.resetMassData();b.setActive(v.active);b.setLinearVelocity({x:v.vx,y:v.vy});b.setAngularVelocity(v.w);b.setAwake(v.awake);}
   const motors=[...s.joints,...s.gates.map(g=>g.joint),...s.liftBridges.map(b=>b.joint)];if(motors.length!==d.motors.length)return null;for(let i=0;i<motors.length;i++){motors[i].enableMotor(d.motors[i][0]);motors[i].setMotorSpeed(d.motors[i][1]);motors[i].setMaxMotorTorque(d.motors[i][2]);}
   // Rebuild broad-phase contacts without advancing the attempt clock or bodies.
   s.world.step(0);const joints=jointList(s);if(joints.length!==d.solver.joints.length)return null;for(let i=0;i<joints.length;i++){const j=joints[i],saved=d.solver.joints[i];if(j.getType()!==saved.type)return null;for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(saved.impulses)){if(typeof j[k]!==typeof v)return null;if(typeof v==='number')j[k]=v;else Object.assign(j[k],v);}}
   const map=fixtureKeys(s),warm=new Map(d.solver.contacts.map(c=>[c.key,c.manifold]));for(let c=s.world.getContactList();c;c=c.getNext()){const saved=warm.get(contactKey(c,map));if(!saved)continue;const m=c.getManifold();m.type=saved.type;m.pointCount=saved.pointCount;Object.assign(m.localPoint,saved.localPoint);Object.assign(m.localNormal,saved.localNormal);for(let n=0;n<m.points.length;n++){const p=m.points[n],v=saved.points[n];if(!v)continue;Object.assign(p.localPoint,v.localPoint);Object.assign(p.id,v.id);p.normalImpulse=v.normalImpulse;p.tangentImpulse=v.tangentImpulse;}}
   for(let i=0;i<list.length;i++)list[i].setAwake(d.bodies[i].awake);s.groundCount=d.state.groundCount;s.events=[];
   return s;
  }catch(e){return null;}
 }
 const api={capture,validate,restore};if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=api;else root.RunCheckpoint=api;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

 if(sim)leaveRun();persist();const settings={};for(const k of ['sound','haptics','music','volume','quality','effects','motion','bigPedals','dialogues','radio','ghostEnabled'])settings[k]=save[k];

FILE: project/game/assets/coach.js

'use strict';
let lesson={active:false,step:0,held:0},driverMood='calm',driverMoodUntil=0,radioUntil=0,radioNext=0,wasAir=false;
const LESSONS=[
 ['1 / 4 · ТРОГАНИЕ','Подержи правую педаль — машина плавно наберёт ход.','gas'],
 ['2 / 4 · ТОРМОЖЕНИЕ','Отпусти газ и подержи левую педаль. Так бережём груз.','brake'],
 ['3 / 4 · МЯГКАЯ ЕЗДА','Снова разгонись. После 25 метров отпусти обе педали: дай подвеске отработать.','coast'],
 ['4 / 4 · ТВОЙ ПЕРВЫЙ РЕЙС','Сначала доставь ящик в лесничество: у жёлтого знака держи тормоз. Потом — на базу. За спуски до 36 км/ч без жёстких посадок получишь премию.','done']
];
function startLesson(){lesson={active:true,step:0,held:0};showLesson();}
function showLesson(){const row=LESSONS[lesson.step];$('lesson-title').textContent=row[0];$('lesson-text').textContent=lesson.step===3&&sim?.edition!==20?'Довези груз до базы. В полёте газ поднимает нос, тормоз опускает.':row[1];$('lesson-next').textContent=lesson.step===3?'ПОНЯЛ, ЕДУ!':'ПРОПУСТИТЬ';$('lesson-meter').style.width='0%';visibility('lesson',true);for(const id of ['gas','brake'])$(id).classList.toggle('coach-focus',row[2]===id);}
function endLesson(done=true){lesson.active=false;save.tutorialDone=done;persist();visibility('lesson',false);for(const id of ['gas','brake'])$(id).classList.remove('coach-focus');}
function updateCoach(dt){
 if(mode!=='drive'||!sim)return;
 if(lesson.active){
  const valid=lesson.step===0?gas&&!brake:lesson.step===1?brake&&!gas:lesson.step===2?sim.distance>=25&&!gas&&!brake:false;
  lesson.held=valid?lesson.held+dt:0;$('lesson-meter').style.width=Math.min(100,lesson.held/.85*100)+'%';
  if(lesson.held>=.85&&lesson.step<3){lesson.step++;lesson.held=0;showLesson();}
 }
 if(sim.time>driverMoodUntil)driverMood='calm';
 if(sim.time>radioUntil)visibility('driver-radio',false);
 if(lesson.active)return;
 const flying=sim.groundCount===0;
 if(flying&&!wasAir&&Math.abs(sim.car.getLinearVelocity().y)>2&&sim.time>2)sayDriver('Полёт: выровняй нос педалями.','focus');
 if(!flying&&wasAir)sayDriver('Приземлились. Плавнее газ.','focus');
 if(sim.fuel<20)sayDriver(CP.fuelPlan(sim.roadId).length?'Бак почти пуст. Следи за канистрами.':'Канистр нет. Отпускай газ на ровной дороге.','worried');if(sim.slipTime>.8)sayDriver('Колёса буксуют. Попробуй плавнее газ или лебёдку.','focus');
 if(Math.abs(sim.car.getAngle())>1.15&&Math.abs(sim.car.getLinearVelocity().x)<.4)sayDriver('Задний ход: держи тормоз.','worried');const surface=CP.surfaceAt(sim.car.getPosition().x,sim.roadId).type;
 if(surface==='ice')sayDriver('Лёд: тормози заранее.','focus');if(surface==='snow')sayDriver('Снег: держи ровную тягу.','focus');if(surface==='sand')sayDriver('Песок: не бросай тягу.','focus');
 if(surface==='mud')sayDriver('Грязь! Тягу держи ровно, без рывков.','focus');
 wasAir=flying;
}
function sayDriver(text,mood='focus',force=false){if(!save.radio||!sim||(!force&&sim.time<radioNext)||lesson.active)return;driverMood=mood;driverMoodUntil=sim.time+2.4;radioUntil=sim.time+2.4;radioNext=sim.time+14;$('driver-radio-text').textContent=text;visibility('driver-radio',true);}
function resetCoach(){lesson.active=false;visibility('lesson',false);visibility('driver-radio',false);driverMood='calm';radioNext=3;radioUntil=0;wasAir=false;for(const id of ['gas','brake'])$(id).classList.remove('coach-focus');}



==============================================================================
FILE: project/game/assets/courses26-model.js
==============================================================================
'use strict';
(function(root){
 function create({routes,profiles,zones,landmarks,bridges,gaps,height,contractRoute}){
  // Explicit sequences per career ID. Placement is deterministic and keeps each
  // existing interaction and crossing in its original, level collision envelope.
  const sequence=['ct','kc','rt','ct','dt','kr','kc','rt','kr','rc','kt','kt','ct','rc','cr','tc','rt','kc','dt','ct','dc','ct','tr','ck','tc','rt','ct','dt','ss','ss','st','ss','rc','tc','rt','ct','tr','kc','dt','ss','ct','tc','rt','ss','dc','kt','rc','tc'];
  const motifs={
   k:{name:'ОСТРЫЙ ГРЕБЕНЬ',tip:'Сбавь перед вершиной. В воздухе выровняй нос педалями.',points:[[0,0],[6,0],[13,3.8],[20,3.8],[27,-.7],[30,-.7],[34,0]]},
   r:{name:'ДВОЙНАЯ ВОЛНА',tip:'Не держи газ: разгрузка колёс, затем короткая посадка.',points:[[0,0],[6,0],[11,2.4],[14,2.4],[19,-.2],[23,1.6],[26,1.6],[31,-.3],[36,0]]},
   c:{name:'ТЯГОВЫЙ ПОДЪЁМ',tip:'Перед уклоном сбавь и включи 4L. На вершине отпусти газ.',points:[[0,0],[6,0],[19,5.6],[27,5.6],[36,.1],[40,.1],[44,0]]},
   t:{name:'КАМЕННЫЕ ПОЛКИ',tip:'Тормози до края. Сходи по ступеням без разгона.',points:[[0,0],[6,0],[18,4.6],[26,4.6],[31,1.7],[34,1.7],[40,0]]},
   s:{name:'ПЕРЕЛИВ НА СКЛОНЕ',tip:'Плавная тяга. Затормози до спуска, береги объём в цистерне.',points:[[0,0],[6,0],[16,3.5],[20,3.5],[29,.1],[33,.1],[38,1],[42,0]]},
   d:{name:'ТЯЖЁЛАЯ СЦЕПКА',tip:'Подъём — 4L. На спуске удерживай скорость без рывков.',points:[[0,0],[6,0],[20,4.2],[32,4.2],[38,-.5],[42,-.5],[46,0]]}
  };
  const cache=new Map(),byRoad=new Map(),edition=id=>id===0?21:id===3?22:id===4?23:20;
  const oldKey=id=>id===0?'light:pending':id===3?'water:22':id===4?'recovery:23':id;
  function protectedRanges(id,key,r){const result=[[0,id===0?45:20],[r.length-16,r.length+40]];
   if(id===3)result.push([91,117]);
   for(const p of [...(r.drops||r.stops||[]),...(r.services||[]),...(r.checkpoints||[])])result.push([p.x-13,p.x+13]);
   for(const g of r.gates||[])result.push([g.x-17,g.x+12]);for(const rock of r.rocks||[])result.push([rock.x-7,rock.x+8]);
   for(const f of r.fords||[])result.push([f.from-10,f.to+10]);
   for(const b of [bridges[key],r.liftBridge].filter(Boolean))result.push([Math.min(b.control??b.from,b.from)-10,b.to+10]);
   const gap=gaps[key];if(gap)result.push([gap.from-17,gap.to+15]);return result;
  }
  function build(id,key=oldKey(id)){
   const tag=id+':'+key;if(cache.has(tag))return cache.get(tag);
   const original=key.toString().startsWith('light:')?{...contractRoute(0,21),...routes[key]}:contractRoute(id,edition(id));
   const r={...original,...(id===6?{limit:130}:id===8?{limit:180}:{})};
   const road='course:26:'+tag,avoid=protectedRanges(id,key,r),hazards=[];let points=profiles[key].map(p=>p.slice());
   const kinds=sequence[id];
   for(let n=0;n<kinds.length;n++){
    const type=kinds[n],m=motifs[type],full=m.points.at(-1)[0];let found=null;
    // Two authored target regions per route. Short fallback fits crowded roads,
    // never moves a stop, floods a service or lands a ramp on a bridge control.
    for(const ratio of [1,.8,.65]){const width=full*ratio,target=r.length*(n===0?.29:.70)+(id%3-1)*5;
     for(let from=20;from+width<=r.length-16;from+=1){const to=from+width;if(avoid.some(([a,b])=>from<b&&to>a))continue;let fit=1;for(let j=1;j<m.points.length;j++){const a=m.points[j-1],b=m.points[j],x=from+(a[0]+b[0])*.5*ratio,base=(height(x+.1,key)-height(x-.1,key))/.2,added=1.5*(b[1]-a[1])/((b[0]-a[0])*ratio);if(added>.01)fit=Math.min(fit,(Math.max(.56,base+.025)-base)/added);if(added<-.01)fit=Math.min(fit,(-Math.max(.72,-base+.025)-base)/added);}const distance=Math.abs((from+to)/2-target)+(1-Math.max(0,Math.min(1,fit)))*90;if(!found||distance<found.distance)found={from,to,width,ratio,distance};}
     if(found)break;
    }
    if(!found)continue;
    const {from,to,ratio}=found,progress=id<9?.70:id<24?.85:1;
    let strength=progress*ratio*(id===4?.85:1),patch;
    const sample=(x,ps)=>{for(let j=1;j<ps.length;j++){if(x>ps[j][0])continue;const a=ps[j-1],b=ps[j],t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x-a[0])/(b[0]-a[0])));return a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t*t*(3-2*t);}return 0;};
    // Overlay the new feature on the real landscape, with zero offset and zero
    // added gradient at each boundary. Limit added grades rather than creating
    // an unclimbable wall where an authored mountain already rises underneath.
    for(let attempt=0;attempt<36;attempt++){
     patch=m.points.map(([x,y])=>[from+x*ratio,y*strength]);let fair=true;
     for(let x=from+.125;x<to;x+=.25){const base=(height(x+.05,key)-height(x-.05,key))/.1,actual=base+(sample(x+.05,patch)-sample(x-.05,patch))/.1;if(actual>Math.max(.56,base+.025)||actual<-Math.max(.72,-base+.025)){fair=false;break;}}
     if(fair)break;strength*=.86;
    }
    points=points.concat(patch.map(([x,y])=>[x,height(x,key)+y])).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]);
    const h={points:patch,strength,type,name:m.name,tip:m.tip,from,to,peak:from+m.points.find(p=>p[1]===Math.max(...m.points.map(p=>p[1])))[0]*ratio};hazards.push(h);avoid.push([from-6,to+6]);
   }
   routes[road]={...r,roadId:road};profiles[road]=[...new Map(points.map(p=>[p[0],p])).values()];zones[road]=(zones[key]||[]).map(o=>({...o}));landmarks[road]=(landmarks[key]||[]).map(o=>({...o}));if(bridges[key])bridges[road]={...bridges[key]};if(gaps[key])gaps[road]={...gaps[key]};
   for(const h of hazards){landmarks[road].push({x:h.from-3,type:'sign',text:h.name});if(h.type==='c'&&!zones[road].some(z=>z.from<h.peak&&z.to>h.from))zones[road].push({from:h.from+5,to:h.peak-1,type:r.region===5?'snow':'mud',friction:r.region===5?.88:.76});}
   const data={key:road,hazards,protected:protectedRanges(id,key,r),route:{...r,roadId:road,hazards,sectors:[0,3,4].includes(id)?[]:hazards.map(h=>({from:h.peak,to:h.to-2,limit:3.3,reward:35,name:'ЧИСТЫЙ СПУСК'}))}};cache.set(tag,data);byRoad.set(road,{...data,baseKey:key});return data;
  }
  function apply(s){const data=build(s.id,s.roadId);s.roadId=data.key;s.route={...s.route,...(data.route.limit?{limit:data.route.limit}:{}),roadId:data.key,hazards:data.hazards,sectors:data.route.sectors};s.route.services=(s.route.services||[]).map(o=>({...o,refuel:24}));s.course26={edition:26};s.course26Model=api;return s.route;}
  function courseHeight(x,road){const data=byRoad.get(road);if(!data)throw Error('Unknown course '+road);const base=height(x,data.baseKey),h=data.hazards.find(h=>x>=h.from&&x<=h.to);if(!h)return base;const points=h.points;for(let n=1;n<points.length;n++){if(x>points[n][0])continue;const a=points[n-1],b=points[n],t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x-a[0])/(b[0]-a[0])));return base+a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t*t*(3-2*t);}return base;}
  function preview(id){return {...build(id).route};}
  function valid(d){return d?.course26?.edition===26&&Object.keys(d.course26).length===1&&!d.ridge25&&((d.id===0&&!!d.expedition)||(d.id===3&&!!d.waterRun)||(d.id===4&&!!d.recoveryRun)||(![0,3,4].includes(d.id)&&!!d.journey));}
  const api={edition,build,apply,preview,valid,motifs,height:courseHeight};return api;
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=create;else root.Courses26Model=create;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

'use strict';
(function(root){
 function factory({P,V,height,groundHeight}){return {
  createCrossings(ground){
   this.waterDepth=0;this.intakeWet=0;this.inWater=false;this.peakWaterDepth=0;this.liftBridges=[];
   const br=this.route.liftBridge;if(!br)return;const span=br.to-br.from,angle=1.05,pivot=V(br.from,br.y-.10),body=this.world.createDynamicBody({position:V(pivot.x+Math.cos(angle)*span/2,pivot.y+Math.sin(angle)*span/2),angle,angularDamping:.7,bullet:true});body.setUserData('bridge');body.createFixture(P.Box(span/2,.10),{density:4,friction:1.25});
   const joint=this.world.createJoint(P.RevoluteJoint({referenceAngle:0,enableLimit:true,lowerAngle:0,upperAngle:1.05,enableMotor:true,motorSpeed:0,maxMotorTorque:25000},ground,body,pivot));
   const boat=this.world.createKinematicBody({position:V(br.to+4,br.y-1.10),bullet:true});boat.setUserData('boat');boat.createFixture(P.Box(1.7,.30),{friction:.5});boat.setActive(false);
   this.liftBridges.push({...br,span,pivot,body,joint,boat,elapsed:0,wait:0,requested:false,done:false,water:{from:br.from,to:br.to,level:br.y-.55,name:br.name}});
  },
  nextLiftBridge(){return this.liftBridges.find(b=>!b.done)||null;},
  updateLiftBridges(gas,brake,dt){
   for(const br of this.liftBridges){
    if(br.boat.isActive()&&br.boat.getPosition().x<br.from-5)br.boat.setActive(false);
    if(br.done){br.joint.setMotorSpeed(0);continue;}
    if(!br.requested){const p=this.car.getPosition(),v=this.car.getLinearVelocity(),a=Math.atan2(Math.sin(this.car.getAngle()),Math.cos(this.car.getAngle())),ready=Math.abs(p.x-br.control)<=2.8&&this.groundCount>=2&&Math.abs(v.x)<.45&&Math.abs(v.y)<.7&&Math.abs(a)<.4&&brake&&!gas;br.elapsed=ready?br.elapsed+dt:0;if(br.elapsed>=1){br.requested=true;br.boat.setActive(true);br.boat.setLinearVelocity(V(-4.5,0));this.events.push({type:'bridgeRequest'});}}
    if(br.requested){this.parkingHold=true;br.wait+=dt;br.joint.setMotorSpeed(br.wait>=4?-.75:0);if(br.wait>=4&&br.joint.getJointAngle()<.015){br.done=true;br.joint.setMotorSpeed(0);this.events.push({type:'bridgeReady'});}}
   }
  },
  waterLevel(ford){return ford.level+.025*Math.sin(this.time*1.6+this.route.seed);},
  updateFords(dt){
   const p=this.car.getPosition(),v=this.car.getLinearVelocity(),m=this.vehicle;let ford=(this.route.fords||[]).find(f=>p.x>=f.from&&p.x<=f.to);if(!ford){const br=this.liftBridges.find(b=>p.x>=b.from&&p.x<=b.to);if(br)ford=br.water;}const bottom=Math.min(...this.wheels.map(w=>w.getPosition().y-m.radius));this.waterDepth=ford?Math.max(0,this.waterLevel(ford)-bottom):0;this.peakWaterDepth=Math.max(this.peakWaterDepth,this.waterDepth);
   if(this.waterDepth>.1){if(!this.inWater)this.events.push({type:'waterEnter'});this.inWater=true;const fraction=Math.min(1,this.waterDepth/1.4),mass=this.car.getMass();this.car.applyForce(V(-v.x*Math.abs(v.x)*mass*.10*fraction,mass*12*.16*fraction-v.y*mass*.08*fraction),this.car.getWorldCenter(),true);
    const intake=this.car.getWorldPoint(V(m.width*(m.id==='mule'?.80:.55),m.id==='mule'?1.45:['timber','tanker'].includes(m.id)?.50:.25));if(intake.y<this.waterLevel(ford))this.intakeWet+=dt;else this.intakeWet=Math.max(0,this.intakeWet-dt*2);if(this.intakeWet>2.5)this.end('drowned');
   }else{this.inWater=false;this.intakeWet=Math.max(0,this.intakeWet-dt*2);}
  }
 };}
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=factory;else root.CrossingPhysics=factory;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

function crossingEvent(ev){if(ev.type==='waterEnter')sayDriver('Брод: держи ровную тягу.','focus');if(ev.type==='bridgeRequest')toast('ПЕРЕПРАВА · ЖДИ СУДНО');if(ev.type==='bridgeReady'){toast('МОСТ ОПУЩЕН · МОЖНО ЕХАТЬ');if(save.sound)beep(410);}}
function crossingWater(ford,reflection=true){
 const left=sx(ford.from),right=sx(ford.to);if(right<-50||left>W+50)return;const level=sim.waterLevel(ford),top=sy(level),bed=[];for(let x=ford.from;x<=ford.to+.01;x+=.5)bed.push([sx(x),Math.max(top,sy(CP.groundHeight(x,sim.roadId)))]);
 const points=[[left,top],...bed,[right,top]],g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,top,0,top+scale*2);g.addColorStop(0,'#84bcb4ba');g.addColorStop(1,'#285b6fef');path(points,g);
 ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(...p):ctx.moveTo(...p));ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();
 if(save.effects&&save.quality!=='low'&&reflection&&sim.car.getPosition().x>ford.from-3&&sim.car.getPosition().x<ford.to+3){ctx.save();ctx.translate(0,top*2);ctx.scale(1,-1);ctx.globalAlpha=.13;truck(sim.car,sim.wheels);if(sim.vehicle.closed)vanFrame(sim.car);for(const box of sim.boxes)if(!box.lost&&!box.delivered)crate(box);ctx.restore();}
 for(let x=ford.from+.6;x<ford.to;x+=2.1){const y=top+3+(hash(x+sim.id)*scale*.33);line(sx(x),y,sx(x+.75),y,'#d4eade4b',1);}
 ctx.restore();line(left,top,right,top,'#c1ded6aa',1.5);

}
function crossingScenery(){

  for(const xx of [br.from,br.to]){const y=sy(br.y);path([[sx(xx-.30),y],[sx(xx+.30),y],[sx(xx+.38),y+k*3.6],[sx(xx-.38),y+k*3.6]],'#526b68');line(sx(xx-.27),y+k*.3,sx(xx+.30),y+k*.3,'#95a69b70',2);}

  circle(sx(br.from),sy(br.y-.10),k*.16,'#d7c492');circle(sx(br.from),sy(br.y-.10),k*.06,'#425b57');
  const x=sx(br.control),y=sy(CP.height(br.control,sim.roadId));for(let dx=-2.8;dx<2.8;dx+=.55)line(sx(br.control+dx),y-1,sx(br.control+dx+.30),y-1,br.done?'#a3d19d':'#deb58b',k*.06);line(x+1.8*k,y,x+1.8*k,y-2.35*k,'#758c79',k*.07);ctx.fillStyle='#294947';ctx.fillRect(x+1.6*k,y-2.65*k,k*.42,k*.50);circle(x+1.8*k,y-2.40*k,k*.095,br.done?'#a8d78e':br.requested?'#e3cc80':'#dd8f76');

 }

'use strict';
const GameStorage=(()=>{
 const allowed=['cargo-save-v1','cargo-career-v1','cargo-active-profile'];
 function revision(value){try{const s=JSON.parse(value);return s&&typeof s==='object'&&!Array.isArray(s)?Math.max(0,Number(s._saveRevision)||0):-1;}catch(e){return -1;}}
 function read(key){if(!allowed.includes(key))return null;let local=null,native=null;try{local=localStorage.getItem(key);}catch(e){}try{if(typeof AndroidGameStore!=='undefined')native=AndroidGameStore.read(key)||null;}catch(e){}
  if(key==='cargo-active-profile'){
   const decode=value=>{if(['test','career'].includes(value))return {profile:value,_saveRevision:0};try{const p=JSON.parse(value);return p&&['test','career'].includes(p.profile)?p:null;}catch(e){return null;}};
   const l=decode(local),n=decode(native);return !l&&!n?null:!l?n.profile:!n?l.profile:revision(native)>=revision(local)?n.profile:l.profile;
  }
  const l=revision(local),n=revision(native);return l<0&&n<0?null:n>l?native:local;
 }
 function write(key,value){if(!allowed.includes(key)||typeof value!=='string'||value.length>2000000)return false;
  if(key==='cargo-active-profile'){if(!['test','career'].includes(value))return false;let rev=Date.now();try{rev=Math.max(rev,revision(localStorage.getItem(key))+1);}catch(e){}try{if(typeof AndroidGameStore!=='undefined')rev=Math.max(rev,revision(AndroidGameStore.read(key))+1);}catch(e){}value=JSON.stringify({profile:value,_saveRevision:rev});}
  let ok=false;try{localStorage.setItem(key,value);ok=true;}catch(e){}try{if(typeof AndroidGameStore!=='undefined'&&AndroidGameStore.write(key,value))ok=true;}catch(e){}return ok;
 }
 return {read,write};
})();

function deliveryDepots(){

  line(px,py,px,py-k*1.8,'#b5b795',k*.05);circle(px,py-k*1.9,k*.31,paint);ctx.fillStyle='#244232';ctx.font='900 '+Math.max(9,k*.28)+'px monospace';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(stop.done?'✓':String(stop.index+1),px,py-k*1.80);
  ctx.font='700 '+Math.max(6,k*.12)+'px monospace';ctx.fillStyle='#e8d8aa';ctx.fillText(stop.name,bx,by-h-k*.70);ctx.textAlign='start';
  // A visible recipient and the accepted parcel remain at this checkpoint.
  const nx=bx-k*.86,ny=by-k*.16;line(nx,ny,nx,ny-k*.54,'#386354',k*.13);circle(nx,ny-k*.68,k*.11,'#d9ad72');line(nx-k*.11,ny-k*.76,nx+k*.12,ny-k*.76,'#e3bb65',k*.055);line(nx,ny-k*.33,nx-k*.14,ny-k*.23,'#cdb685',k*.052);
  if(stop.done){const box=sim.boxes.find(b=>b.delivered&&b.stopIndex===stop.index);if(box)crate({...box,body:{getPosition:()=>({x:stop.x+4.25,y:ground+.39}),getAngle:()=>0}});}
 }
}
function cargoLabel(body){
 if(!sim)return;const kind={3:'water',7:'medical',9:'light',12:'wood',15:'fuel',16:'heat'}[sim.id];if(!kind)return;
 const fill={water:'#507f96',medical:'#b46858',light:'#b3a160',wood:'#6f8c65',fuel:'#b68748',heat:'#b26c4d'}[kind];
 bpoly(body,[[-.13,-.11],[.13,-.11],[.13,.13],[-.13,.13]],fill,'#e3d9b2',scale*.012);
 const ink='#f3e2bc',w=Math.max(1,scale*.025);
 if(kind==='medical'){bodyLine(body,-.085,.01,.085,.01,ink,.035);bodyLine(body,0,-.075,0,.095,ink,.035);}
 else if(kind==='water'){bpoly(body,[[0,.10],[-.07,-.02],[-.045,-.065],[.045,-.065],[.07,-.02]],ink);}
 else if(kind==='light'||kind==='heat'){bpoly(body,[[.015,.10],[-.075,-.005],[-.005,-.005],[-.02,-.085],[.08,.025],[.015,.025]],ink);}
 else if(kind==='fuel'){bpoly(body,[[-.06,-.07],[.06,-.07],[.06,.07],[-.06,.07]],ink);bodyLine(body,-.045,.09,.045,.09,ink,.025);}
 else for(let y=-.055;y<.10;y+=.055)bodyLine(body,-.08,y,.08,y,ink,.024);
}

'use strict';
(function(root){
 const urgent=Object.freeze({edition:24,kind:'urgent',deadline:110,bonus:180,lateFee:80});
 function valid(data,id,edition){return data&&id===1&&edition===20&&data.edition===24&&data.kind==='urgent'&&data.deadline===urgent.deadline&&data.bonus===urgent.bonus&&data.lateFee===urgent.lateFee;}
 function create(kind,id,edition){return kind==='urgent'&&id===1&&edition===20?{...urgent}:null;}
 function adjustment(s){if(!s.dispatchTerms||s.state!=='win')return 0;return s.time>s.dispatchTerms.deadline?-Math.min(s.dispatchTerms.lateFee,Math.round(s.route.reward*s.remaining()/s.route.boxes)):s.remaining()===s.route.boxes?s.dispatchTerms.bonus:0;}
 const api={urgent,valid,create,adjustment};if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=api;else root.DispatchTerms24=api;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

'use strict';
const dispatch={phase:null,elapsed:0,duration:1.35};
function beginLoading(){dispatch.phase='loading';dispatch.elapsed=0;dispatch.duration=1.35;setPage('loading');showDispatch('ПОГРУЗКА / '+(sim.route.story||STORY.jobs[sim.id]).from,'Нина: груз проверен. '+(sim.fluidCapacity?sim.fluidCapacity+' л в цистерне.':sim.route.boxes+' '+cargoWord(sim.route)+' в кузове.'));if(!save.motion)completeDispatch();}
function beginUnloading(){dispatch.phase='unloading';dispatch.elapsed=0;dispatch.duration=1.65;cam.x=sim.car.getPosition().x+2;cam.y=sim.car.getPosition().y+.7;setPage('unloading');showDispatch('ГРУЗ ПРИНЯТ / '+(sim.route.story||STORY.jobs[sim.id]).to,sim.fluidCapacity?'Перекачиваем '+Math.floor(sim.fluidLitres)+' л на базу.':'Разгружаем '+sim.aboard()+' '+cargoWord(sim.route)+'. Доставка на месте.');if(!save.motion)completeDispatch();}
function showDispatch(title,text){$('dispatch-title').textContent=title;$('dispatch-copy').textContent=text;$('dispatch-skip').textContent=dispatch.phase==='loading'?'ПОЕХАЛИ →':'К ИТОГАМ →';visibility('dispatch-card',true);}
function completeDispatch(){
 const phase=dispatch.phase;if(!phase)return;dispatch.phase=null;visibility('dispatch-card',false);last=performance.now();acc=0;
 if(phase==='loading'){sim.started=true;setPage('drive');checkpointRun(true);if(!save.tutorialDone&&(save.runs===0||sim.repeatLesson))startLesson();}else showResult();
}
function updateDispatch(dt){if(mode!==dispatch.phase)return;if(!dispatch.phase)return;dispatch.elapsed+=dt;$('dispatch-progress').style.width=Math.min(100,dispatch.elapsed/dispatch.duration*100)+'%';if(dispatch.elapsed>=dispatch.duration)completeDispatch();}
function dispatchCrate(box){if(box.kind==='liquid'||box.delivered)return;
 if(!dispatch.phase||!['loading','unloading','pause'].includes(mode)||box.lost){crate(box);return;}
 const at=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,dispatch.elapsed/dispatch.duration)),q=box.body.getPosition(),a=box.body.getAngle();
 const offset=dispatch.phase==='loading'?Math.pow(Math.max(0,1-at*(1.6+box.index*.2)),2):Math.pow(Math.max(0,(at-.12-box.index*.12)/.55),2);
 const proxy={...box,body:{getPosition:()=>({x:q.x+(dispatch.phase==='loading'?-3:3)*offset,y:q.y+2*offset}),getAngle:()=>a}};
 ctx.save();if(dispatch.phase==='unloading')ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,1-offset);crate(proxy);ctx.restore();
}
function medalRewards(s,stats){
 const awards=[],whole=s.state==='win'&&s.wholeCargo();
 if(whole&&!stats.whole){stats.whole=true;awards.push({name:'ВЕСЬ ГРУЗ',amount:30});}
 if(whole&&s.hardLandings===0&&!stats.smooth){stats.smooth=true;awards.push({name:'МЯГКИЙ ХОД',amount:40});}
 if(s.state==='win'&&(s.stops||[]).length&&s.stops.every(x=>x.done)&&!stats.relay){stats.relay=true;awards.push({name:'РАЗВОЗЧИК',amount:60});}
 return awards;
}
function renderMedals(){
 const whole=sim.state==='win'&&sim.wholeCargo(),smooth=whole&&sim.hardLandings===0;
 $('result-medals').innerHTML=[['ВЕСЬ ГРУЗ',whole],['МЯГКИЙ ХОД',smooth],...(sim.stops.length?[['РАЗВОЗЧИК',sim.state==='win'&&sim.stops.every(s=>s.done)]]:[])].map(([name,done])=>'<span class="'+(done?'won':'')+'">'+(done?'✓ ':'')+name+'</span>').join('');
 const bonus=(sim.awards||[]).reduce((a,b)=>a+b.amount,0),delivery=sim.reward()-sim.coins-DispatchTerms24.adjustment(sim);
 const sectors=sim.sectors||[],sectorBonus=sectors.filter(s=>s.passed).reduce((a,s)=>a+s.reward,0);$('result-breakdown').textContent='ДОРОГА +'+(sim.coins-sectorBonus)+(sectors.length?' · СПУСКИ '+sectors.filter(s=>s.passed).length+'/'+sectors.length+' +'+sectorBonus:'')+' · ГРУЗ +'+delivery+' · НОВЫЕ ЗНАКИ +'+bonus;
}

 else shape='<rect x="'+(x-32)+'" y="'+(y-22)+'" width="64" height="45" rx="1" fill="'+ink+'"/><path d="M'+(x-40)+' '+(y-23)+'l40-24 40 24Z" fill="'+ink+'"/><rect x="'+(x-23)+'" y="'+(y-12)+'" width="14" height="14" fill="'+window+'"/><rect x="'+(x+8)+'" y="'+(y-12)+'" width="14" height="14" fill="'+window+'"/>'+(kind==='clinic'?'<path d="M'+x+' '+(y-40)+'v17m-8-9h16" stroke="#d9c9a4" stroke-width="5"/>':kind==='pump'?'<circle cx="'+x+'" cy="'+(y+17)+'" r="10" fill="#8aa5a0" stroke="'+ink+'" stroke-width="3"/>':'');
 return '<g>'+shape+'<text x="'+x+'" y="'+(y+47)+'" text-anchor="middle" fill="#304438" font-size="14" font-weight="700">'+n.name+'</text><text x="'+x+'" y="'+(y+64)+'" text-anchor="middle" fill="'+(on?'#426c49':'#776f57')+'" font-size="12">'+(on?'РАБОТАЕТ':'ЖДЁТ ГРУЗ')+'</text></g>';
}
function openVillage(){
 $('village-progress').textContent=save.stats.slice(0,9).filter(x=>x.wins>0).length+' / 9 РЕЙСОВ ПОСЁЛКА';
 $('village-map').innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 610 295" role="img" aria-label="Карта восстановления Лисьего Брода"><rect width="610" height="295" fill="#d7caa6"/><path d="M20 166H590M210 45v230" stroke="#aea482" stroke-width="14"/><path d="M20 166H590" stroke="#e4dcc2" stroke-width="2" stroke-dasharray="9 8"/>'+VILLAGE.map(villageBuilding).join('')+'</svg>';

 visibility('village',true);
}

'use strict';
(function(root){
 function create({height}){
  const reasons=['','cargo','impact','strap','sway'],incidentKinds=['impact','cargo','strap','flipped','ravine'];
  const angle=a=>Math.atan2(Math.sin(a),Math.cos(a));
  function init(s){s.driving27={edition:27,previousVy:s.boxes.map(b=>b.body.getLinearVelocity().y),previousStraps:s.boxes.map(b=>!!b.strap),previousCargo:s.remaining(),previousCondition:s.condition,previousHits:s.hardLandings,airTime:0,fallSpeed:0,awardX:0,landings:0,landingBonus:0,sectors:s.sectors.map(()=>({jolt:0,sway:0,reason:''})),incident:null};}
  function incident(s,kind){const r=s.driving27,p=s.car.getPosition(),prior=r.incident,priority={cargo:5,impact:4,strap:3,flipped:2,ravine:2};if(prior&&priority[prior.kind]>priority[kind])return;r.incident={kind,x:p.x,time:s.time,speed:Math.abs(s.car.getLinearVelocity().x)*10.8,angle:angle(s.car.getAngle())};}
  function update(s,dt){
   const r=s.driving27,x=s.car.getPosition().x,velocity=s.car.getLinearVelocity();let jolt=0,sway=0,snapped=false;
   for(let i=0;i<s.boxes.length;i++){const b=s.boxes[i],vy=b.body.getLinearVelocity().y;if(!b.lost&&!b.delivered){if(b.kind==='liquid')sway=Math.max(sway,Math.abs(s.fluidSlosh)/.32);else{const q=s.car.getLocalPoint(b.body.getPosition());sway=Math.max(sway,Math.hypot(q.x-b.home.x,q.y-b.home.y));jolt=Math.max(jolt,vy-r.previousVy[i]);if(r.previousStraps[i]&&!b.strap&&!s.vehicle.closed)snapped=true;}}r.previousVy[i]=vy;r.previousStraps[i]=!!b.strap;}
   const remaining=s.remaining(),hit=s.hardLandings>r.previousHits,lost=remaining<r.previousCargo;
   if(lost)incident(s,'cargo');else if(hit)incident(s,'impact');else if(snapped)incident(s,'strap');
   const air=s.groundCount===0;
   if(air){r.airTime=Math.min(20,r.airTime+dt);r.fallSpeed=Math.max(r.fallSpeed,-velocity.y);}
   else if(r.airTime>0){const slope=Math.atan((height(x+1,s.roadId)-height(x-1,s.roadId))/2),alignment=Math.abs(angle(s.car.getAngle()-slope));if(r.airTime>.20&&r.fallSpeed<7&&alignment<.35&&!lost&&!hit&&s.condition>=r.previousCondition&&s.aboard()>0&&x>r.awardX+8){r.awardX=x;r.landings++;const bonus=5*Math.min(4,s.aboard());r.landingBonus+=bonus;s.coins+=bonus;s.events.push({type:'softLanding27',bonus});}r.airTime=0;r.fallSpeed=0;}
   for(let i=0;i<s.sectors.length;i++){
    const sector=s.sectors[i],tech=r.sectors[i];if(sector.done||x<sector.from)continue;
    if(!sector.entered&&x>sector.to){sector.done=true;continue;}
    if(!sector.entered){sector.entered=true;sector.initialCargo=s.fluidCapacity?s.fluidLitres+s.fluidDelivered:remaining;sector.initialHits=s.hardLandings;}
    sector.maxSpeed=Math.max(sector.maxSpeed,Math.abs(velocity.x));tech.jolt=Math.max(tech.jolt,jolt);tech.sway=Math.max(tech.sway,sway);
    const failure=lost?'cargo':hit?'impact':snapped?'strap':(!s.fluidCapacity&&(sway>.72||jolt>3.4&&sway>.30))?'sway':'';
    if(!tech.reason&&failure){tech.reason=failure;s.events.push({type:'roughSector27',reason:failure,index:i});}
    if(x>sector.to){sector.done=true;sector.passed=!tech.reason&&(s.fluidCapacity?s.fluidLitres+s.fluidDelivered:remaining)>=sector.initialCargo-.5&&s.hardLandings===sector.initialHits;if(sector.passed)s.coins+=sector.reward;s.events.push({type:'sector',passed:sector.passed,reward:sector.reward,physical:true,reason:tech.reason});}
   }
   r.previousCargo=remaining;r.previousCondition=s.condition;r.previousHits=s.hardLandings;
  }
  function valid(d){const r=d?.driving27,num=(v,min,max)=>typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=min&&v<=max;if(!d.course26||!r||r.edition!==27||!Array.isArray(r.previousVy)||r.previousVy.length!==d.groups.boxes.length||r.previousVy.some(v=>!num(v,-500,500))||!Array.isArray(r.previousStraps)||r.previousStraps.length!==r.previousVy.length||r.previousStraps.some(v=>typeof v!=='boolean')||!Array.isArray(r.sectors)||r.sectors.length!==(d.journey?.sectors.length||0))return false;
   if(!Number.isInteger(r.previousCargo)||!num(r.previousCargo,0,100)||!num(r.previousCondition,0,100)||!Number.isInteger(r.previousHits)||!num(r.previousHits,0,10000)||!num(r.airTime,0,20)||!num(r.fallSpeed,0,500)||!num(r.awardX,0,2000)||!Number.isInteger(r.landings)||!num(r.landings,0,250)||!Number.isInteger(r.landingBonus)||r.landingBonus<r.landings*5||r.landingBonus>r.landings*20)return false;
   if(r.sectors.some(s=>!s||!num(s.jolt,0,1000)||!num(s.sway,0,10000)||!reasons.includes(s.reason)))return false;
   return r.incident===null||r.incident&&incidentKinds.includes(r.incident.kind)&&num(r.incident.x,-100,2000)&&num(r.incident.time,0,d.state.time)&&num(r.incident.speed,0,5400)&&num(r.incident.angle,-Math.PI,Math.PI);
  }
  return {init,update,incident,valid};
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=create;else root.Driving27Model=create;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

function mountainLayers(id,night){
 const colors=night?['#405b72','#324e64','#2c4755','#1f3e43']:id===1?['#b4a28a','#9b8971','#7c806a','#566958']:id===3?['#9fc9c6','#8fb9b1','#719b91','#4d7e76']:id===4?['#9cbaaa','#82a193','#638875','#436a58']:id===5?['#cbdfe0','#adc8ce','#89aebc','#668fa0']:['#afc6ba','#88aaa0','#658f82','#3e7163'];

  const spacing=layer<2?86:64,shift=cam.x*scale*(.03+layer*.07),begin=Math.floor(shift/spacing)-2,end=begin+Math.ceil(W/spacing)+5;
  const points=[];for(let n=begin;n<=end;n++){const rough=hash(n+layer*411),elevation=H*(.35+layer*.085)+sceneryY(.025+layer*.018)-H*(.07+layer*.009)*(rough+Math.sin(n*.75+layer)*.7);points.push({x:n*spacing-shift,y:elevation,n});}
  path([[points[0].x,H],...points.map(p=>[p.x,p.y]),[points[points.length-1].x,H]],colors[layer]);
  if(layer<3&&save.quality!=='low')for(let j=1;j<points.length-1;j++){
   const p=points[j],prev=points[j-1],next=points[j+1];if(p.y>=prev.y||p.y>=next.y)continue;
   const base=Math.max(prev.y,next.y)+H*(.06+layer*.01),ridgeX=p.x+spacing*(.17+hash(p.n)*.14);
   path([[p.x,p.y],[ridgeX,base],[prev.x,prev.y]],id===5?'#eff7f055':night?'#9dbac813':'#eff0cf18');
   path([[p.x,p.y],[next.x,next.y],[ridgeX,base]],night?'#152f4015':'#234d5013');
   if(id===5||layer===0&&id===1){const depth=id===5?H*.026:H*.012;path([[p.x,p.y],[p.x+(next.x-p.x)*.27,p.y+(next.y-p.y)*.27],[p.x+spacing*.05,p.y+depth],[p.x-spacing*.12,p.y+depth*.60],[p.x-(p.x-prev.x)*.25,p.y+(prev.y-p.y)*.25]],id===5?'#f1f5eccc':'#e3dfc463');}
  }
 }
}

'use strict';
(function(){
 const originalHud=hud,originalWallet=wallet,originalRouteArt=routeArt;
 const el=id=>document.getElementById(id);
 function shift(){
  if(!sim||mode!=='drive')return;
  if(sim.setLowGear(!sim.lowGear)){hud();beep(sim.lowGear?140:230);checkpointRun(true);toast(sim.lowGear?'4L · ТЯГА +55% / СКОРОСТЬ НИЖЕ':'4H · ДОРОЖНАЯ ПЕРЕДАЧА');}
  else toast(sim.groundCount<1?'ПЕРЕКЛЮЧАЙ НА ЗЕМЛЕ':'СБАВЬ СКОРОСТЬ ДО 38 КМ/Ч');
 }
 el('transfer-case').onclick=shift;
 addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.code==='KeyQ'&&!e.repeat)shift();});
 hud=function(){
  originalHud();if(!sim)return;
  const low=!!sim.lowGear;
  el('gear-value').textContent=low?'4L':'4H';el('gear-name').textContent=low?'ТЯГА':'ДОРОГА';el('transfer-case').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(low));
  let instability=0,broken=0;
  for(const box of sim.boxes){if(box.lost||box.delivered)continue;if(box.kind==='liquid'){instability=Math.max(instability,Math.abs(sim.fluidSlosh)/.32);continue;}
   const q=sim.car.getLocalPoint(box.body.getPosition());instability=Math.max(instability,Math.hypot(q.x-box.home.x,q.y-box.home.y)/1.1);if(!box.strap&&!sim.vehicle.closed)broken++;
  }
  instability=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,instability));el('load-stability-fill').style.width=(instability*100)+'%';el('load-stability-fill').style.background=instability>.68||broken?'#e09567':'#acbd8d';
  el('load-stability-label').textContent=broken?'БЕЗ РЕМНЯ':instability>.68?'КАЧАЕТ':'СТАБИЛЕН';
  const speed=Math.abs(sim.car.getLinearVelocity().x)*10.8;el('gauge-needle').style.transform='rotate('+Math.min(120,speed*1.8)+'deg)';
  el('road-readout').textContent=sim.inWater?'БРОД':sim.groundCount===0?'В ВОЗДУХЕ':sim.slipTime>.3?'ПРОБУКСОВКА':CP.surfaceAt(sim.car.getPosition().x,sim.roadId).type==='mud'?'ГРЯЗЬ':low?'ПОНИЖЕННАЯ':'4×4';
 };
 wallet=function(){originalWallet();if(!pendingRun())el('home-play-label').textContent='НАЧАТЬ РЕЙС';};
 // Miniature landscape maps show the region as well as the measured road profile.
 routeArt=function(id){
  const r=CP.routes[id],old=originalRouteArt(id),colors=[['#b9c8aa','#70916c','#395c40'],['#cbbda4','#9e9477','#606c4b'],['#526e65','#385b46','#203f2c'],['#b0d0bb','#83ad92','#496e4d'],['#a4b695','#709562','#395f35'],['#d0dcd1','#8caea3','#537c70']][r.region];
  const mountain='<path d="M0 76L23 58L51 68L82 32L119 69L150 51L184 64L220 26L263 58L300 49V120H0Z" fill="'+colors[1]+'"/><path d="M0 84L29 74L62 82L99 62L140 78L175 67L214 82L247 61L300 75V120H0Z" fill="'+colors[2]+'"/>';
  let details='';for(let n=0;n<10;n++){const x=n*34+8,top=65+hash(n+id)*26;details+='<path d="M'+x+' '+top+'l-7 17h5l-8 12h20l-8-12h5Z" fill="'+colors[2]+'" opacity=".7"/>';}
  const profile=old.match(/<path d="([^"]+)" stroke="#e5ca8b"[^>]*\/>/g)||[];
  return '<svg viewBox="0 0 300 120" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" aria-label="Карта '+r.name+'"><rect width="300" height="120" fill="'+colors[0]+'"/><circle cx="252" cy="24" r="10" fill="#eadab5"/>'+mountain+details+'<g transform="translate(0 18) scale(1 .85)">'+profile.join('')+'</g><path d="M14 103H286" stroke="#e0d4ad" stroke-width=".6" opacity=".25"/><circle cx="14" cy="96" r="3" fill="#e3c49a"/><path d="M284 85V101M284 86l8 3-8 4" stroke="#ead2a7" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none"/><text x="10" y="115" fill="#e8dec3" font-size="7" font-family="sans-serif">'+String(id+1).padStart(2,'0')+' / '+r.length*3+' М</text></svg>';
 };
 const originalLesson=startLesson;
 startLesson=function(){originalLesson();if(lesson.active&&el('lesson-text'))el('lesson-text').textContent='Справа — газ, слева — тормоз. Кнопка 4H включает пониженную 4L: больше тяги, меньше скорости. Береги ящики на спусках.';};
 const brand=el('brand-home').querySelector('svg');brand.innerHTML='<path d="M5 4h9c8 0 11 5 11 11s-3 11-11 11H5V4Z"/><path d="M10 10h4c4 0 6 2 6 5s-2 5-6 5h-4M10 10v10"/>';
 document.querySelectorAll('#routes .eyebrow').forEach(n=>n.textContent='ЭКСПЕДИЦИОННЫЙ АТЛАС');
 const title=el('routes').querySelector('h2');if(title)title.textContent='Дороги и заказы';
 wallet();
})();

'use strict';
(function(root){
 function create({P,V,routes,profiles,bridges,zones,landmarks,height,groundHeight,groundVertices,fuelPlan}){
  const common=[[0,0],[17,0],[29,.4],[43,.4]];
  const paths={
   ridge:{name:'Подвесной мост',length:160,reward:450,description:'Короткий путь. Подвижный настил, крутой спуск. Береги крепления.',profile:[...common,[51,1.3],[56,1.3],[74,1.3],[85,.2],[88,.2],[108,5.5],[113,5.5],[120,.2],[160,.2]]},
   detour:{name:'Лесной объезд',length:240,reward:420,description:'Длиннее на 240 м. Пологие уклоны, больше расход топлива.',profile:[...common,[62,.4],[81,2.0],[95,2],[115,.3],[132,.3],[152,2.4],[167,2.4],[190,.2],[240,.2]]}
  };
  const story={cargo:'Тяжёлый генератор',from:'Северный склад',to:'Мастерская Лисьего Брода',radio:'Без генератора мастерская стоит. У указателя выбери мост или объезд. Генератор тяжёлый: дозируй газ, тормози до спуска. На базе нужно не меньше 60% исправности.',reply:'Выберу дорогу по грузу. Доставлю рабочим.',finish:'В мастерской загорелся свет. Посёлок снова может чинить машины.'};
  const base={...routes[0],name:'Первый свет',kind:'delivery',cargo:'generator',requires:'rover',boxes:1,length:240,reward:420,seed:61,weather:'clear',fuelPoints:[],stops:[],services:[],sectors:[],story,fork:{x:36,paths}};
  function route(path='pending',powered=false){const p=paths[path]||paths.detour;return {...base,length:p.length,reward:p.reward,services:powered?[{x:p.length+2,name:'МАСТЕРСКАЯ · ЕСТЬ ТОК',required:false}]:[]};}
  for(const path of ['pending','ridge','detour']){const key='light:'+path;routes[key]=route(path);profiles[key]=(paths[path]||paths.detour).profile;zones[key]=path==='detour'?[{from:128,to:142,type:'sand',friction:.88}]:[];landmarks[key]=[{x:38,type:'sign',text:'МОСТ / ОБЪЕЗД'}];}
  bridges['light:ridge']={from:56,to:74,y:1.3};
  function init(s,options={}){const path=['ridge','detour'].includes(options.path)?options.path:'pending';s.path=path;s.roadId='light:'+path;s.workshopPowered=options.powered===true;s.forkWaiting=false;s.machine={integrity:100,previousVy:0,cooldown:0};return route(path,s.workshopPowered);}
  const methods={
   choosePath(path){
    if(this.edition!==21||this.state!=='drive'||!this.forkWaiting||this.path!=='pending'||!paths[path])return false;
    this.path=path;this.roadId='light:'+path;this.route=route(path,this.workshopPowered);if(this.course26)this.course26Model.apply(this);
    this.groundBody.destroyFixture(this.groundBody.getFixtureList());const points=groundVertices(this.roadId).map(p=>V(p.x,p.y));this.groundVertices=points;this.groundBody.createFixture(P.Chain(points),{friction:1});
    this.createRoadBridge(this.groundBody);this.services=this.route.services.map(s=>({...s,done:false,elapsed:0}));
    const picked=new Set(this.items.filter(i=>i.got).map(i=>i.type+':'+i.x));this.items=[];for(let x=22;x<this.route.length-9;x+=12)this.items.push({x,y:height(x,this.roadId)+1.05,type:'coin',got:picked.has('coin:'+x)});
    for(const p of fuelPlan(this.roadId))this.items.push({x:p.x,y:height(p.x,this.roadId)+1,type:'fuel',amount:p.amount,got:picked.has('fuel:'+p.x)});
    this.forkWaiting=false;this.events.push({type:'path',path});return true;
   },
   updateMachine(dt){
    if(this.edition!==21)return;const box=this.boxes[0],m=this.machine,vy=box.body.getLinearVelocity().y,jolt=vy-m.previousVy;m.previousVy=vy;m.cooldown=Math.max(0,m.cooldown-dt);
    if(jolt>2.3&&m.cooldown<=0){const damage=Math.min(35,Math.round((jolt-2.3)*14));if(damage>0){m.integrity=Math.max(0,m.integrity-damage);m.cooldown=.45;this.events.push({type:'machineHit',damage});}}
    if(this.path==='pending'&&this.car.getPosition().x>=36){this.forkWaiting=true;this.events.push({type:'fork'});}
   }
  };
  return {paths,story,route,init,methods};
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=create;else root.FirstLightModel=create;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

function weatherEffects(){
 if(!sim||!save.effects)return;const w=CP.weatherAt(sim.id,sim.time),count=save.quality==='low'?16:44;
 if(w.type==='rain'||w.type==='storm'){
  const intensity=w.intensity;for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const x=(hash(i+121)*W+clock*(28+w.wind*90))%(W+40)-20,y=(hash(i+38)*H+clock*(145+hash(i+41)*90))%(H+20)-20;line(x,y,x-3-intensity*2,y+9+intensity*6,'#c9e3dc66',1);}
  ctx.fillStyle='#12384d'+(w.type==='storm'?'22':'10');ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  // Stable glints on wet ground, independent of camera sampling.
  const from=Math.floor((cam.x-W*.4/scale)/2)*2,to=cam.x+W*.7/scale;for(let x=from;x<to;x+=2){const g=CP.gaps[sim.roadId];if(g&&x>g.from-.5&&x<g.to+.5)continue;line(sx(x),sy(CP.groundHeight(x,sim.roadId))-1,sx(x+.55),sy(CP.groundHeight(x+.55,sim.roadId))-1,'#cbdfca55',Math.max(1,scale*.023));}
 }else if(w.type==='snow'||w.type==='blizzard'){
  const density=w.type==='blizzard'?count*1.6:count*.7;for(let i=0;i<density;i++){const x=(hash(i+12)*W+clock*(w.type==='blizzard'?55:10)*(1+hash(i+93)))%W,y=(hash(i+75)*H+clock*(18+hash(i+22)*15))%H;circle(x,y,.8+hash(i+34)*1.2,'#edf6ec9c');}
 }
 if(['fog','storm','blizzard'].includes(w.type)){const fog=ctx.createLinearGradient(W*.40,0,W,0);fog.addColorStop(0,'#c3d5ce00');fog.addColorStop(1,w.type==='blizzard'?'#dfe9de70':w.type==='fog'?'#c0d2bc55':'#93b1af30');ctx.fillStyle=fog;ctx.fillRect(W*.4,0,W*.6,H);}
 if(w.type==='wind'&&save.quality!=='low')for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const x=(hash(i+412)*W+clock*34)%W,y=H*(.34+hash(i+86)*.40);line(x,y,x+9,y-2,'#d6ca9350',1);}
}

'use strict';
function validateGhost(g){
 if(!g||(g.course26!==undefined&&g.course26!==26)||!['rover','scrambler','mule','timber','tanker'].includes(g.car)||!['standard','trail','economy'].includes(g.setup)||!Array.isArray(g.levels)||g.levels.length!==3||g.levels.some(v=>!Number.isInteger(v)||v<0||v>5)||!Number.isFinite(g.duration)||g.duration<=0||g.duration>401||!Array.isArray(g.points)||g.points.length<2||g.points.length>800)return null;
 let last=-1;for(const p of g.points){if(!Array.isArray(p)||p.length!==4||p.some(v=>!Number.isInteger(v)||Math.abs(v)>1000000)||p[0]<=last||p[0]<0||p[0]>24060)return null;last=p[0];}if(Math.abs(last/60-g.duration)>.1)return null;
 return {...(g.course26===26?{course26:26}:{}),car:g.car,setup:g.setup,levels:g.levels.slice(),duration:g.duration,points:g.points.map(p=>p.slice())};
}
function ghostMatches(g,s){return !!g&&(g.course26||0)===(s.course26?26:0)&&g.car===s.vehicle.id&&g.setup===s.vehicle.setup&&g.levels.every((v,i)=>v===s.levels[i]);}
function saveBestGhost(s,stats){
 if(s.state!=='win'||s.traceOverflow||s.trace.length<2)return;const old=stats.ghost;if(ghostMatches(old,s)&&old.duration<=s.time)return;
 const p=s.car.getPosition(),points=s.trace.map(p=>p.slice()),final=[Math.round(s.time*60),Math.round(p.x*100),Math.round(p.y*100),Math.round(s.car.getAngle()*1000)];if(points[points.length-1][0]!==final[0])points.push(final);else points[points.length-1]=final;
 if(points.length>800)return;stats.ghost=validateGhost({...(s.course26?{course26:26}:{}),car:s.vehicle.id,setup:s.vehicle.setup,levels:s.levels.slice(),duration:s.time,points});
}
function ghostPoseAt(g,time){if(!g||time>g.duration)return null;const tick=time*60,p=g.points;let lo=0,hi=p.length-1;while(lo+1<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(p[mid][0]<=tick)lo=mid;else hi=mid;}const a=p[lo],b=p[hi],t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(tick-a[0])/(b[0]-a[0])));return {x:(a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t)/100,y:(a[2]+(b[2]-a[2])*t)/100,a:(a[3]+(b[3]-a[3])*t)/1000};}
function drawBestGhost(){
 if(mode!=='drive'||!save.ghostEnabled||!sim||sim.edition===21||sim.edition===22||sim.edition===23||sim.edition===25)return;const g=save.stats[sim.id].ghost;if(!ghostMatches(g,sim))return;const p=ghostPoseAt(g,sim.time);if(!p||sx(p.x)<-scale*5||sx(p.x)>W+scale*5)return;
 const body={getPosition:()=>({x:p.x,y:p.y}),getAngle:()=>p.a},m=sim.vehicle,c=Math.cos(p.a),sn=Math.sin(p.a),wheels=(m.axles||[-m.base,m.base]).map(off=>({getPosition:()=>({x:p.x+off*c+.5*sn,y:p.y+off*sn-.5*c}),getAngle:()=>-sim.time*4}));
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.23;truck(body,wheels);if(m.closed)vanFrame(body);ctx.restore();ctx.fillStyle='#d1ded29c';ctx.font='700 '+Math.max(8,Math.round(scale*.18))+'px sans-serif';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('ТВОЙ РЕКОРД',sx(p.x),sy(p.y+2.4));ctx.textAlign='start';
}
if(typeof module==='object')module.exports={validateGhost,ghostMatches,saveBestGhost,ghostPoseAt};

const JourneyMaps20=(()=>{
 const cache=new Map();
 function map(id,detail=false){
  const key=id+':'+detail;if(cache.has(key))return cache.get(key);
  const r=CP.contractRoute(id),road=r.roadId??id,samples=Array.from({length:161},(_,n)=>({x:r.length*n/160,y:CP.groundHeight(r.length*n/160,road)}));
  const lo=Math.min(...samples.map(p=>p.y)),hi=Math.max(...samples.map(p=>p.y)),px=x=>22+x/r.length*596,py=y=>155-(y-lo)/Math.max(8,hi-lo)*100;
  const line=samples.map((p,n)=>(n?'L':'M')+px(p.x).toFixed(1)+' '+py(p.y).toFixed(1)).join(' '),col=['#384d3b','#4d4b3e','#273e3b','#43574d','#344b3c','#465956'][r.region];
  let contours='';for(let n=0;n<8;n++){let d='';for(let x=0;x<=640;x+=16)d+=(x?'L':'M')+x+' '+(n*34-30+Math.sin(x*.012+id*.8+n*.42)*23+Math.cos(x*.024-n)*9).toFixed(1);contours+='<path d="'+d+'"/>';}

  for(const b of [CP.bridges[road],r.liftBridge].filter(Boolean))marks+='<path d="M'+px(b.from)+' '+py(b.y)+'H'+px(b.to)+'" stroke="#d7ded1" stroke-width="5" stroke-dasharray="2 3"/>';
  for(const f of r.fords||[])marks+='<path d="M'+px(f.from)+' 170H'+px(f.to)+'" stroke="#79bac8" stroke-width="5"/>';
  const pins=[...(r.liftBridge?[{x:r.liftBridge.control,name:r.liftBridge.name,label:'↕',color:'#d7c49c'}]:[]),...(r.fords||[]).map(f=>({x:f.from,name:f.name,label:'≈',color:'#79bac8'})),...(CP.gaps[road]?[{x:(CP.gaps[road].from+CP.gaps[road].to)/2,name:'РАЗРЫВ ДОРОГИ',label:'×',color:'#d49b79'}]:[]),...(r.drops||r.stops||[]).map((s,n)=>({...s,label:String(n+1),color:'#ebc98b'})),...(r.services||[]).map(s=>({...s,label:'+',color:'#95bda3'})),...(r.gates||[]).map(s=>({...s,label:'!',color:'#e9a57f'})),...(r.hazards||[]).map(s=>({x:s.peak,name:s.name,label:'⌃',color:'#e9b180'}))];
  let pinArt='';const labels=new Map();for(const [n,p] of pins.entries()){const x=px(p.x),y=py(CP.height(p.x,road)),lx=Math.max(75,Math.min(560,x)),name=p.name.slice(0,18),half=name.length*2.9+5,shelves=n%2?[184,168,24,40]:[24,40,184,168],ly=shelves.find(row=>!(labels.get(row)||[]).some(([a,b])=>lx-half<b&&lx+half>a))??shelves[0];if(!labels.has(ly))labels.set(ly,[]);labels.get(ly).push([lx-half,lx+half]);pinArt+='<g data-map-pin="'+p.x+'"><path d="M'+x+' '+y+'V'+ly+'" stroke="'+p.color+'" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="3 3"/><circle cx="'+x+'" cy="'+y+'" r="9" fill="'+p.color+'" stroke="#17271f" stroke-width="2"/><text x="'+x+'" y="'+(y+3)+'" text-anchor="middle" fill="#1b2b21" font-size="10" font-weight="700">'+p.label+'</text>'+(detail?'<text class="map-pin-label" x="'+lx+'" y="'+ly+'" text-anchor="middle" fill="#dedfcb" font-size="10">'+p.name.slice(0,18)+'</text>':'')+'</g>';}

'use strict';
function previewStarGoals(r){return [{id:'delivery',title:'Доставка',detail:'Выполни условия заказа',done:false},{id:'cargo',title:'Весь груз',detail:r.cargo==='liquid'?'Довези ≥98,5% объёма':'Не потеряй груз',done:false},{id:'careful',title:'Бережный рейс',detail:'Весь груз, кузов ≥70%'+(r.kind==='timed'?' · запас времени ≥15%':''),done:false}];}
function renderStarGoals(id,goals,preview=false){$(id).innerHTML=goals.map(g=>'<div class="star-goal '+(g.done?'done':'')+'" data-star-goal="'+g.id+'"><b>'+icon('star')+g.title+'</b><small>'+g.detail+(preview?'':g.done?' · выполнено':' · не выполнено')+'</small></div>').join('');}
function navigationData(s,pedals={}){
 const x=s.car.getPosition().x,candidates=[];
 for(const stop of s.stops)if(!stop.done)candidates.push({type:'stop',x:stop.x,name:stop.name,elapsed:stop.elapsed,duration:s.fluidCapacity?2:(stop.duration||1.3),object:stop});
 for(const br of s.liftBridges||[])if(!br.done)candidates.push({type:'bridge',x:br.control,name:br.name,elapsed:br.elapsed,duration:1,object:br});
 for(const gate of s.gates)if(!gate.open)candidates.push({type:'gate',x:gate.x,name:gate.name,elapsed:gate.elapsed,duration:1,object:gate});
 for(const site of s.services)if(!site.done&&(site.required||s.condition<70||s.fuel<30||s.temperature>100))candidates.push({type:'service',x:site.x,name:site.name,elapsed:site.elapsed,duration:2.2,object:site,optional:!site.required});
 for(const check of s.checkpoints)if(!check.done)candidates.push({type:'survey',x:check.x,name:check.name,object:check});
 const missed=candidates.filter(c=>!c.optional&&x>c.x+(c.type==='survey'?6:3.1)).sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x)[0];
 // Passed optional services are never instructions to turn back.
 const ahead=candidates.filter(c=>!c.optional||c.x>=x-3).sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);
 let next=missed||ahead[0]||{type:'base',x:s.route.length+6,name:'БАЗА / '+s.route.name};
 if(!missed){const ford=(s.route.fords||[]).find(f=>x>=f.from-20&&x<=f.to&&f.from<next.x);if(ford)next={type:'water',x:ford.from,name:ford.name,object:ford};}
 if(!missed&&s.fuel<25){const can=s.items.filter(i=>!i.got&&i.type==='fuel'&&i.x>=x-1&&i.x<next.x).sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x)[0];if(can)next={type:'fuel',x:can.x,name:'КАНИСТРА +'+can.amount+'%',object:can};}
 const metres=Math.max(0,Math.round(Math.abs(next.x-x)*3)),near=Math.abs(next.x-x)<=(next.type==='gate'?2.8:3.1);let action=metres+' м · '+({base:'до базы',stop:'до разгрузки',gate:'до поста',service:'до ремонта',survey:'до проверки',fuel:'до канистры',bridge:'до переправы',water:'до брода'})[next.type],progress=0;
 if(next.type==='water'&&x>=next.object.from-2){action=s.intakeWet>.4?'Мотор в воде · на берег!':'Ровная тяга · без разгона';}
 else if(missed)action='Вернись · '+metres+' м';
 else if(next.type==='bridge'&&next.object.requested){action=next.object.wait<4?'Жди прохода судна':'Мост опускается';progress=next.object.wait<4?Math.min(1,next.object.wait/4):Math.min(1,1-next.object.joint.getJointAngle()/1.05);}
 else if(next.type==='gate'&&next.object.requested){action='Жди зелёный свет';progress=Math.min(1,-next.object.joint.getJointAngle()/1.4);}
 else if(near&&['stop','gate','service','bridge'].includes(next.type)){
  progress=Math.min(1,(next.elapsed||0)/next.duration);
  action=pedals.gas?'Отпусти газ':Math.abs(s.car.getLinearVelocity().x)>.45?'Остановись тормозом':progress>0?({stop:s.fluidCapacity?'Перекачка':'Разгрузка',gate:'Запрос проезда',service:'Ремонт',bridge:'Запрос переправы'})[next.type]+' '+Math.round(progress*100)+'%':'Держи тормоз '+next.duration.toLocaleString('ru-RU')+' с';
 }else if(near&&next.type==='survey')action=s.groundCount?'Проедь пост по земле':'Приземлись у поста';
 else if(near&&next.type==='fuel')action='Подбери канистру';
 return {...next,metres,near,missed:!!missed,action,progress};
}
function updateNavigator(){
 const next=navigationData(sim,{gas,brake});$('navigation-name').textContent=next.name;$('navigation-action').textContent=next.action;$('navigation-progress').style.width=next.progress*100+'%';$('navigation-panel').dataset.kind=next.type;updateJourneyCue(next);
}
function drawRoadProfile(){
 if(!sim)return;const c=$('road-profile'),g=c.getContext('2d'),w=c.width,h=c.height,x=pose(sim.car).x,from=x-5,to=x+55,points=[];let lo=Infinity,hi=-Infinity;
 for(let xx=from;xx<=to;xx+=1){const br=sim.route.liftBridge,old=CP.bridges[sim.roadId];const yy=br&&sim.liftBridges[0].done&&xx>br.from&&xx<br.to?br.y:old&&xx>old.from&&xx<old.to?old.y:CP.groundHeight(xx,sim.roadId);points.push([xx,yy]);lo=Math.min(lo,yy);hi=Math.max(hi,yy);}const range=Math.max(7,hi-lo+2),px=v=>(v-from)/(to-from)*w,py=v=>h-8-(v-lo+1)/range*(h-20);
 g.clearRect(0,0,w,h);g.strokeStyle='#b1c697';g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?g.lineTo(px(p[0]),py(p[1])):g.moveTo(px(p[0]),py(p[1])));g.stroke();
 const pins=[...(sim.liftBridges||[]).map(b=>({x:b.control,type:'bridge'})),...(sim.route.fords||[]).map(f=>({x:f.from,type:'water'})),...sim.stops.filter(o=>!o.done).map(o=>({...o,type:'stop'})),...sim.gates.filter(o=>!o.open).map(o=>({...o,type:'gate'})),...sim.services.filter(o=>!o.done).map(o=>({...o,type:'service'})),...sim.items.filter(o=>!o.got&&o.type==='fuel'),{x:sim.route.length+6,type:'base'}];
 for(const p of pins){if(p.x<from||p.x>to)continue;const xx=px(p.x),yy=py(CP.height(p.x,sim.roadId));g.fillStyle=p.type==='water'?'#90d7d5':p.type==='bridge'?'#d1b981':p.type==='gate'?'#dda479':p.type==='fuel'?'#e69873':p.type==='service'?'#9ccb9b':'#e8c575';g.fillRect(xx-3,yy-10,6,7);}
 g.fillStyle='#f2dfa0';g.beginPath();g.arc(px(x),py(CP.height(x,sim.roadId)),4,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
}
if(typeof module==='object')module.exports={navigationData,previewStarGoals};

  for(const b of prep.querySelectorAll('[data-prep-setup]'))b.onclick=()=>{save.setups[m.id]=b.dataset.prepSetup;persist();renderPreparation(id);wallet();};
  $('preparation-buy').onclick=()=>{const levels=currentLevels(),i=tip.upgrade,price=90+levels[i]*100;if(levels[i]>=5||save.wallet<price)return;save.wallet-=price;levels[i]++;persist();renderPreparation(id);wallet();beep(430);};
  const goals=previewStarGoals(r);if(id===1&&termsChoice==='urgent')goals[2]={...goals[2],detail:goals[2].detail+' · договор за 1:50'};renderStarGoals('briefing-goals',goals,true);
  for(const b of prep.querySelectorAll('[data-dispatch]'))b.onclick=()=>{termsChoice=b.dataset.dispatch;renderPreparation(id);};
 }
 const oldBrief=openBriefing;openBriefing=function(id){termsRoute=id;termsChoice='ordinary';oldBrief(id);prepJump.textContent=id===1?'ДОГОВОР':'ПОДГОТОВКА';renderPreparation(id);};
 const oldStart=start;start=function(){if(selected===1&&routeUnlocked(1)&&!pendingRun()&&(!sim||sim.state!=='drive'||sim.started===false)){if(!selectWorkVehicle())return;leaveRun();openBriefing(1);return;}oldStart();};for(const id of ['play','route-start','stats-play','restart','retry'])$(id).onclick=start;
 const oldBegin=beginDrive;beginDrive=function(options={}){oldBegin(options);termsRoute=null;termsChoice='ordinary';};
 const oldHud=hud;hud=function(){oldHud();const t=sim?.dispatchTerms;if(!sim)return;if(t){visibility('order-clock',true);$('order-clock').firstChild.nodeValue=sim.time>t.deadline?'УДЕРЖАНИЕ −80 · ':'НАДБАВКА +180 · ';$('order-countdown').textContent=sim.time>t.deadline?'СРОК ВЫШЕЛ':timeText(Math.max(0,t.deadline-sim.time));$('order-clock').classList.toggle('danger',sim.time>t.deadline);$('order-goal').textContent='СРОЧНЫЙ ДОГОВОР · ВСЕ 4 ЯЩИКА';}else{$('order-clock').classList.remove('danger');$('order-clock').firstChild.nodeValue='ОСТАЛОСЬ ';}};
 const impact=document.createElement('div');impact.id='result-impact24';$('result-unlock').after(impact);
 const oldResult=showResult;showResult=function(){oldResult();const state=Restoration24.state(save),chapter=state.rows.find(c=>c.id===sim.id),won=sim.state==='win';impact.classList.toggle('hidden',!won||!chapter);if(won&&chapter){impact.innerHTML='<b>ЛИСИЙ БРОД · '+state.done+' / 9</b><p>'+(chapter.done?chapter.benefit:'Груз принят.
