'use strict';
(function(root){
 function attach({gas,brake,isDriving,onChange,onPause,onResume,isPaused,onAudio,target=root}){
  const held={gas:new Set(),brake:new Set()},pressures=new Map(),keys={ArrowRight:'gas',KeyD:'gas',ArrowLeft:'brake',KeyA:'brake'};
  function throttle(){if([...held.gas].some(k=>k[0]==='k'))return 1;return pressures.size?Math.max(...pressures.values()):1;}
  function pressure(e){const rect=gas.getBoundingClientRect();return Math.max(.28,Math.min(1,.28+(rect.bottom-e.clientY)/Math.max(1,rect.height)*.72));}
  function update(){for(const id of ['gas','brake']){const button=id==='gas'?gas:brake,down=held[id].size>0;button.classList.toggle('pressed',down);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(down));}onChange(held.gas.size>0,held.brake.size>0);}
  function clear(){held.gas.clear();held.brake.clear();pressures.clear();gas.style.setProperty('--pedal-pressure',0);update();}
  for(const [id,button] of [['gas',gas],['brake',brake]]){
   button.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(!isDriving())return;if(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0)return;held[id].add('p'+e.pointerId);if(id==='gas'){pressures.set(e.pointerId,pressure(e));gas.style.setProperty('--pedal-pressure',throttle());}try{button.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(e){}update();onAudio();});
   button.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(id==='gas'&&held.gas.has('p'+e.pointerId)){pressures.set(e.pointerId,pressure(e));gas.style.setProperty('--pedal-pressure',throttle());}});
   const up=e=>{held[id].delete('p'+e.pointerId);if(id==='gas'){pressures.delete(e.pointerId);gas.style.setProperty('--pedal-pressure',held.gas.size?throttle():0);}update();};for(const type of ['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'])button.addEventListener(type,up);
   button.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
  }
  target.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(keys[e.code]||e.code==='Space')e.preventDefault();if(e.code==='Space'||e.code==='Escape'){if(e.repeat)return;if(isDriving())onPause();else if(isPaused())onResume();return;}if(keys[e.code]&&isDriving()){held[keys[e.code]].add('k'+e.code);update();}});
  target.addEventListener('keyup',e=>{if(keys[e.code]){held[keys[e.code]].delete('k'+e.code);update();}});
  target.addEventListener('blur',()=>{clear();onPause();});
  return {clear,held,throttle};
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports={attach};else root.PedalInput={attach};
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

 const originalTruck=truck,originalDraw=draw;
 const treeCache=new Map(),soilCache=new Map();

 function remember(cache,key,value,limit){cache.set(key,value);if(cache.size>limit)cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value);return value;}
 const palettes=[
  {sky:['#8caaa1','#d6d1b4','#bbc3a5'],peaks:['#a4b5a5','#7e9b8c','#517d6a','#294f3c'],trees:'#294f38',earth:['#8b8860','#666347','#343b29'],road:'#c6be87'},

 function pineSprite(variant,region){
  const key=region+':'+variant;if(treeCache.has(key))return treeCache.get(key);
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=110;c.height=200;const a=c.getContext('2d');
  const col=palettes[region].trees;const shade=region===5?'#87a095':'#78916b';
  a.fillStyle='#384b34';a.fillRect(51,38,6,155);
  for(let tier=0;tier<12;tier++){
   const y=16+tier*12,w=8+tier*3.5+hash(tier+variant*19)*5;
   a.beginPath();a.moveTo(54,y-18);
   for(let j=0;j<7;j++)a.lineTo(54+w*(j/6),y+j*2.6+hash(j+tier*21+variant)*4);
   a.lineTo(54,y+20);
   for(let j=6;j>=0;j--)a.lineTo(54-w*(j/6),y+j*2.6+hash(j+tier*51+variant)*4);
   a.closePath();a.fillStyle=col;a.fill();
   a.beginPath();a.moveTo(54,y-15);a.lineTo(54+w*.86,y+14);a.lineTo(54,y+13);a.closePath();a.fillStyle=shade;a.globalAlpha=region===5?.46:.23;a.fill();a.globalAlpha=1;
   if(region===5){a.strokeStyle='#e8e8d5';a.lineWidth=2;a.beginPath();a.moveTo(54,y-16);a.lineTo(54+w*.9,y+13);a.stroke();}
  }
  a.fillStyle='#202f2180';a.fillRect(52,166,3,28);
  treeCache.set(key,c);return c;
 }
 function ridge(region,layer){
  const p=palettes[region],night=region===2,factor=.02+layer*.07,spacing=95-layer*12,shift=cam.x*scale*factor;
  const start=Math.floor(shift/spacing)-2,points=[];
  for(let n=start;n<start+Math.ceil(W/spacing)+6;n++){
   const height=H*(.37+layer*.086)-(Math.sin(n*.64+region*2.1)+hash(n+layer*109)*.72)*H*(.068+layer*.012);
   points.push([n*spacing-shift,height+(cam.y-2)*scale*(.015+layer*.028)]);
  }
  path([[points[0][0],H],...points,[points[points.length-1][0],H]],p.peaks[layer]);
  for(let i=1;i<points.length-1;i++){
   const prev=points[i-1],a=points[i],next=points[i+1];if(a[1]>prev[1]||a[1]>next[1])continue;
   const trough=Math.max(prev[1],next[1])+H*.08;
   path([a,[a[0]+spacing*.34,trough],prev],region===5?'#e8ead580':night?'#c0d8bd09':'#d8d4b51b');
   if(region===5){path([a,[a[0]+(next[0]-a[0])*.35,a[1]+(next[1]-a[1])*.35],[a[0]+7,a[1]+H*.025],[a[0]-17,a[1]+H*.02],[a[0]-(a[0]-prev[0])*.25,a[1]+(prev[1]-a[1])*.25]],'#e2e5d0');}
  }
  if(layer>=2&&region!==1&&region!==3){
   const step=24,scroll=cam.x*scale*(factor+.03),begin=Math.floor(scroll/step)-2;
   for(let n=begin;n<begin+Math.ceil(W/step)+5;n++){
    const h=H*(.08+hash(n+region*33)*.12),x=n*step-scroll,base=H*(.61+layer*.052)+Math.sin(n*.4)*H*.025+(cam.y-2)*scale*.08;
    ctx.globalAlpha=layer===2?.46:.85;ctx.drawImage(pineSprite(Math.abs(n)%4,region),x-h*.26,base-h,h*.55,h);
   }ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
 }
 // Reuse world-space geometry and submit each colour as one path. This
 // avoids hundreds of per-frame ellipse calls without large bitmap layers.
 function soilTexture(route,tile){
  const key=route+':'+tile;let value=soilCache.get(key);if(value)return value;
  const from=tile*8,to=from+8,light=new Path2D(),dark=new Path2D();
  for(let n=Math.ceil(from/.48);n*.48<to;n++){
   const x=n*.48,base=CP.groundHeight(x,route);
   for(let j=0;j<4;j++){
    const seed=x*37+j*41,px=x+hash(seed)*.3,py=base-.23-j*.65-hash(seed+17)*.35,size=.015+hash(seed+28)*.04,path=j%2?light:dark;
    path.moveTo(px+size*1.8*Math.cos(-.4),py+size*1.8*Math.sin(-.4));path.ellipse(px,py,size*1.8,size,-.4,0,Math.PI*2);
   }
  }
  value={light,dark};cacheStats.soilBuilds++;return remember(soilCache,key,value,16);
 }
 function house(x,y,k,region){

 function terrain(){
  const r=sim?sim.roadId:selected,id=CP.routes[r].region,p=palettes[id],from=cam.x-W*.4/scale-4,to=cam.x+W*.73/scale+4;
  const mesh=CP.groundVertices(r),visible=mesh.slice(Math.max(0,Math.floor((from+30)*2)),Math.min(mesh.length,Math.ceil((to+30)*2)+1));if(visible.length<2)return;
  const road=visible.map(v=>[sx(v.x),sy(v.y)]),shape=[[road[0][0],H+15],...road,[road[road.length-1][0],H+15]];
  const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,H*.52,0,H);g.addColorStop(0,p.earth[0]);g.addColorStop(.27,p.earth[1]);g.addColorStop(1,p.earth[2]);path(shape,g);
  ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();shape.forEach((q,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(...q):ctx.moveTo(...q));ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();
  for(let row=0;row<3;row++){
   ctx.beginPath();visible.forEach((v,i)=>{const x=sx(v.x),y=sy(v.y-.45-row*.8+Math.sin(v.x*.63+row)*.11);if(i)ctx.lineTo(x,y);else ctx.moveTo(x,y);});ctx.lineWidth=scale*(row===0?.08:.035);ctx.strokeStyle=row===0?'#ddd6b12b':'#202f2240';ctx.stroke();
  }
  if(save.quality!=='low')for(let tile=Math.floor(from/8);tile<=Math.floor(to/8);tile++){
   const texture=soilTexture(r,tile);ctx.save();ctx.translate(sx(0),sy(0));ctx.scale(scale,-scale);ctx.fillStyle='#d9c79929';ctx.fill(texture.light);ctx.fillStyle='#14271735';ctx.fill(texture.dark);ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.restore();
  ctx.beginPath();road.forEach((v,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(...v):ctx.moveTo(...v));ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(7,scale*.23);ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.strokeStyle='#354634';ctx.stroke();ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(4,scale*.13);ctx.strokeStyle=p.road;ctx.stroke();ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeStyle='#f1e2b74b';ctx.stroke();
  if(save.quality!=='low')for(let x=Math.floor(from/.6)*.6;x<to;x+=.6){
   const y=CP.groundHeight(x,r),a=sx(x),b=sy(y),h=hash(x*3+id*101);const bridge=CP.bridges[r];if(bridge&&x>bridge.from&&x<bridge.to)continue;
   if(id===5){line(a-4,b-1,a+5,b-1,'#e5e7d7',3);}else if((id===0||id===4)&&h>.42){for(let j=0;j<3;j++)line(a+j*2,b-1,a+j*2-2,b-4-h*6,id===4?'#88945c':'#8c9a64',1);}else if(h>.78){ctx.fillStyle='#57644d';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(a,b-1,2+hash(x)*3,1.5,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
  }

 }
 let wheelSprite=null;
 function makeWheel(){
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=192;const a=c.getContext('2d'),r=90;
  a.translate(96,96);const disk=(radius,color)=>{a.fillStyle=color;a.beginPath();a.arc(0,0,radius,0,Math.PI*2);a.fill();};
  const polygon=(pts,color)=>{a.fillStyle=color;a.beginPath();pts.forEach((q,i)=>i?a.lineTo(...q):a.moveTo(...q));a.closePath();a.fill();};
  const rubber=a.createRadialGradient(-r*.2,-r*.3,r*.1,0,0,r);rubber.addColorStop(0,'#475446');rubber.addColorStop(.5,'#18251c');rubber.addColorStop(1,'#111a13');disk(r,rubber);

 }
 function wheel(w,m){

  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(-p.a);const extent=r*192/90;ctx.drawImage(wheelSprite,-extent/2,-extent/2,extent,extent);ctx.restore();

 }
 function metal(body,m,top=1.5){

  g.addColorStop(0,m.accent);g.addColorStop(.12,m.color);g.addColorStop(.49,m.color);g.addColorStop(.52,m.accent);g.addColorStop(.58,m.color);g.addColorStop(1,m.shadow||'#48563b');return g;
 }
 function driver(body,hx,hy,wx,wy,k){

 function rig(body,wheels,preview=false){
  originalTruck(body,wheels,preview);const m=vehicleLook(preview?currentVehicle():sim.vehicle);
  // Integrated accessories: protective rails, reflective tape, roof rack and dirt.
  if(m.id==='rover'||m.id==='mule'){
   bodyLine(body,m.width-.1,.02,m.width+.10,.13,'#243426',.10);
   bodyLine(body,.16,1.23,.99,1.26,'#374c35',.06);
   for(let i=0;i<4;i++)bodyLine(body,-1.52+i*.28,.15,-1.37+i*.28,.15,'#ddd5af',.035);
   vehicleText(body,.71,.28,m.name,'#f0dbb2',.14);
   bpoly(body,[[.18,.61],[.30,1.05],[.95,1.10],[.56,.87]],'#cfdfbc14');
  }
  if(!preview&&save.effects){
   const dirt=CP.surfaceAt(body.getPosition().x,sim.roadId).type;const dirty=sim.distance/200;
   ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.45,dirty*.25);
   for(let i=0;i<14;i++)circle(...bodyPoint(body,-m.width+hash(i+7)*m.width*2,.04+hash(i+71)*.2),Math.max(.6,scale*(.025+hash(i+28)*.018)),dirt==='snow'?'#d8dfbd':'#857b50');ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
 }
 function menuScene(){

'use strict';
(function(root){
 function create({P,V,routes,profiles,zones,landmarks}){
  const key='recovery:23';
  const story={cargo:'Сломанный фургон и инструмент',from:'Обочина лесной дороги',to:'Ремонтная база Лисьего Брода',radio:'Фургон механика остался на обочине. Он стоит сзади. Остановись и нажми «Прицепить». Тяни плавно, на подъёме включай 4L. У базы останови обе машины и отцепи фургон на площадке. За помощь механик даст запасной трос для следующих рейсов.',reply:'Подцеплю, вытяну и поставлю у мастерской.',finish:'Фургон на базе. Механик выдал запасной трос: в следующих рейсах лебёдку можно использовать три раза.'};
  const base={...routes[4],name:'Своих не бросаем',kind:'delivery',limit:undefined,cargo:'towkit',requires:'rover',length:190,boxes:1,reward:690,seed:63,weather:'clear',fuelPoints:[],stops:[],services:[],sectors:[],story};
  function route(){return {...base};}
  routes[key]=route();profiles[key]=[[0,0],[26,0],[44,2.1],[58,2.1],[74,.3],[90,.3],[111,4.4],[116,4.4],[128,.3],[154,.3],[173,1.2],[190,.2]];
  zones[key]=[{from:78,to:91,type:'gravel',friction:1.05}];landmarks[key]=[{x:30,type:'sign',text:'ТЯЖЁЛАЯ СЦЕПКА'},{x:108,type:'sign',text:'СПУСК · ТОРМОЗИ ЗАРАНЕЕ'}];
  function init(s,options={}){s.roadId=key;s.tow={attached:options.attached===true,support:options.support===true,condition:68,breaks:0,strain:0,overload:0,cooldown:0,previousVx:0,previousVy:0,delivered:false};return route();}
  const methods={
   createRecoveryRig(){
    const b=this.world.createDynamicBody({position:V(-2,.94),bullet:true,linearDamping:.06,angularDamping:.8}),fd={filterGroupIndex:-2,friction:.7};b.setUserData('recovery');
    b.createFixture(P.Box(1.48,.14,V(0,.04)),{...fd,density:1.6});b.createFixture(P.Box(.88,.54,V(-.4,.7)),{...fd,density:.5});b.createFixture(P.Polygon([V(.48,.18),V(1.48,.18),V(1.18,1.1),V(.5,1.1)]),{...fd,density:.8});
    const wheels=[],joints=[];for(const x of [-1.02,1.02]){const w=this.world.createDynamicBody({position:V(-2+x,.44),bullet:true});w.setUserData('recoveryWheel');w.createFixture(P.Circle(.44),{density:1.2,friction:1.3,filterGroupIndex:-2});wheels.push(w);joints.push(this.world.createJoint(P.WheelJoint({enableMotor:false,maxMotorTorque:0,frequencyHz:3.0,dampingRatio:.8},b,w,w.getPosition(),V(0,1))));}
    this.recovery={body:b,wheels,joints,rope:null};if(this.tow.attached)this.attachRecoveryRope();
   },
   towAnchors(){return {a:this.car.getWorldPoint(V(-1.7,.10)),b:this.recovery.body.getWorldPoint(V(1.46,.10))};},
   attachRecoveryRope(){this.recovery.rope=this.world.createJoint(P.RopeJoint({maxLength:5.4,collideConnected:true,localAnchorA:V(-1.7,.10),localAnchorB:V(1.46,.10)},this.car,this.recovery.body));this.tow.attached=true;},
   canToggleTow(){if(this.edition!==23||this.state!=='drive'||this.tow.delivered||this.groundCount<1||Math.abs(this.car.getLinearVelocity().x)>.85||Math.abs(this.recovery.body.getLinearVelocity().x)>.85)return false;if(this.tow.attached){const p=this.recovery.body.getPosition();return Math.abs(p.x-this.route.length)>4||this.canDeliverRecovery();}const {a,b}=this.towAnchors();return a.x>b.x+.4&&Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y)<=5.3;},
   canDeliverRecovery(){const p=this.recovery.body.getPosition(),v=this.recovery.body.getLinearVelocity(),a=this.recovery.body.getAngle();return p.x>=this.route.length-4&&p.x<=this.route.length+4&&Math.abs(v.x)<.45&&Math.abs(this.car.getLinearVelocity().x)<.45&&Math.abs(v.y)<.6&&Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(a),Math.cos(a)))<.45&&this.tow.condition>=40&&this.remaining()===1;},
   toggleTow(){
    if(!this.canToggleTow())return false;const t=this.tow;
    if(t.attached){this.world.destroyJoint(this.recovery.rope);this.recovery.rope=null;t.attached=false;t.strain=0;t.overload=0;if(this.canDeliverRecovery()){t.delivered=true;this.events.push({type:'towDelivered'});this.end('delivered');}else this.events.push({type:'towDetach'});}
    else{this.attachRecoveryRope();t.overload=0;t.strain=0;this.events.push({type:'towAttach'});}return true;
   },
   updateRecovery(dt){
    if(this.edition!==23)return;const t=this.tow,r=this.recovery,v=r.body.getLinearVelocity(),vertical=v.y-t.previousVy,horizontal=Math.abs(v.x-t.previousVx);t.previousVy=v.y;t.previousVx=v.x;t.cooldown=Math.max(0,t.cooldown-dt);
    if(t.cooldown<=0&&(vertical>2.5||horizontal>2.5)){const damage=Math.min(25,Math.round(Math.max(0,vertical-2.5)*6+Math.max(0,horizontal-2.5)*4));if(damage){t.condition=Math.max(0,t.condition-damage);t.cooldown=.5;this.events.push({type:'towHit',damage});}}
    if(r.rope){const f=r.rope.getReactionForce(120),ratio=Math.hypot(f.x,f.y)/(t.support?1200:900);t.strain+=(ratio-t.strain)*.3;t.overload=ratio>1?t.overload+dt*(ratio-1):Math.max(0,t.overload-dt*.7);if(t.overload>.10){this.world.destroyJoint(r.rope);r.rope=null;t.attached=false;t.breaks++;t.overload=0;t.strain=0;this.events.push({type:'towBreak'});}}
    if(t.condition<=0)this.end('towDamage');if(this.car.getPosition().x>this.route.length+24&&!t.delivered)this.end('tow');
   },
   recoveryGoals(){const won=this.state==='win',full=this.remaining()===1&&this.tow.condition>=60;return [{id:'delivery',title:'Фургон на базе',detail:'Состояние ≥40%, инструмент цел',done:won},{id:'cargo',title:'Бережная буксировка',detail:'Фургон ≥60%, инструмент цел',done:won&&full},{id:'careful',title:'Без обрывов',detail:'Весь груз, кузов ≥70%, трос цел',done:won&&full&&this.condition>=70&&this.tow.breaks===0}];}
  };
  return {key,story,route,init,methods};
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=create;else root.RecoveryModel=create;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

'use strict';
function sx(x){return W*.36+(x-cam.x)*scale;}function sy(y){return H*.55-(y-cam.y)*scale;}
function path(points,fill,stroke,width){ctx.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1]):ctx.moveTo(p[0],p[1]));ctx.closePath();if(fill){ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();}if(stroke){ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=width||1;ctx.stroke();}}
function circle(x,y,r,color){ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,Math.max(0,r),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
function line(x,y,a,b,color,w){ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(a,b);ctx.stroke();}
function hash(n){let x=Math.sin(n*127.1+311.7)*43758.5453;return x-Math.floor(x);}
function pine(x,y,size,color){ScenerySprites.pine(ctx,x,y,size,color,save.quality);}

function cloud(x,y,r,night){ctx.globalAlpha=night?.11:.20;let g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,y-r,0,y+r);g.addColorStop(0,'#fff1d6');g.addColorStop(1,'#d0ded2');for(let i=0;i<6;i++)circle(x+(i-2.5)*r*.55,y-Math.sin(i*1.3)*r*.16,r*(.5+hash(i+17)*.25),g);ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
function background(){let id=sim&&['drive','pause','fork','result','loading','unloading'].includes(mode)?CP.routes[sim.id].region:CP.routes[selected].region,night=id===2;const weather=CP.weatherAt(sim&&['drive','pause','fork','result','loading','unloading'].includes(mode)?sim.id:selected,sim?sim.time:0);let look=skyPalette(REGION_LOOKS[id],weather),g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);g.addColorStop(0,look.sky[0]);g.addColorStop(.53,look.sky[1]);g.addColorStop(1,look.sky[2]);ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
let sunX=W*.78-cam.x*.15,sunY=H*.20;let glow=ctx.createRadialGradient(sunX,sunY,2,sunX,sunY,H*.3);glow.addColorStop(0,night?'#c7d8bd28':'#ffe3ac55');glow.addColorStop(1,'#ffe3ac00');ctx.fillStyle=glow;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.globalAlpha=['rain','storm','fog','blizzard'].includes(weather.type)?.22:1;circle(sunX,sunY,H*.059,night?'#e6e0be':'#ffe1a4');if(night){circle(sunX-H*.025,sunY-H*.02,H*.062,'#1c3047');for(let i=0;i<55;i++)circle(hash(i+10)*W,hash(i+99)*H*.44,hash(i+27)>0.8?1.2:.65,'#e3e7d49a');}
ctx.globalAlpha=1;if(save.quality!=='low')for(let i=0;i<4;i++)cloud((i*W*.35+W*.2-(cam.x*scale*.028))%(W*1.4),H*(.14+hash(i)*.17),H*(.035+hash(i+8)*.012),night);
cloudWeatherBackdrop(weather);mountainLayers(id,night);
skyLight(id);detailedScenery(id,night);
if(save.effects){let haze=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,H*.4,0,H*.74);haze.addColorStop(0,'#cecbb200');haze.addColorStop(.6,night?'#9dbfc90c':'#d3d1b315');haze.addColorStop(1,'#cecbb200');ctx.fillStyle=haze;ctx.fillRect(0,H*.4,W,H*.4);}
}
function terrain(){let routeId=sim&&['drive','pause','fork','result','loading','unloading'].includes(mode)?sim.roadId:selected,id=CP.routes[routeId].region,from=cam.x-W*.4/scale-4,to=cam.x+W*.72/scale+4;const mesh=CP.groundVertices(routeId),visible=mesh.slice(Math.max(0,Math.floor((from+30)*2)),Math.min(mesh.length,Math.ceil((to+30)*2)+1));if(visible.length<2)return;let surface=visible.map(p=>[sx(p.x),sy(p.y)]),pts=[[surface[0][0],H+10],...surface,[surface[surface.length-1][0],H+10]];let look=REGION_LOOKS[id],g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,H*.48,0,H);g.addColorStop(0,look.earth[0]);g.addColorStop(.30,look.earth[1]);g.addColorStop(1,look.earth[2]);path(pts,g);ctx.beginPath();surface.forEach((p,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(...p):ctx.moveTo(...p));ctx.strokeStyle=look.road;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(5,scale*.17);ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle='#e4cc966e';ctx.lineWidth=1.3;ctx.stroke();
if(save.quality!=='low'){ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();pts.forEach((p,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(...p):ctx.moveTo(...p));ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();if(save.quality!=='low'){for(let layer=0;layer<4;layer++){ctx.beginPath();visible.forEach((p,i)=>{const x=sx(p.x),y=sy(p.y-.55-layer*.72+Math.sin(p.x*.43+layer)*.13);if(i)ctx.lineTo(x,y);else ctx.moveTo(x,y);});ctx.strokeStyle=layer%2?'#f4d6ad18':'#12323a30';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,scale*.05);ctx.stroke();}}for(let x=Math.floor(from/.65)*.65;x<to;x+=.65){let ground=CP.groundHeight(x,routeId);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){let y=ground-.30-j*.9-hash(x*12+j)*.4;let r=scale*(.025+hash(x*22+j)*.055);circle(sx(x+.23*hash(j+x)),sy(y),r,j%2?'#b3a47928':'#182e3037');}}ctx.restore();}
for(let x=Math.floor(from/2)*2;x<to;x+=2){let y=CP.groundHeight(x,routeId),h=hash(x+id*200);let br=CP.bridges[routeId],gap=CP.gaps[routeId];if(br&&x>br.from-1&&x<br.to+1||gap&&x>gap.from-1&&x<gap.to+1)continue;if(x>18&&h>.83&&x<CP.routes[routeId].length-15){if(id===1)path([[sx(x-.5),sy(y)+2],[sx(x-.35),sy(y+.45)],[sx(x+.1),sy(y+.61)],[sx(x+.5),sy(y)]],'#6d7560','#52634d',1);else {for(let i=0;i<3;i++)line(sx(x+i*.10),sy(y),sx(x+i*.09-.05),sy(y+.20+i*.05),'#84905b',1.5);}}else if(h<.21){path([[sx(x-.3),sy(y)],[sx(x-.16),sy(y+.13)],[sx(x+.04),sy(y+.18)],[sx(x+.3),sy(y)]],'#929a85','#525f54',1);}else if(save.quality!=='low'){for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(sx(x+i*.08),sy(y),sx(x+i*.07-.03),sy(y+.12+hash(x+i)*.13),'#809764aa',1);}}
if(sim){let end=sim.route.length+6,x=sx(end),y=sy(CP.height(end,routeId));if(x<W+250&&x>-300){line(x,y,x,y-scale*3.25,'#d5d1b8',scale*.055);path([[x,y-scale*3.25],[x+scale*1.1,y-scale*3.07],[x,y-scale*2.56]],'#edb263');ctx.fillStyle='#ffe2a2';ctx.font='bold '+Math.round(scale*.24)+'px sans-serif';ctx.fillText(sim.id===8?'СТАНЦИЯ 17':'ПРИЁМКА',x+scale*.1,y-scale*3.04);let bx=x+scale*1.5;ctx.fillStyle='#364b48';ctx.fillRect(bx,y-scale*2.2,scale*3.4,scale*2.2);path([[bx-scale*.18,y-scale*2.2],[bx+scale*1.7,y-scale*3.2],[bx+scale*3.55,y-scale*2.2]],'#a77a59','#d4b184',1);ctx.fillStyle='#20343b';ctx.fillRect(bx+scale*.5,y-scale*1.7,scale*1.9,scale*1.7);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(bx+scale*.5,y-scale*(.4+i*.4),bx+scale*2.4,y-scale*(.4+i*.4),'#47605b',2);ctx.fillStyle='#c5ab70';ctx.fillRect(bx+scale*2.7,y-scale*1.5,scale*.5,scale*.65);}}

function pose(body){let p=body.getPosition(),a=body.getAngle?body.getAngle():0;let prev=sim&&mode==='drive'&&sim.previous&&sim.previous.get(body);if(prev){let t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,acc*60));return{x:prev.x+(p.x-prev.x)*t,y:prev.y+(p.y-prev.y)*t,a:prev.a+(a-prev.a)*t};}return{x:p.x,y:p.y,a};}
function bodyPoint(body,x,y){let p=pose(body),c=Math.cos(p.a),s=Math.sin(p.a);return[sx(p.x+x*c-y*s),sy(p.y+x*s+y*c)];}
function bpoly(body,points,fill,stroke,w){path(points.map(p=>bodyPoint(body,p[0],p[1])),fill,stroke,w);}
function crate(box){if(box.kind==='liquid')return;if(box.kind==='log'){logCargo(box);return;}let b=box.body,c=box.lost?'#8f896e':'#c29a62';bpoly(b,[[-.32,-.32],[.32,-.32],[.32,.32],[-.32,.32]],c,'#6b5239',scale*.024);for(let y=-.14;y<.3;y+=.16)line(...bodyPoint(b,-.31,y),...bodyPoint(b,.31,y),'#805e3e66',scale*.012);for(let off of [-.22,.22]){line(...bodyPoint(b,off,-.3),...bodyPoint(b,off,.3),'#e1bd82',scale*.07);for(let y of [-.24,.24]){let q=bodyPoint(b,off,y);circle(...q,scale*.018,'#6c6249');}}line(...bodyPoint(b,-.26,-.25),...bodyPoint(b,.26,.25),'#ddbb7c',scale*.05);bpoly(b,[[-.09,-.04],[.08,-.04],[.08,.13],[-.09,.13]],'#eddbb599');cargoLabel(b);}

function vehicleShadow(){if(!sim)return;let p=pose(sim.car),ground=CP.groundHeight(p.x,sim.roadId),height=Math.max(0,p.y-ground-1.1);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(.06,.22-height*.06);ctx.fillStyle='#081b25';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(sx(p.x),sy(ground)+4,scale*(sim.vehicle.width+height*.12),scale*.16,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
function headlights(){if(!sim||sim.route.region!==2||!save.effects)return;let p=sim.car;let start=bodyPoint(p,sim.vehicle.width-.04,.40),farTop=bodyPoint(p,11,1.9),farBottom=bodyPoint(p,11,-2);let g=ctx.createLinearGradient(start[0],start[1],farTop[0],farTop[1]);g.addColorStop(0,'#f7e5aa44');g.addColorStop(1,'#ffe8b100');path([start,farTop,farBottom],g);}
function dust(dt){if(!save.effects){particles=[];return;}if(sim&&mode==='drive'&&gas&&sim.groundCount>0&&(Math.abs(sim.car.getLinearVelocity().x)>1||sim.slipTime>.25)&&Math.random()<(save.quality==='low'?.22:.6)){let p=sim.wheels[0].getPosition(),surface=CP.surfaceAt(p.x,sim.roadId),wet=['wet','water'].includes(surface.type);particles.push({x:p.x-.25,y:p.y-.38,vx:-1-Math.random()*2,vy:.4+Math.random()*(wet?2:1),life:wet?.65:.9,age:0,size:wet?.04:.07+Math.random()*.12,color:wet?'#86aaa1':surface.type==='mud'?'#897548':'#d9c399'});}for(let p of particles){p.age+=dt;p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,(1-p.age/p.life)*.28);circle(sx(p.x),sy(p.y),scale*p.size*(1+p.age*3),p.color||'#d9c399');}ctx.globalAlpha=1;particles=particles.filter(p=>p.age<p.life);}

 }

'use strict';
(function(root){
 function create({routes,profiles,zones,landmarks,height,gaps}){
  const key='ridge:25',camp=82;
  const base={name:'Гребень',region:0,kind:'delivery',cargo:'ridge',requires:'rover',length:149,boxes:1,reward:0,seed:71,weather:'clear',fuelPoints:[],stops:[],services:[],sectors:[]};
  routes[key]={...base};profiles[key]=[[0,0],[16,0],[25,2.4],[28,2.4],[37,0],[43,1.8],[47,2.8],[51,.9],[55,.9],[57,.2],[67,4.5],[70,4.5],[80,.6],[87,.6],[96,4],[99,4],[108,-.5],[114,2],[120,-.5],[123,-.5],[132,5.5],[135,5.5],[143,0],[149,0]];
  gaps[key]={from:47,to:51,y:2.8,depth:3.5};zones[key]=[];landmarks[key]=[];
  function init(s,options={}){const load=options.load===3?3:1;s.roadId=key;s.ridge={load,stage:0,waiting:false,landings:0,awardX:0,airTime:0,peakFall:0,roofTime:0,stallTime:0};return {...base,boxes:load};}
  function vehicle(m,load){return {...m,density:2.1,wheelDensity:1.5,torque:36,speed:19.5,spring:3.1,damping:.60,angularDamping:.45,airTorque:22,gripFactor:1.15,cargoX:load===1?-.8:m.cargoX};}
  function valid(d){const r=d?.ridge25;return !!r&&d.id===0&&d.car==='rover'&&d.setup==='standard'&&d.levels.every(x=>x===0)&&!d.journey&&!d.expedition&&!d.waterRun&&!d.recoveryRun&&!d.dispatchTerms&&r.edition===25&&[1,3].includes(r.load)&&[0,1].includes(r.stage)&&typeof r.waiting==='boolean'&&(!r.waiting||r.stage===0||!!d.ridge28)&&Number.isInteger(r.landings)&&r.landings>=0&&r.landings<=1000&&Number.isFinite(r.awardX)&&r.awardX>=0&&r.awardX<(d.ridge28?500:200)&&['airTime','peakFall','roofTime','stallTime'].every(k=>Number.isFinite(r[k])&&r[k]>=0&&r[k]<100);}
  const methods={
   ridgeContinue(){if(this.edition!==25||!this.ridge.waiting||this.state!=='drive')return false;this.ridge.stage=1;this.ridge.waiting=false;return true;},
   ridgeDeliver(){if(this.edition!==25||!this.ridge.waiting||this.state!=='drive'||!this.remaining())return false;this.ridge.waiting=false;this.end('delivered');return true;},
   ridgeScore(){return this.state==='win'?this.remaining()*(this.ridge.stage?250:100)+this.ridge.landings:0;},
   ridgeUpdate(dt,wasAir){const r=this.ridge,p=this.car.getPosition(),v=this.car.getLinearVelocity(),air=this.groundCount===0;
    if(air){r.airTime=Math.min(10,r.airTime+dt);r.peakFall=Math.max(r.peakFall,-v.y);}else if(wasAir){const slope=Math.atan((height(p.x+1,key)-height(p.x-1,key))/2),a=Math.atan2(Math.sin(this.car.getAngle()-slope),Math.cos(this.car.getAngle()-slope));if(r.airTime>.20&&r.peakFall<7&&Math.abs(a)<.35&&this.remaining()&&p.x>r.awardX+6){r.awardX=p.x;const points=10*this.remaining();r.landings+=points;this.events.push({type:'ridgeLanding',points});}r.airTime=0;r.peakFall=0;}
    r.stallTime=Math.abs(v.x)<.15&&this.throttle>.2?Math.min(10,r.stallTime+dt):0;if(r.stallTime>=2&&r.stallTime-dt<2)this.events.push({type:'ridgeStall'});
    const a=Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(this.car.getAngle()),Math.cos(this.car.getAngle())));r.roofTime=a>2&&p.y<height(p.x,key)+2.3?r.roofTime+dt:0;if(r.roofTime>.35)this.end('flipped');
    if(this.state==='drive'&&r.stage===0&&p.x>=camp&&this.groundCount>=2&&a<.55){r.waiting=true;this.events.push({type:'ridgeCamp'});}
   }
  };
  return {key,camp,distance:base.length*3,init,vehicle,valid,methods};
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=create;else root.Ridge25Model=create;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

(function(){

'use strict';
// Procedural sound; no network, recordings or audio assets required.
const RoadAudio={
 context:null,master:null,engine:null,road:null,wind:null,filter:null,tones:[],lastVy:0,lastHit:-10,
 init(volume){
  if(!this.context){
   const a=this.context=new(window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
   this.master=a.createGain();this.master.connect(a.destination);
   this.engine=a.createGain();this.engine.gain.value=0;this.engine.connect(this.master);
   const low=a.createBiquadFilter();low.type='lowpass';low.frequency.value=420;low.connect(this.engine);this.filter=low;
   for(const [type,hz,level] of [['sawtooth',44,.35],['triangle',22,.65],['sine',88,.22]]){
    const o=a.createOscillator(),g=a.createGain();o.type=type;o.frequency.value=hz;g.gain.value=level;o.connect(g);g.connect(low);o.start();this.tones.push(o);
   }
   const buffer=a.createBuffer(1,a.sampleRate*2,a.sampleRate),samples=buffer.getChannelData(0);
   for(let i=0;i<samples.length;i++)samples[i]=Math.random()*2-1;
   const noise=a.createBufferSource();noise.buffer=buffer;noise.loop=true;noise.start();this.noise=buffer;
   this.road=a.createGain();this.road.gain.value=0;
   const roadFilter=a.createBiquadFilter();roadFilter.type='bandpass';roadFilter.frequency.value=650;roadFilter.Q.value=.55;noise.connect(roadFilter);roadFilter.connect(this.road);this.road.connect(this.master);this.roadFilter=roadFilter;
   this.wind=a.createGain();this.wind.gain.value=0;const windFilter=a.createBiquadFilter();windFilter.type='lowpass';windFilter.frequency.value=260;noise.connect(windFilter);windFilter.connect(this.wind);this.wind.connect(this.master);
  }
  this.master.gain.value=volume/100;this.context.resume().catch(()=>{});return this.context;
 },
 mute(){if(!this.context)return;const t=this.context.currentTime;for(const g of [this.engine,this.road,this.wind]){g.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t);g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t);}},
 update(sim,active,gas,brake){
  if(!this.context)return;if(!active||!sim){this.mute();this.lastVy=0;return;}
  const a=this.context,t=a.currentTime,v=sim.car.getLinearVelocity(),speed=Math.abs(v.x),ground=sim.groundCount>0,surface=CargoPhysics.surfaceAt(sim.car.getPosition().x,sim.roadId).type;
  const hz=32+Math.min(75,Math.abs(sim.wheels[0].getAngularVelocity())*2)+(gas?18:0)+(sim.vehicle.id==='scrambler'?15:sim.vehicle.id==='tanker'?-10:sim.vehicle.id==='timber'?-6:sim.vehicle.id==='mule'?-7:0);
  this.tones.forEach((o,i)=>o.frequency.setTargetAtTime(hz*[1,.5,2][i],t,.08));
  this.filter.frequency.setTargetAtTime(gas?500:240,t,.15);this.engine.gain.setTargetAtTime(brake?.028:gas?.065:.032,t,.09);
  this.roadFilter.frequency.setTargetAtTime(['wet','water'].includes(surface)?1600:surface==='mud'?320:sim.bridge.length&&sim.car.getPosition().x>CargoPhysics.bridges[sim.id].from&&sim.car.getPosition().x<CargoPhysics.bridges[sim.id].to?900:650,t,.2);
  this.road.gain.setTargetAtTime(ground?Math.min(.055,speed*.003)*(surface==='wet'?1.35:1):0,t,.12);
  this.wind.gain.setTargetAtTime(Math.min(.028,speed*.0009),t,.2);
  if(ground&&this.lastVy<-2&&v.y-this.lastVy>2.5&&sim.time-this.lastHit>.35){this.hit(Math.min(1,(v.y-this.lastVy)/10));this.lastHit=sim.time;}
  this.lastVy=v.y;
 },
 hit(strength=.5){if(!this.context)return;const a=this.context,t=a.currentTime,o=a.createOscillator(),g=a.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(110,t);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(38,t+.15);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.06*strength,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+.18);o.connect(g);g.connect(this.master);o.start();o.stop(t+.2);},
 event(kind){if(!this.context)return;const a=this.context,t=a.currentTime;
  const notes=kind==='win'?[392,494,587,784]:kind==='lost'?[190,110]:kind==='fuel'?[262,392]:[660,880];
  notes.forEach((hz,i)=>{const o=a.createOscillator(),g=a.createGain(),at=t+i*.09;o.type=kind==='lost'?'triangle':'sine';o.frequency.value=hz;g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,at);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(.04,at+.012);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,at+.16);o.connect(g);g.connect(this.master);o.start(at);o.stop(at+.18);});
 },
 musicGain:null,musicVoices:new Set(),musicNext:0,musicBeat:0,musicRegion:-1,
 stopMusic(){
  if(this.musicGain)this.musicGain.gain.value=0;
  for(const voice of this.musicVoices){try{voice.stop();}catch(e){}}this.musicVoices.clear();this.musicNext=0;
 },
 musicNote(hz,start,duration,level){
  const a=this.context,o=a.createOscillator(),g=a.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=hz;o.connect(g);g.connect(this.musicGain);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,start);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(level,start+.035);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,start+duration);
  o.start(start);o.stop(start+duration+.02);this.musicVoices.add(o);o.onended=()=>{this.musicVoices.delete(o);o.disconnect();g.disconnect();};
 },
 updateMusic(mode,enabled){
  if(!this.context)return;
  if(!enabled||['boot','pause','settings'].includes(mode)||this.context.state!=='running'){if(this.musicNext)this.stopMusic();return;}
  const a=this.context,region=typeof selected==='number'?CargoPhysics.routes[selected].region:0;
  if(!this.musicGain){this.musicGain=a.createGain();this.musicGain.connect(this.master);}
  this.musicGain.gain.value=mode==='drive'?.12:.24;
  if(this.musicRegion!==region){this.stopMusic();this.musicRegion=region;this.musicBeat=0;this.musicGain.gain.value=mode==='drive'?.12:.24;}
  if(!this.musicNext||this.musicNext<a.currentTime-.2)this.musicNext=a.currentTime+.03;
  const roots=[146.83,130.81,174.61,110],melody=[0,7,12,7,3,7,10,7,0,7,15,12,7,3,7,0];
  while(this.musicNext<a.currentTime+.13){
   const beat=this.musicBeat++,root=roots[Math.floor(beat/8)%4]*(region===1?1.122:region===2?.891:1),at=this.musicNext;
   this.musicNote(root*Math.pow(2,melody[beat%16]/12),at,.72,.20);
   if(beat%8===0){this.musicNote(root/2,at,2.6,.24);this.musicNote(root*Math.pow(2,7/12),at,2.2,.08);}
   this.musicNext+=region===2?.43:.36;
  }
 }
};

'use strict';
let checkpointAt=-5,newRunIntent=null;
function pendingRun(){return save.activeRun&&!save.settledRuns.includes(save.activeRun.attempt)?save.activeRun:null;}
function checkpointRun(force=false){
 if(!sim||sim.state!=='drive'||sim.paid||sim.started===false)return false;
 if(!force&&sim.time-checkpointAt<5)return true;
 const data=RunCheckpoint.capture(sim,CP);if(!data)return false;data.coach=lesson.active?{step:lesson.step,held:lesson.held}:null;
 save.activeRun=data;checkpointAt=sim.time;const ok=persist();
 if(force){$('pause-save-status').textContent=ok?'Рейс сохранён. Можно закрыть игру.':'Не удалось сохранить. Продолжи рейс и попробуй позже.';$('pause-save-status').classList.toggle('save-error',!ok);}
 return ok;
}
function updateResumeHome(){
 const run=pendingRun();$('home-play-label').textContent=run?'ПРОДОЛЖИТЬ РЕЙС':'ИГРАТЬ';$('home-current-route').textContent=run?CP.routes[run.id].name+' · '+Math.floor(run.state.distance)+' м · бак '+Math.floor(run.state.fuel)+'%':CP.routes[selected].name+' · '+CP.routes[selected].length*3+' м';
 $('saved-run-note').textContent=run?'Машина и груз ждут там, где ты остановился.':'';visibility('saved-run-note',!!run);if(run){$('home-job-kind').textContent='СОХРАНЁННЫЙ РЕЙС';$('home-weather').textContent=CP.weatherAt(run.id,run.state.time).name;$('home-work-info').textContent=timeText(run.state.time)+' в пути · кузов '+Math.floor(run.state.condition)+'%';}
}
function resumeSavedRun(){
 const data=pendingRun();if(!data)return false;const restored=RunCheckpoint.restore(data,CP);
 if(!restored){save.activeRun=null;persist();wallet();$('saved-run-note').textContent='Этот рейс восстановить не удалось. Можно начать новый.';visibility('saved-run-note',true);return false;}
 sim=restored;selected=sim.id;save.selected=selected;save.vehicle=sim.vehicle.id;region=sim.route.region;dispatch.phase=null;checkpointAt=sim.time;clearInput();acc=0;particles=[];resetCoach();if(data.coach&&Number.isInteger(data.coach.step)&&data.coach.step>=0&&data.coach.step<LESSONS.length&&Number.isFinite(data.coach.held)&&data.coach.held>=0&&data.coach.held<=60){lesson={active:true,step:data.coach.step,held:data.coach.held};showLesson();}configureRunHud();cam={x:sim.car.getPosition().x+(sim.edition===23?-3.5:3),y:sim.car.getPosition().y+.8};const br=sim.nextLiftBridge();scale=Math.min(H/(br&&Math.abs(sim.car.getPosition().x-br.control)<18?13.5:sim.edition===23?10.3:sim.vehicle.axles?10.3:9.5),W/(sim.edition===23?29:br?34:sim.vehicle.axles?29:26));
 setPage('pause');$('pause-copy').textContent='Рейс восстановлен. }

'use strict';
// Continuous land/water planes cover the entire view, including deep valleys.
// Objects use world cell IDs, so movement and tile boundaries never reroll detail.
function sceneryY(depth){return Math.max(-H*.13,Math.min(H*.17,cam.y*scale*depth));}
function sceneryContour(n,layer){return H*(.66+layer*.08)+sceneryY(.10+layer*.035)-H*(.015+hash(n+layer*91)*.05);}
function sceneryCabin(x,y,k,night,snow){
 ctx.fillStyle=night?'#253e47':'#5a7761';ctx.fillRect(x,y-k*.62,k,k*.62);
 path([[x-k*.09,y-k*.62],[x+k*.45,y-k*.99],[x+k*1.09,y-k*.62]],snow?'#e1e9dc':night?'#213642':'#49625a');
 if(save.quality!=='low'){for(let i=1;i<6;i++)line(x,y-k*i*.10,x+k,y-k*i*.10,night?'#819ca617':'#233c3a38',1);line(x+k*.04,y-k*.61,x+k*.45,y-k*.9,'#d1d7ac40',1);}
 ctx.fillStyle=night?'#edd498':'#a7c0ac';ctx.fillRect(x+k*.16,y-k*.44,k*.18,k*.2);ctx.fillRect(x+k*.62,y-k*.44,k*.18,k*.2);line(x+k*.25,y-k*.44,x+k*.25,y-k*.24,'#384f49',1);
 ctx.fillStyle='#2b4845';ctx.fillRect(x+k*.41,y-k*.39,k*.15,k*.39);ctx.fillStyle=night?'#354e54':'#586c5a';ctx.fillRect(x+k*.70,y-k*.95,k*.12,k*.24);
 if(save.effects){ctx.strokeStyle='#c5d3c82b';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+k*.76,y-k*.97);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+k*.6,y-k*1.08,x+k*.95,y-k*1.14,x+k*.74,y-k*1.25);ctx.stroke();}
}
function sceneryGrove(x,y,k,n,id){
 if(id===5){ScenerySprites.pine(ctx,x,y,k,'#456878',save.quality,true);return;}
 if(Math.abs(n)%4!==0&&id!==4){pine(x,y,k,id===2?'#193c43':'#28594a');return;}
 ScenerySprites.grove(ctx,x,y,k,n,id,save.quality);
}
function detailedScenery(id,night){
 const low=save.quality==='low',water=id===3||id===4;
 if(water){const horizon=H*(id===3?.48:.58)+sceneryY(.055),g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,horizon,0,H);g.addColorStop(0,id===3?'#84beba':'#547f71');g.addColorStop(.35,id===3?'#4d999c':'#3c6e63');g.addColorStop(1,id===3?'#244f65':'#254d45');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,horizon,W,H-horizon+1);
  // Irregular short reflections have no common lower edge.
  for(let j=0;j<(low?18:65);j++){const row=hash(j+90),x=(hash(j+700)*W-cam.x*scale*.045+j*3)%(W+70),y=horizon+row*(H-horizon);line(x,y,x+8+row*33,y,'#d2ede42a',.6+row);}
 }
 const landColors=id===1?['#837e66','#656e59','#4e6253']:id===5?['#94b5bb','#72959f','#547b8c']:night?['#294953','#22434a','#19383f']:id===4?['#587c63','#426b55','#315a49']:['#638c70','#477759','#315e4b'];
 const layers=low?2:3;
 for(let layer=0;layer<layers;layer++){
  const depth=.12+layer*.07,cell=low?125:92,shift=cam.x*scale*depth,begin=Math.floor(shift/cell)-2,end=begin+Math.ceil(W/cell)+5;
  const base=n=>sceneryContour(n,layer)+(id===3?H*.10:0);const pts=[];for(let n=begin;n<=end;n++)pts.push([n*cell-shift,base(n)]);
  path([[pts[0][0],H+H*.3],...pts,[pts[pts.length-1][0],H+H*.3]],id===3?['#78947a','#567d6a','#396455'][layer]:landColors[layer]);
  for(let n=begin;n<end;n++){
   const x=n*cell-shift,y=base(n),k=H*(.11+hash(n+layer*83)*.075);
   if(id===1||id===3){if(id===1||Math.abs(n)%3===0){path([[x-cell*.25,y+2],[x-cell*.20,y-k*.20],[x-cell*.05,y-k*.30],[x+cell*.16,y-k*.19],[x+cell*.24,y+3]],id===3?'#789582':'#8b8971');path([[x-cell*.05,y-k*.30],[x+cell*.16,y-k*.19],[x+cell*.24,y+3],[x+cell*.05,y-k*.05]],'#284a3e27');if(!low){line(x-cell*.17,y-k*.16,x+cell*.12,y-k*.10,'#d0c3a839',1);line(x-cell*.15,y-k*.05,x+cell*.20,y-k*.01,'#273f3a40',1);}}}
   else sceneryGrove(x,y,k,n,id);
   if(layer===0&&Math.abs(n)%5===0&&id!==1){if(id===3){const py=H*.53+sceneryY(.055);path([[x-14,py],[x+29,py],[x+20,py+7],[x-8,py+7]],'#416670');line(x+7,py,x+7,py-36,'#69847d',1.5);path([[x+9,py-35],[x+9,py-2],[x+29,py-2]],'#e2e4c6');}else sceneryCabin(x+cell*.38,y,H*.095,night,id===5);}
   if(layer===1&&id===1&&Math.abs(n)%4===0){const top=y-k*1.45;line(x,y,x,top,'#4c5c50',3);line(x,top,x+cell*1.3,top+k*.12,'#576254',3);line(x,top,x+cell*.6,top-k*.16,'#61745c',1);line(x+cell*.6,top-k*.16,x+cell*1.3,top+k*.12,'#61745c',1);line(x+cell,top+k*.1,x+cell,y-k*.3,'#455c4f',1);ctx.fillStyle='#b39d6359';ctx.fillRect(x-7,top+3,14,10);}
   if(!low&&layer===1&&id===3&&Math.abs(n)%7===0){const h=H*.19,top=y-h;path([[x-9,y],[x-6,top],[x+6,top],[x+10,y]],'#c9d2ad');for(let j=0;j<3;j++)path([[x-8,y-h*(.13+j*.27)],[x+9,y-h*(.13+j*.27)],[x+8,y-h*(.23+j*.27)],[x-7,y-h*(.23+j*.27)]],'#987e6a');ctx.fillStyle='#466d75';ctx.fillRect(x-8,top-9,16,9);path([[x-11,top-9],[x,top-16],[x+11,top-9]],'#315361');circle(x,top-4,2,'#f0d897');line(x+14,y+2,x+55,y+2,'#9aa481',2);for(let off of [18,37,52])line(x+off,y+2,x+off,y+9,'#557464',2);}
   if(!low&&layer===1&&night&&Math.abs(n)%4===0){sceneryCabin(x+30,y,H*.075,true,false);sceneryCabin(x+63,y+3,H*.055,true,false);const top=y-H*.14;line(x+13,y,x+13,top,'#314e53',2);line(x+13,top,x+26,top,'#314e53',2);circle(x+26,top,2,'#dacb97');}
   if(!low&&layer===2){
    for(let j=0;j<10;j++){const px=x+hash(n*31+j)*cell,py=y+H*(.03+hash(n*67+j)*.25);circle(px,py,.6+hash(n*53+j)*1.5,id===5?'#dce9df20':'#b8bd7f14');}
    if(id===4){for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const px=x+j*7,py=y+4;line(px,py,px-2,py-12-hash(n+j)*10,'#869b68',1);line(px-2,py-17,px-2,py-25,'#b3996450',2);}}
    if(id===0&&Math.abs(n)%6===0){const py=y+7;ctx.fillStyle='#715e43';ctx.fillRect(x,py-7,8,7);path([[x-3,py],[x+11,py],[x+8,py-3],[x,py-3]],'#574f39');ctx.fillStyle='#ae9963';ctx.fillRect(x-1,py-8,10,2);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)line(x+15+j*6,py-7,x+15+j*6,py+2,'#a1a88680',1);line(x+14,py-4,x+29,py-4,'#89957460',1);}
   }
   if(layer===2&&!low){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const px=x+j*cell*.23,py=y+H*(.03+hash(n+j)*.065);if(id===1||id===3){path([[px,py],[px+5,py-4],[px+12,py],[px+7,py+3]],'#c8c7a220');}else line(px,py,px-2,py-5-hash(n+j)*7,id===5?'#d4e6da38':'#a5b97b42',1);}}
  }
 }
 if(!low){
  // Meandering streams and layered quarry strata add detail below the skyline.
  const shift=cam.x*scale*.22;
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.25;
  if(id===1){for(let j=0;j<3;j++){ctx.strokeStyle=j%2?'#c6b68a':'#243e36';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();for(let x=-30;x<W+40;x+=25){const y=H*(.84+j*.045)+sceneryY(.17)+Math.sin((x+shift)*.005+j)*H*.022;x===-30?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);}ctx.stroke();}}
  else if(id===0||id===4){ctx.strokeStyle=id===4?'#91c1a9':'#8bbeb3';ctx.lineWidth=H*.011;ctx.beginPath();for(let x=-30;x<W+40;x+=20){const y=H*.87+sceneryY(.18)+Math.sin((x+shift)*.008)*H*.035;x===-30?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);}ctx.stroke();}
  ctx.restore();
 }
}



==============================================================================
FILE: project/game/assets/scenery-sprites.js
==============================================================================
'use strict';
// Vector details are rasterized once, then reused at subpixel world positions.
// Native transparent canvases keep this entirely offline and bound memory.
const ScenerySprites=(()=>{
 const cache=new Map(),limit=24;let hits=0,misses=0;
 function pine(target,x,y,size,color,quality,snow=false){
  const key=color+':'+(quality==='low'?'low':'detailed')+':'+snow;let sprite=cache.get(key);
  if(!sprite){misses++;const image=document.createElement('canvas'),k=192,d=2;image.width=k*d;image.height=Math.ceil(k*1.06)*d;const g=image.getContext('2d');g.scale(d,d);const cx=k/2,base=k*1.02;
   const polygon=(pts,fill)=>{g.beginPath();pts.forEach((p,i)=>i?g.lineTo(p[0],p[1]):g.moveTo(p[0],p[1]));g.closePath();g.fillStyle=fill;g.fill();};
   const stroke=(a,b,c,e,fill,w)=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a,b);g.lineTo(c,e);g.strokeStyle=fill;g.lineWidth=w;g.stroke();};
   g.fillStyle='#2a4032';g.fillRect(cx-k*.024,base-k*.32,k*.05,k*.34);stroke(cx-k*.012,base-k*.31,cx-k*.012,base,'#82905d50',k*.015);
   for(let j=0;j<7;j++){const at=j/7,yy=base-k+k*at*.84,width=k*(.06+at*.32),height=k*(.20+at*.06),tip=(hash(j+576)-.5)*k*.027;polygon([[cx+tip,yy],[cx-width,yy+height],[cx-width*.52,yy+height*.83],[cx-width*.11,yy+height],[cx+width*.19,yy+height*.84],[cx+width*.65,yy+height],[cx+width,yy+height*.94]],color);polygon([[cx+tip,yy],[cx-width,yy+height],[cx-width*.08,yy+height*.86]],'#172f3036');if(quality!=='low')for(let n=0;n<3;n++)stroke(cx,yy+height*.35,cx+width*(n%2?-.65:.66),yy+height*(.78+n*.06),'#b1c79e16',1);}
   if(snow)for(let j=1;j<5;j++)polygon([[cx,base-k*(1-j*.13)],[cx-k*j*.065,base-k*(.86-j*.13)],[cx+k*j*.065,base-k*(.86-j*.13)]],'#dce8dbaa');
   sprite=image;if(cache.size>=limit)cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value);cache.set(key,sprite);
  }else{hits++;cache.delete(key);cache.set(key,sprite);}
  target.drawImage(sprite,x-size*.5,y-size*1.02,size,size*1.06);
 }
 function grove(target,x,y,size,n,id,quality){
  const variant=((n%8)+8)%8,night=id===2,key='grove:'+night+':'+variant+':'+(quality==='low'?'low':'detailed');let sprite=cache.get(key);
  if(!sprite){misses++;const image=document.createElement('canvas'),k=128,d=2;image.width=Math.ceil(k*1.4)*d;image.height=Math.ceil(k*1.3)*d;const g=image.getContext('2d');g.scale(d,d);const cx=k*.7,base=k*1.15;g.strokeStyle=night?'#1e3941':'#3a5946';g.lineWidth=k*.055;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(cx,base);g.lineTo(cx-k*.025,base-k*.8);g.stroke();
   for(let j=0;j<6;j++){const px=cx+(j-2.5)*k*.095,py=base-k*(.68+hash(variant*7+j)*.16);g.beginPath();g.arc(px,py,k*(.16+hash(variant+j)*.05),0,Math.PI*2);g.fillStyle=night?'#234950':['#64886a','#426f58','#527e61'][j%3];g.fill();if(quality!=='low'){g.beginPath();g.arc(px-k*.04,py-k*.065,k*.06,0,Math.PI*2);g.fillStyle='#a7c79618';g.fill();}}
   sprite=image;if(cache.size>=limit)cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value);cache.set(key,sprite);
  }else{hits++;cache.delete(key);cache.set(key,sprite);}target.drawImage(sprite,x-size*.7,y-size*1.15,size*1.4,size*1.3);
 }
 return {pine,grove,get stats(){return {hits,misses,entries:cache.size,limit};}};
})();

'use strict';
function tankerRig(body,wheels,preview){
 const m=vehicleLook(preview?currentVehicle():sim.vehicle),k=scale,metal=vehicleMetal(body,m,1.6);
 panel(body,[[-2.59,-.15],[2.61,-.15],[2.61,-.32],[-2.59,-.32]],'#263e3d');wheels.forEach((wheel,i)=>{shock(body,wheel,m.axles[i]);vehicleWheel(wheel,m);});
 panel(body,[[.67,.05],[.79,1.45],[2.12,1.45],[2.47,.59],[2.62,.42],[2.67,.05]],metal);
 panel(body,[[.94,.70],[1.06,1.27],[1.99,1.27],[2.23,.69]],'#48757d');drawMax(body,1.46,1.04,2.04,.70,k,preview);bodyLine(body,.94,1.4,2.07,1.4,'#ede3bb',.04);bodyLine(body,1.76,.48,2.02,.48,'#314c43',.045);
 panel(body,[[2.3,.18],[2.63,.18],[2.63,.48],[2.29,.48]],'#29433d');for(let y=.23;y<.46;y+=.055)bodyLine(body,2.34,y,2.59,y,'#bdbd99',.016);panel(body,[[2.38,.49],[2.64,.49],[2.64,.63],[2.34,.63]],'#ecdbac');bodyLine(body,2.32,.04,2.79,.04,'#bdc8a6',.075);
 // The rounded outline follows the reservoir collision envelope.
 const pts=[[-2.16,.4],[.58,.4],[.73,.51],[.85,.80],[.85,1.25],[.71,1.55],[.55,1.66],[-2.15,1.66],[-2.46,1.54],[-2.69,1.30],[-2.78,1.03],[-2.68,.73],[-2.46,.51]];
 panel(body,pts,metal);bpoly(body,[[-2.4,1.47],[.55,1.47],[.74,1.27],[.75,1.05],[-2.67,1.05],[-2.62,1.27]],'#e0dabc44');bodyLine(body,-2.45,.65,.67,.65,'#6b776851',.04);
 for(const x of [-1.82,-.22]){bodyLine(body,x,.44,x,1.61,'#707c6c',.064);bodyLine(body,x+.035,.47,x+.035,1.60,'#d7d6ac',.02);}
 panel(body,[[-1.36,1.64],[-1.36,1.77],[-.87,1.77],[-.87,1.64]],'#687a6a');bodyLine(body,-2.19,1.71,.38,1.71,'#d0c7a1',.026);for(const x of [-2.12,.33])bodyLine(body,x,1.63,x,1.93,'#6e8069',.027);bodyLine(body,-2.12,1.93,.33,1.93,'#bebf97',.029);
 panel(body,[[-1.56,.77],[.30,.77],[.30,1.26],[-1.56,1.26]],'#284f53');const ratio=preview?1:mode==='result'?0:(sim.fluidLitres/sim.fluidCapacity)*(dispatch.phase==='loading'?Math.min(1,dispatch.elapsed/dispatch.duration):dispatch.phase==='unloading'?Math.max(0,1-dispatch.elapsed/dispatch.duration):1),shift=preview?Math.sin(clock*1.3)*.035:sim.fluidSlosh;
 const bottom=.79,level=bottom+.44*ratio,tilt=Math.max(-.065,Math.min(.065,shift*.18));bpoly(body,[[-1.53,bottom],[.27,bottom],[.27,level-tilt],[-1.53,level+tilt]],'#79b1a4');bodyLine(body,-1.52,level+tilt,.26,level-tilt,'#d2ded1',.018);
 for(const x of [-.94,-.30])bodyLine(body,x,.79,x,1.24,'#a9bca582',.024);
 vehicleText(body,-.63,1.04,'BRINE / 900L','#f0e3b8',.15);vehicleText(body,1.50,.28,'6×6','#e5d8ac',.15);
 for(const x of [-2.37,-2.17]){bodyLine(body,x,.40,x,1.45,'#4b685a',.023);for(let y=.55;y<1.4;y+=.18)bodyLine(body,-2.38,y,-2.15,y,'#c4c8a5',.024);}
 for(const off of m.axles)panel(body,[[off-.63,-.01],[off-.46,.34],[off+.42,.34],[off+.62,-.01],[off+.48,.04],[off+.31,.21],[off-.32,.21],[off-.46,.03]],'#344e44');
}
function tankerDepots(){
 if(!sim||!sim.fluidCapacity)return;const k=scale;
 for(const stop of sim.stops){const x=sx(stop.x),y=sy(CP.height(stop.x,sim.roadId));if(x<-k*9||x>W+k*8)continue;const color=stop.done?'#86b78b':'#b6cf9c';
 for(let q=stop.x-3;q<stop.x+3;q+=.5)line(sx(q),sy(CP.groundHeight(q,sim.roadId))-.025*k,sx(q+.3),sy(CP.groundHeight(q+.3,sim.roadId))-.025*k,color,Math.max(2,k*.07));
 const bx=x+k*5,by=sy(CP.height(stop.x+5,sim.roadId));path([[bx-k*.75,by],[bx-k*.75,by-k*1.6],[bx-k*.58,by-k*1.95],[bx+k*.58,by-k*1.95],[bx+k*.75,by-k*1.6],[bx+k*.75,by]],'#8fa79b','#304b45',k*.035);line(bx-k*.67,by-k*.29,bx+k*.67,by-k*.29,'#4e7468',k*.04);line(bx-k*.67,by-k*1.5,bx+k*.67,by-k*1.5,'#cdd6b7',k*.03);
 line(x,y,x,y-k*1.9,'#5b7a64',k*.04);circle(x,y-k*2.1,k*.28,color);ctx.fillStyle='#244f3e';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.font='900 '+Math.max(9,k*.26)+'px monospace';ctx.fillText(stop.done?'✓':String(stop.index+1),x,y-k*2.0);ctx.fillStyle='#dfd6b0';ctx.font='bold '+Math.max(7,k*.13)+'px monospace';ctx.fillText(stop.name,bx,by-k*2.1);ctx.textAlign='start';
 if(stop.elapsed>0&&!stop.done){const nozzle=bodyPoint(sim.car,-1.93,.55);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(...nozzle);ctx.bezierCurveTo(nozzle[0]+k*.3,nozzle[1]+k*.5,bx-k*1.2,by,bx-k*.55,by-k*.3);ctx.strokeStyle='#203e39';ctx.lineWidth=k*.065;ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle='#8eb8a1';ctx.lineWidth=k*.018;ctx.stroke();vehicleText(sim.car,-.7,1.34,'ПЕРЕКАЧКА','#ecdea9',.11);}
 }
}
function gateScenery(){
 if(!sim)return;const k=scale;for(const gate of sim.gates){const x=sx(gate.x),y=sy(CP.height(gate.x,sim.roadId));if(x<-k*12||x>W+k*12)continue;
 for(let q=gate.x-2.8;q<gate.x+2.8;q+=.6)line(sx(q),sy(CP.groundHeight(q,sim.roadId))-.025*k,sx(q+.30),sy(CP.groundHeight(q+.3,sim.roadId))-.025*k,gate.open?'#95bd88':'#c99372',Math.max(2,k*.08));
 const px=sx(gate.pivot.x),py=sy(gate.pivot.y);line(px,sy(CP.height(gate.pivot.x,sim.roadId)),px,py,'#6d8069',k*.12);panel(gate.body,[[-1.6,-.095],[1.6,-.095],[1.6,.095],[-1.6,.095]],'#d3c89e');for(let q=-1.5;q<1.5;q+=.4)bpoly(gate.body,[[q,-.085],[q+.17,-.085],[q+.33,.085],[q+.16,.085]],'#a45f49');circle(px,py,k*.13,'#c5c7a4');

function updateTankerHud(){

 if(gate){$('gate-label').textContent=gate.name;$('gate-distance').textContent=gate.requested?'ЖДИ ЗЕЛЁНЫЙ СВЕТ':gate.elapsed>0?'ЗАПРОС ПРОЕЗДА '+Math.round(gate.elapsed*100)+'%':near&&gas?'ОТПУСТИ ГАЗ':near?'ДЕРЖИ ТОРМОЗ 1 С':Math.round(Math.abs(gate.x-x)*3)+' М ДО ПОСТА';$('gate-progress').style.width=(gate.requested?Math.min(100,-gate.joint.getJointAngle()/1.4*100):gate.elapsed*100)+'%';}

}

'use strict';
function vehicleMetal(body,m,top=1.2){const a=bodyPoint(body,0,top)[1],b=bodyPoint(body,0,-.3)[1],g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,a,0,Math.max(a+2,b));g.addColorStop(0,m.accent);g.addColorStop(.38,m.color);g.addColorStop(1,m.shadow||'#624f3d');return g;}
function panel(body,points,fill){bpoly(body,points,fill,'#122b30',Math.max(1.4,scale*.04));}
function bodyLine(body,x,y,a,b,color,width=.025){line(...bodyPoint(body,x,y),...bodyPoint(body,a,b),color,Math.max(1,scale*width));}
function vehicleText(body,x,y,text,color,size=.13){const p=bodyPoint(body,x,y);ctx.save();ctx.translate(...p);ctx.rotate(-pose(body).a);ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.font='900 '+Math.max(6,scale*size)+'px monospace';ctx.fillText(text,0,0);ctx.restore();}
function shock(body,wheel,off){
 const anchor=bodyPoint(body,off,.16),hub=[sx(pose(wheel).x),sy(pose(wheel).y)],dx=hub[0]-anchor[0],dy=hub[1]-anchor[1],length=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
 line(...bodyPoint(body,off-.22,-.06),...hub,'#142d31',scale*.09);line(...bodyPoint(body,off+.20,-.06),...hub,'#9aac9c',scale*.045);
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(...anchor);ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(dy,dx)-Math.PI/2);line(0,0,0,length,'#d1d2b3',scale*.055);line(0,length*.30,0,length*.76,'#b27837',scale*.095);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,length*.1);for(let i=0;i<=10;i++)ctx.lineTo((i%2?1:-1)*scale*.075,length*(.14+i*.06));ctx.strokeStyle='#edd697';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,scale*.022);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
}
function vehicleWheel(wheel,m){
 const p=pose(wheel),x=sx(p.x),y=sy(p.y),r=scale*m.radius;
 const rubber=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-r*.26,y-r*.34,r*.12,x,y,r);rubber.addColorStop(0,'#394747');rubber.addColorStop(.6,'#192a2e');rubber.addColorStop(.86,'#081e24');rubber.addColorStop(1,'#122e33');circle(x,y,r,rubber);
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(-p.a);for(let i=0;i<18;i++){ctx.save();ctx.rotate(i*Math.PI/9);ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'#667068':'#444e47';ctx.fillRect(-r*.075,-r*.99,r*.15,r*.12);ctx.restore();}
 circle(0,0,r*.71,'#0e252c');circle(0,0,r*.53,'#b8c4b3');circle(0,0,r*.42,'#2b454a');
 for(let i=0;i<6;i++){ctx.save();ctx.rotate(i*Math.PI/3);line(0,r*.10,0,r*.44,'#d7ddc7',Math.max(1.5,r*.11));circle(0,r*.25,r*.045,'#172f35');ctx.restore();}
 circle(0,0,r*.14,'#8eaa9c');circle(0,0,r*.055,'#e4cf99');ctx.restore();
 ctx.strokeStyle='#acb5a04d';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,scale*.016);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r*.85,Math.PI*1.15,Math.PI*1.75);ctx.stroke();
}

'use strict';
(function(root){
 function create({routes,profiles,zones,landmarks}){
  const key='water:22';
  const story={cargo:'Насос и шкаф управления',from:'Мастерская Лисьего Брода',to:'Водонапорная станция',radio:'Свет есть. Теперь нужна вода. Перед бродом остановись у поста: тормоз одну секунду. На насосной выгрузи насос — держи тормоз три секунды. Шкаф управления довези до водонапорной. Если мастерская запущена, её бригада поможет после брода.',reply:'Насос поставлю на место. Шкаф довезу сухим.',finish:'Насос запущен. В Лисьем Броде снова есть вода.'};
  const base={...routes[3],name:'Вода после шторма',kind:'careful',cargo:'waterkit',requires:'rover',boxes:2,length:200,reward:560,seed:62,weather:'clear',fuelPoints:[],sectors:[],gates:[{x:40,name:'ПОСТ У ПЕРЕПРАВЫ'}],fords:[{from:54,to:82,level:0,name:'ПОДТОПЛЕННАЯ ДОРОГА'}],stops:[{x:136,name:'УСТАНОВКА НАСОСА',cargoIndex:0,duration:3}],story};
  function route(support=false){return {...base,services:support?[{x:104,name:'ВЫЕЗДНАЯ БРИГАДА',required:false}]:[]};}
  routes[key]=route();profiles[key]=[[0,0],[20,0],[38,1.1],[48,1.1],[58,-.65],[76,-.65],[88,.5],[114,.5],[130,1.2],[151,1.2],[171,2.9],[180,2.9],[190,.2],[200,.2]];
  zones[key]=[{from:54,to:82,type:'wet',friction:.80},{from:91,to:110,type:'mud',friction:.72}];landmarks[key]=[{x:49,type:'sign',text:'БРОД · ТИХИЙ ХОД'},{x:124,type:'sign',text:'НАСОСНАЯ →'}];
  function init(s,options={}){s.roadId=key;s.workshopSupport=options.support===true;s.pump={integrity:100,previousVy:0,cooldown:0};return route(s.workshopSupport);}
  const methods={
   updatePump(dt){if(this.edition!==22)return;const box=this.boxes[0],m=this.pump;if(box.delivered||box.lost)return;const vy=box.body.getLinearVelocity().y,jolt=vy-m.previousVy;m.previousVy=vy;m.cooldown=Math.max(0,m.cooldown-dt);if(jolt>2.3&&m.cooldown<=0){const damage=Math.min(35,Math.round((jolt-2.3)*12));if(damage>0){m.integrity=Math.max(0,m.integrity-damage);m.cooldown=.45;this.events.push({type:'pumpHit',damage});}}},
   waterGoals(){const won=this.state==='win',full=this.remaining()===2&&this.pump.integrity>=95;return [{id:'delivery',title:'Вода в посёлке',detail:'Установи насос ≥70% и довези шкаф',done:won},{id:'cargo',title:'Целое оборудование',detail:'Насос ≥95%, оба груза доставлены',done:won&&full},{id:'careful',title:'Бережный рейс',detail:'Весь груз, насос ≥95%, кузов ≥70%',done:won&&full&&this.condition>=70}];}
  };
  return {key,story,route,init,methods};
 }
 if(typeof module==='object')module.exports=create;else root.WaterRunModel=create;
})(typeof globalThis!=='undefined'?globalThis:this);

function taskLabel(r){return r.cargo==='liquid'?'АВТОЦИСТЕРНА':r.cargo==='logs'?'ПЕРЕВОЗКА ЛЕСА':(r.drops||r.stops)?'РАЗВОЗКА':({delivery:'ДОСТАВКА',careful:'ХРУПКИЙ ГРУЗ',timed:'ЭКСПРЕСС',eco:'ЭКОНОМИЧНЫЙ РЕЙС',service:'РЕМОНТНАЯ СМЕНА',survey:'ПРОВЕРКА ДОРОГИ'})[r.kind];}
function cargoWord(r){return r.cargo==='liquid'?'л':r.cargo==='logs'?'бревна':'ящика';}
function selectWorkVehicle(){const r=CP.contractRoute(selected),m=currentVehicle();if(!r.requires){if(m.onlyCargo&&m.onlyCargo!==r.cargo){save.vehicle='rover';persist();}return true;}const needed=CP.vehicles.find(v=>v.id===r.requires);if(!save.owned.includes(r.requires)){garage();$('garage-message').textContent='Для этого заказа нужен '+needed.name+' / '+needed.tag+'. Выбери его в гараже.';return false;}if(save.vehicle!==r.requires){save.vehicle=r.requires;persist();}return true;}
function updateHomeWork(){const r=CP.contractRoute(selected);$('home-job-kind').textContent=taskLabel(r);$('home-weather').textContent=CP.weatherAt(selected).name;$('home-work-info').textContent=r.length*3+' м · +'+r.reward+' монет';}
function updateWorkHud(){
 document.querySelectorAll('.survey-pin').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('done',!!sim.checkpoints[+el.dataset.check]?.done));
 $('fuel-number').textContent=Math.ceil(sim.fuel)+'%';$('fuel-number').classList.toggle('danger',sim.fuel<20);$('weather-readout').textContent=CP.weatherAt(sim.id,sim.time).name;
 $('winch-count').textContent=sim.winchTime>0?'ТЯНЕМ ТРОС':'ЛЕБЁДКА ×'+sim.winchCharges;
 $('winch-button').disabled=mode!=='drive'||!sim.winchCharges||sim.winchTime>0||!sim.groundCount||Math.abs(sim.car.getLinearVelocity().x)>.85;
}
function orderDescription(r,id){let text=STORY.jobs[id].cargo+'. ';if(r.cargo==='liquid')text+='На базе ≥'+r.minLitres+' л. Нужна BRINE. ';if(r.cargo==='logs')text+='Нужен лесовоз LARCH. ';if(r.requires==='mule')text+='Нужен грузовик MULE. ';if(r.fords)text+='Брод: тихий ход. ';if(r.liftBridge)text+='Разводной мост: жди зелёный. ';if(r.gates)text+='Остановись у шлагбаума. ';if(r.rocks)text+='Подвижные камни. ';if(r.kind==='service')text+='Обязательный ремонт в пути. ';else if(r.services)text+='Есть мастерская. ';if((r.drops||r.stops)?.length)text+=(r.drops||r.stops).length+' точки и база. ';if(r.kind==='careful')text+='Сохрани весь груз.';else if(r.kind==='timed')text+='Успей за '+timeText(r.limit)+'.';else if(r.kind==='eco')text+='Оставь '+r.minFuel+'% в баке. Канистр нет. Совет: режим Эконом.';else if(r.kind==='survey')text+='Проверь все три поста.';else text+='Доставь груз на базу.';if(r.sectors?.length)text+=' '+r.sectors.length+' спуска: до 36 км/ч, без потери груза и жёстких посадок. Премия +'+r.sectors.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.reward,0)+'.';return text;}
function renderRoutes(){
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-region]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('selected',+b.dataset.region===region));
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('selected',b.dataset.filter===orderFilter));
 let ids=CP.routes.map((r,i)=>i).filter(i=>orderFilter==='all'?CP.routes[i].region===region:orderFilter==='logs'?CP.routes[i].cargo==='logs':orderFilter==='liquid'?CP.routes[i].cargo==='liquid':orderFilter==='stops'?!!(CP.contractRoute(i).drops||CP.contractRoute(i).stops)?.length:['timed','eco','survey','service'].includes(CP.routes[i].kind));
 if(ids.length&&!ids.includes(selected)){selected=ids.find(i=>routeUnlocked(i))??ids[0];save.selected=selected;persist();wallet();}
 $('order-count').textContent=ids.length+' РЕЙСОВ'+(ids.length>2?' · ЛИСТАЙ':'');
 $('route-grid').innerHTML=ids.map(i=>{const r=CP.contractRoute(i),open=routeUnlocked(i),weather=CP.weatherAt(i),needs=r.requires&&!save.owned.includes(r.requires);return '<button class="route-card '+(selected===i?'selected':'')+' '+(!open?'locked':'')+'" data-route="'+i+'" aria-pressed="'+(selected===i)+'" '+(!open?'disabled':'')+'><div class="route-art">'+routeArt(i)+'<span class="route-status">'+(!open?'ЗАКАЗ ЗАКРЫТ':needs?'НУЖЕН '+CP.vehicles.find(v=>v.id===r.requires).name:selected===i?'ВЫБРАН':'ДОСТУПЕН')+'</span><span class="route-challenge">'+taskLabel(r)+'</span></div><div class="route-body"><div class="route-top"><span class="route-num">'+weather.name+'</span><span class="route-difficulty">'+((r.hazards?save.stats[i].course26?.wins:save.stats[i].wins)?'✓ ПРИНЯТО':r.hazards&&open?'НОВЫЙ РЕЛЬЕФ':CP.regions[r.region].short)+'</span></div><h3>'+r.name+'</h3><p class="route-description">'+orderDescription(r,i)+'</p><div class="route-info"><span>'+icon('distance')+r.length*3+' м</span><span>'+icon(r.cargo==='liquid'?'fuel':r.cargo==='logs'?'tools':'cargo')+(r.litres||r.boxes)+' '+cargoWord(r)+'</span>'+(r.limit?'<span>'+icon('clock')+timeText(r.limit)+'</span>':'')+'</div><div class="route-reward"><span>+'+r.reward+' <small>ЗА РЕЙС</small></span>'+stars((r.hazards?save.stats[i].course26?.stars:save.stats[i].stars)||0)+'</div></div></button>';}).join('');
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-route]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{selected=+b.dataset.route;save.selected=selected;persist();wallet();beep(260);renderRoutes();});
 const focused=$('route-grid').querySelector('.route-card.selected');if(focused){const grid=$('route-grid'),a=focused.getBoundingClientRect(),b=grid.getBoundingClientRect();if(a.top<b.top||a.bottom>b.bottom)grid.scrollTop+=a.top-b.top;}
 const r=CP.contractRoute(selected),weather=CP.weatherAt(selected);
 $('selected-order-content').innerHTML='<div class="route-detail-art">'+routeArt(selected,true)+'<span>'+taskLabel(r)+'</span></div><div class="route-detail-heading"><span class="eyebrow">ВЫБРАННЫЙ ЗАКАЗ · '+weather.name+'</span><h3>'+r.name+'</h3></div><div class="route-detail-meta"><span>'+r.length*3+' м · '+(r.litres||r.boxes)+' '+cargoWord(r)+'</span><b>+'+r.reward+' за рейс</b></div><div class="route-detail-description" tabindex="0">'+orderDescription(r,selected)+'</div>';
 $('route-start').disabled=!routeUnlocked(selected);$('route-start').innerHTML=(r.requires&&!save.owned.includes(r.requires)?'В ГАРАЖ':'ПРИНЯТЬ ЗАКАЗ')+' '+icon('arrow');
}
function surveyHud(){const point=sim.checkpoints.find(p=>!p.done),x=sim.car.getPosition().x;visibility('stop-panel',true);$('stop-panel').classList.remove('docking');$('stop-panel').classList.toggle('complete',!point);$('stop-label').textContent=point?'КОНТРОЛЬ '+(sim.checkpoints.indexOf(point)+1)+' / '+sim.checkpoints.length:'ВСЕ ПОСТЫ ПРОВЕРЕНЫ';$('stop-distance').textContent=point?(x>point.x+6?'ВЕРНИСЬ · ':'')+Math.round(Math.abs(point.x-x)*3)+' М ДО ПОСТА':Math.round(Math.max(0,sim.route.length+6-x)*3)+' М ДО БАЗЫ';$('stop-progress').style.width=sim.checkpoints.filter(p=>p.done).length/sim.checkpoints.length*100+'%';}
// Handlers are attached after all game functions and DOM controls exist.
addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{orderFilter=b.dataset.filter;renderRoutes();});
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-region]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{orderFilter='all';region=+b.dataset.region;const id=CP.routes.findIndex((r,i)=>r.region===region&&routeUnlocked(i));selected=id>=0?id:CP.routes.findIndex(r=>r.region===region);save.selected=selected;persist();wallet();renderRoutes();});
 $('winch-button').onclick=()=>{if(mode==='drive'&&sim){if(sim.useWinch())hud();}};
});

'use strict';
function vehiclePull(m,levels){return m.torque*(1+levels[0]*.12)*(m.driven?m.driven.length/(m.axles?m.axles.length:2):1);}
function renderWorkshop(){
 const m=CP.preparedVehicle(save.vehicle,save.setups[save.vehicle]),l=currentLevels(),setup=CP.SETUPS[m.setup];
 $('workshop-summary').innerHTML='<button id="setup-open">Режим езды: '+setup.name.toLowerCase()+(CP.routes[selected].kind==='eco'&&m.setup!=='economy'?' · совет: эконом':'')+' '+icon('tools')+'</button>';
 $('setup-open').onclick=()=>{renderSetups();visibility('setup-dialog',true);};
}
function renderSetups(){
 $('setup-model').textContent=currentVehicle().name+' · текущая: '+CP.SETUPS[save.setups[save.vehicle]].name+(CP.routes[selected].kind==='eco'?' · Для этого заказа рекомендуется ЭКОНОМ':'');
 const descriptions={standard:'Заводской баланс скорости, тяги и расхода.',trail:'Тяга +7%, лучше сцепление и спокойнее подвеска. Скорость −6%, расход +7%.',economy:'Расход −18%, скорость −10%, тяга −4%. Мотор охлаждается немного быстрее.'};
 $('setup-options').innerHTML=Object.entries(CP.SETUPS).map(([id,t])=>'<button data-setup="'+id+'" class="setup-choice '+(save.setups[save.vehicle]===id?'selected':'')+'" aria-pressed="'+(save.setups[save.vehicle]===id)+'"><b>'+t.name+'</b><span>'+descriptions[id]+'</span><small>'+(save.setups[save.vehicle]===id?'УСТАНОВЛЕНО':'ВЫБРАТЬ БЕСПЛАТНО')+'</small></button>').join('');
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-setup]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{save.setups[save.vehicle]=b.dataset.setup;persist();renderSetups();upgrades();});
}
function updateRoadsideHud(){
 $('engine-heat').textContent='МОТОР '+Math.round(sim.temperature)+'°'+(sim.overheated?' · ОХЛАДИ':'');$('engine-heat').classList.toggle('danger',sim.overheated);$('engine-heat').classList.toggle('warm',sim.temperature>=85&&!sim.overheated);
 const site=sim.nextService(),x=sim.car.getPosition().x,near=site&&Math.abs(x-site.x)<=3;visibility('service-panel',!!site&&(site.required||Math.abs(x-site.x)<32));
 if(site){$('service-label').textContent=site.name+(site.required?' · ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНО':'');$('service-distance').textContent=site.elapsed>0?'РЕМОНТ '+Math.round(Math.min(100,site.elapsed/2.2*100))+'%':near&&gas?'ОТПУСТИ ГАЗ':near?'ДЕРЖИ ТОРМОЗ 2,2 С':(x>site.x+3?'ВЕРНИСЬ · ':'')+Math.round(Math.abs(site.x-x)*3)+' М ДО СЕРВИСА';$('service-progress').style.width=Math.min(100,site.elapsed/2.2*100)+'%';$('service-panel').classList.toggle('docking',!!near);}
 document.querySelectorAll('.service-pin').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('done',!!sim.services[+el.dataset.service]?.done));
}
function roadsideEvent(ev){
 if(ev.type==='service'){toast('РЕМОНТ +'+ev.repaired+'% · БАК +'+Math.round(ev.refilled)+'%');if(save.sound)RoadAudio.event('fuel');sayDriver('Машина обслужена. Можно ехать.','happy',true);}
 if(ev.type==='hot'){toast('МОТОР ПЕРЕГРЕТ · ОТПУСТИ ГАЗ');sayDriver('Мотору жарко. Дай ему свободный ход.','worried',true);}
 if(ev.type==='cooled')toast('МОТОР ОСТЫЛ · ТЯГА ВОССТАНОВЛЕНА');
}

package ru.rokiru.cargo;
import android.app.Activity;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.view.Display;
import android.webkit.WebView;
import android.webkit.WebViewClient;
import android.webkit.WebResourceRequest;
import android.webkit.WebResourceResponse;
import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo;
public class MainActivity extends Activity {
 private WebView web;
 private boolean activityPaused=false;
 private int cutLeft=0,cutTop=0,cutRight=0,cutBottom=0;
 private void sendInsets(){if(web==null)return;float density=getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;web.evaluateJavascript("window.androidInsets && window.androidInsets("+(cutLeft/density)+","+(cutTop/density)+","+(cutRight/density)+","+(cutBottom/density)+")",null);}
 public static class SaveStore {
  private final android.content.SharedPreferences prefs;
  SaveStore(android.content.Context context){prefs=context.getSharedPreferences("dustrivo_saves_v1",android.content.Context.MODE_PRIVATE);}
  private boolean allowed(String key){return "cargo-save-v1".equals(key)||"cargo-career-v1".equals(key)||"cargo-active-profile".equals(key);}
  @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface public String read(String key){return allowed(key)?prefs.getString(key,""):"";}
  @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface public boolean write(String key,String value){return allowed(key)&&value!=null&&value.length()<=2000000&&prefs.edit().putString(key,value).commit();}
 }

 public final class DriveFeedback {
  private long lastPulse=0;
  @android.webkit.JavascriptInterface public void pulse(final String kind){
   if(!"landing".equals(kind)&&!"impact".equals(kind))return;
   runOnUiThread(new Runnable(){@Override public void run(){
    if(activityPaused||web==null||!web.hasWindowFocus())return;
    long now=android.os.SystemClock.uptimeMillis();if(now-lastPulse<500)return;lastPulse=now;
    web.performHapticFeedback("landing".equals(kind)?android.view.HapticFeedbackConstants.VIRTUAL_KEY:android.view.HapticFeedbackConstants.LONG_PRESS);
   }});
  }
 }

 private void immersive(){getWindow().getDecorView().setSystemUiVisibility(View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_IMMERSIVE_STICKY|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_FULLSCREEN|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_HIDE_NAVIGATION|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_STABLE|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_FULLSCREEN|View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_HIDE_NAVIGATION);}
 @Override public void onCreate(Bundle b){super.onCreate(b);setRequestedOrientation(ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE);getWindow().addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON);getWindow().setFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_FULLSCREEN,WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_FULLSCREEN);immersive();
 if(android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=28){WindowManager.LayoutParams p=getWindow().getAttributes();p.layoutInDisplayCutoutMode=WindowManager.LayoutParams.LAYOUT_IN_DISPLAY_CUTOUT_MODE_SHORT_EDGES;getWindow().setAttributes(p);}
 Display d=getWindowManager().getDefaultDisplay();Display.Mode best=d.getMode();for(Display.Mode m:d.getSupportedModes())if(m.getPhysicalWidth()==best.getPhysicalWidth()&&m.getPhysicalHeight()==best.getPhysicalHeight()&&Math.abs(m.getRefreshRate()-60f)<Math.abs(best.getRefreshRate()-60f))best=m;WindowManager.LayoutParams wp=getWindow().getAttributes();wp.preferredDisplayModeId=best.getModeId();wp.preferredRefreshRate=60f;getWindow().setAttributes(wp);
 web=new WebView(this);web.setBackgroundColor(0xff20382a);web.getSettings().setJavaScriptEnabled(true);web.getSettings().setDomStorageEnabled(true);web.getSettings().setAllowFileAccess(true);web.getSettings().setAllowContentAccess(false);web.getSettings().setMediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture(false);web.getSettings().setSupportZoom(false);web.getSettings().setBuiltInZoomControls(false);web.setOverScrollMode(View.OVER_SCROLL_NEVER);web.addJavascriptInterface(new SaveStore(this),"AndroidGameStore");web.setHapticFeedbackEnabled(true);web.addJavascriptInterface(new DriveFeedback(),"AndroidDriveFeedback");
 web.setOnApplyWindowInsetsListener(new View.OnApplyWindowInsetsListener(){@Override public android.view.WindowInsets onApplyWindowInsets(View view,android.view.WindowInsets insets){if(android.os.Build.VERSION.SDK_INT>=28){android.view.DisplayCutout cut=insets.getDisplayCutout();cutLeft=cut==null?0:cut.getSafeInsetLeft();cutTop=cut==null?0:cut.getSafeInsetTop();cutRight=cut==null?0:cut.getSafeInsetRight();cutBottom=cut==null?0:cut.getSafeInsetBottom();sendInsets();}return insets;}});
 web.setWebViewClient(new WebViewClient(){@Override public void onPageFinished(WebView v,String url){sendInsets();}@Override public boolean shouldOverrideUrlLoading(WebView v,WebResourceRequest r){return !r.getUrl().toString().startsWith("file:///android_asset/");}@Override public WebResourceResponse shouldInterceptRequest(WebView v,WebResourceRequest r){if(!r.getUrl().toString().startsWith("file:///android_asset/"))return new WebResourceResponse("text/plain","UTF-8",new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]));return null;}});setContentView(web);web.loadUrl("file:///android_asset/index.html");}
 @Override public void onWindowFocusChanged(boolean f){super.onWindowFocusChanged(f);if(f)immersive();else if(web!=null)web.evaluateJavascript("window.androidPause && window.androidPause()",null);}
 @Override protected void onPause(){activityPaused=true;if(web!=null){final WebView target=web;target.evaluateJavascript("window.androidPause && window.androidPause()",new android.webkit.ValueCallback<String>(){public void onReceiveValue(String value){if(activityPaused&&web==target)target.pauseTimers();}});target.onPause();}super.onPause();}
 @Override protected void onResume(){super.onResume();activityPaused=false;if(web!=null){web.onResume();web.resumeTimers();web.evaluateJavascript("window.androidResume && window.androidResume()",null);}immersive();}
 @Override public void onBackPressed(){web.evaluateJavascript("window.androidBack ? window.androidBack() : false",new android.webkit.ValueCallback<String>(){ public void onReceiveValue(String v){if("false".equals(v))finish();}});}
 @Override protected void onDestroy(){if(web!=null){web.stopLoading();web.destroy();web=null;}super.onDestroy();}
}

FILE: project/scripts/bootstrap.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Install verified official Android and Gradle archives into the checkout's ignored .tools."""
import hashlib, os, pathlib, subprocess, tarfile, urllib.request, xml.etree.ElementTree as ET, zipfile
ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
TOOLS = ROOT / '.tools'
TOOLS.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
os.environ['GRADLE_USER_HOME'] = str(TOOLS / 'gradle-home')
os.environ['ANDROID_USER_HOME'] = str(TOOLS / 'android-user')

def download(url, dest, digest=None, algorithm='sha256'):
    if not dest.exists():
        print('Downloading', dest.name, flush=True)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=120) as response, open(dest, 'wb') as output:
            while chunk := response.read(1024 * 1024): output.write(chunk)
    if digest and hashlib.new(algorithm, dest.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != digest:
        dest.unlink()
        raise RuntimeError('Archive checksum verification failed')

jdk = TOOLS / 'jdk-21.0.8'
if not (jdk / 'bin/javac').exists():
    url = 'https://download.oracle.com/java/21/archive/jdk-21.0.8_linux-x64_bin.tar.gz'
    with urllib.request.urlopen(url + '.sha256', timeout=60) as r: digest = r.read().decode().strip()
    dest = TOOLS / 'jdk-21.0.8.tar.gz'
    download(url, dest, digest)
    with tarfile.open(dest) as archive: archive.extractall(TOOLS, filter='data')
os.environ['JAVA_HOME'] = str(jdk)
os.environ['PATH'] = str(jdk / 'bin') + os.pathsep + os.environ['PATH']
sdk = TOOLS / 'android-sdk'
manager = sdk / 'cmdline-tools/19.0/bin/sdkmanager'
if not manager.exists():
    with urllib.request.urlopen('https://dl.google.com/android/repository/repository2-1.xml', timeout=60) as r:
        repo = ET.fromstring(r.read())
    package = next(p for p in repo if p.attrib.get('path') == 'cmdline-tools;19.0')
    archive = next(a for a in package.find('archives') if a.findtext('host-os') == 'linux')
    complete = archive.find('complete')
    checksum = complete.find('checksum')
    dest = TOOLS / 'android-commandline-19.zip'
    download('https://dl.google.com/android/repository/' + complete.findtext('url'), dest,
             checksum.text, checksum.attrib.get('type', 'sha1'))
    staging = TOOLS / 'android-commandline-19'
    with zipfile.ZipFile(dest) as z: z.extractall(staging)
    manager.parent.parent.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (staging / 'cmdline-tools').rename(manager.parent.parent)
    for directory in [manager.parent, manager.parent.parent / 'lib']:
        for p in directory.rglob('*'):
            if p.is_file() and p.suffix not in ['.jar', '.xml']: p.chmod(p.stat().st_mode | 0o111)
    manager.chmod(0o755)
if not (sdk / 'platforms/android-35/android.jar').exists() or not (sdk / 'build-tools/35.0.0/apksigner').exists():
    subprocess.run([str(manager), '--sdk_root=' + str(sdk), '--licenses'], input='y\n' * 100, text=True, check=True,
                   stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    subprocess.run([str(manager), '--sdk_root=' + str(sdk), 'platforms;android-35', 'build-tools;35.0.0', 'platform-tools'], check=True)
gradle = TOOLS / 'gradle-8.13/bin/gradle'
if not gradle.exists():
    url = 'https://services.gradle.org/distributions/gradle-8.13-bin.zip'
    with urllib.request.urlopen(url + '.sha256', timeout=60) as r: digest = r.read().decode().strip()
    dest = TOOLS / 'gradle.zip'
    download(url, dest, digest)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(dest) as z: z.extractall(TOOLS)
    gradle.chmod(0o755)
(ROOT / 'local.properties').write_text('sdk.dir=' + str(sdk) + '\n')
print('JDK 21.0.8, Android SDK 35 and Gradle 8.13 ready.', flush=True)
