#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
if [ ! -x .tools/gradle-8.13/bin/gradle ] || [ ! -x .tools/jdk-21.0.8/bin/javac ] || [ ! -f local.properties ]; then
  python3 scripts/bootstrap.py
fi
export GRADLE_USER_HOME="$PWD/.tools/gradle-home"
export ANDROID_USER_HOME="$PWD/.tools/android-user"
export ANDROID_HOME="$PWD/.tools/android-sdk"
node tests/verify-expedition18.cjs
node tests/verify-contracts20.cjs
node tests/verify-first-light21.cjs
node tests/verify-water-run22.cjs
node tests/verify-recovery23.cjs
node tests/verify-opening24.cjs
node tests/verify-ridge25.cjs
node tests/verify-courses26.cjs
node tests/verify-driving27.cjs
node tests/verify-ridge28.cjs
DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE=${DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE:-auto}
if [ "$DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE" = auto ]; then
  if [ -f .tools/dustrivo-signing/signing.properties ]; then DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE=release; else DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE=debug; fi
fi
case "$DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE" in
 release)
  if [ ! -f .tools/dustrivo-signing/signing.properties ]; then
    printf 'Release signing is not configured. Supply the signing key locally or use DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE=debug for a separate development build.\n' >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  python3 scripts/gradle.py --no-daemon --max-workers=2 :app:assembleRelease :app:lintRelease
  DUSTRIVO_APK_SOURCE=app/build/outputs/apk/release/app-release.apk
  ;;
 debug)
  python3 scripts/gradle.py --no-daemon --max-workers=2 :app:assembleDebug :app:lintDebug
  DUSTRIVO_APK_SOURCE=app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
  ;;
 *) printf 'Unknown DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE: %s\n' "$DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
export DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE
mkdir -p artifacts
cp "$DUSTRIVO_APK_SOURCE" artifacts/Dustrivo-0.28.apk
.tools/android-sdk/build-tools/35.0.0/apksigner verify --verbose artifacts/Dustrivo-0.28.apk
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import zipfile, subprocess, os
apk=Path('artifacts/Dustrivo-0.28.apk')
badging=subprocess.check_output(['.tools/android-sdk/build-tools/35.0.0/aapt','dump','badging',str(apk)],text=True)
assert "name='ru.rokiru.cargo' versionCode='29' versionName='0.28'" in badging
if os.environ['DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE']=='release':
    assert 'application-debuggable' not in badging, 'Release APK must not be debuggable'
    cert=subprocess.check_output(['.tools/android-sdk/build-tools/35.0.0/apksigner','verify','--print-certs',str(apk)],text=True)
    assert '3b5dab139957cb61bef40f2d0c98c97a0498f57e5683118a0b20066863a6e1e1' in cert, 'Signing identity changed; existing installs must remain updatable'
print('Verified Android metadata; build type: '+os.environ['DUSTRIVO_BUILD_TYPE'])
with zipfile.ZipFile(apk) as z:
    assert z.testzip() is None, 'APK CRC failure'
    for source in Path('game/assets').rglob('*'):
        if source.is_file():
            assert z.read('assets/'+source.relative_to('game/assets').as_posix())==source.read_bytes(), 'APK asset mismatch: '+str(source)
print('All bundled assets match the tested game.')
PY
sha256sum artifacts/Dustrivo-0.28.apk > artifacts/Dustrivo-0.28.apk.sha256
printf 'APK: %s/artifacts/Dustrivo-0.28.apk\n' "$PWD"



==============================================================================
FILE: project/scripts/gradle.py
==============================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run pinned Gradle through the machine's existing proxy and verified CA trust."""
import os, pathlib, shutil, subprocess, sys
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
ROOT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
TOOLS=ROOT/'.tools'
os.environ['GRADLE_USER_HOME']=str(TOOLS/'gradle-home')
os.environ['ANDROID_USER_HOME']=str(TOOLS/'android-user')
os.environ['ANDROID_HOME']=str(TOOLS/'android-sdk')
os.environ['JAVA_HOME']=str(TOOLS/'jdk-21.0.8')
os.environ['PATH']=str(TOOLS/'jdk-21.0.8/bin')+os.pathsep+os.environ['PATH']
(TOOLS/'java-user').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
props=['-Duser.home='+str(TOOLS/'java-user')]
for protocol in ['https','http']:
    proxy=os.getenv(protocol.upper()+'_PROXY') or os.getenv(protocol+'_proxy')
    if proxy:
        parsed=urlsplit(proxy)
        if parsed.username or parsed.password:
            raise RuntimeError('Authenticated proxy needs platform-supported Java configuration; never persist credentials.')
        props.extend(['-D'+protocol+'.proxyHost='+parsed.hostname,
                      '-D'+protocol+'.proxyPort='+str(parsed.port or 80)])
cert=os.getenv('CODEX_PROXY_CERT')
if cert and pathlib.Path(cert).is_file():
    java=pathlib.Path(shutil.which('java')).resolve().parents[1]
    trust=TOOLS/'java-truststore'
    if not trust.exists():
        shutil.copyfile(java/'lib/security/cacerts',trust)
        subprocess.run(['keytool','-importcert','-noprompt','-trustcacerts','-alias','iskra-platform-ca',
                        '-file',cert,'-keystore',str(trust),'-storepass','changeit'],check=True)
    props.extend(['-Djavax.net.ssl.trustStore='+str(trust),'-Djavax.net.ssl.trustStorePassword=changeit'])
result=subprocess.run([str(TOOLS/'gradle-8.13/bin/gradle'),*props,*sys.argv[1:]],cwd=ROOT)
sys.exit(result.returncode)

   if(lastCamp>=2){const held=restore(s);held.ridgeContinue();for(let k=0;k<9000&&held.state==='drive'&&!held.ridge.waiting;k++)held.step(true,false);reckless.push({camp:lastCamp,state:held.state,reason:held.reason,condition:held.condition,cargo:held.remaining()});if(lastCamp===3){assert.equal(held.state,'fail');assert.equal(held.ridgeScore(),0,'last safe bank is at stake');}}

   const d=C.capture(s,P);for(const change of [d=>d.ridge28.edition=29,d=>d.ridge28.camp=4,d=>d.ridge28.camp=0,d=>d.ridge28.unknown=true,d=>delete d.ridge25,d=>d.ridge25.awardX=Infinity,d=>d.course26={edition:26}]){const bad=structuredClone(d);change(bad);assert.equal(C.validate(bad,P),null);}

  s.step(...input(s,P));

const assert=require('node:assert/strict'),fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),P=require('../game/assets/physics.js'),C=require('../game/assets/run-checkpoint.js'),input=require('./driving-input26.cjs');

const rows=[];let airRestore=false,roughRestore=false,fastClean=false;

 const s=make(id);let controls=[false,false],stall=0,restored=false,twin=null,twinSteps=0;
 for(let n=0;n<18000&&s.state==='drive';n++){
  if(n%12===0||s.forkWaiting){controls=input(s,P);if(s.edition!==23&&s.groundCount&&Math.abs(s.car.getLinearVelocity().x)<.12&&controls[0])stall+=.2;else stall=0;if(stall>1.6){s.useWinch();stall=0;}}
  if(twin&&twin.lowGear!==s.lowGear)assert.ok(twin.setLowGear(s.lowGear));
  s.step(...controls);
  if(twin){twin.step(...controls);if(++twinSteps===120){assert.equal(s.state,twin.state);assert.equal(s.driving27.landings,twin.driving27.landings);assert.equal(s.driving27.landingBonus,twin.driving27.landingBonus);assert.ok(Math.abs(s.car.getPosition().x-twin.car.getPosition().x)<.05,'air warm trajectory');twin=null;}}
  if(!restored&&s.state==='drive'&&s.car.getPosition().x>s.route.hazards[0].from+8){checkpoint(s);restored=true;}
  if(id===1&&!airRestore&&s.driving27.airTime>.20&&s.state==='drive'){
   airRestore=true;twin=checkpoint(s);
  }
 }
 assert.equal(s.state,'win','route '+id+' '+s.reason);assert.ok(restored);assert.ok(s.driving27.sectors.every(x=>!x.reason),'careful driver clean '+id+' '+JSON.stringify(s.driving27.sectors));assert.ok(s.sectors.every(x=>x.passed),'all sectors '+id);assert.ok(s.driving27.landingBonus>=s.driving27.landings*5&&s.driving27.landingBonus<=s.driving27.landings*20,'bonus counts aboard cargo '+id);
 rows.push({id,time:+s.time.toFixed(2),condition:s.condition,stars:s.rating(),landings:s.driving27.landings,landingBonus:s.driving27.landingBonus,sectors:s.driving27.sectors});console.log('DELIVERED skill27',id,s.rating(),s.driving27.landingBonus);
}
assert.ok(airRestore,'warm save during real jump');
for(const id of [1,5]){const s=make(id);for(let n=0;n<9000&&s.state==='drive';n++){
 s.step(true,false);if(s.sectors.some(x=>x.passed&&x.maxSpeed*10.8>60))fastClean=true;
 if(id===5&&!roughRestore&&s.state==='drive'&&s.driving27.sectors.some(x=>x.reason)){
  roughRestore=true;const r=checkpoint(s);for(let k=0;k<90&&s.state==='drive';k++){s.step(true,false);r.step(true,false);}assert.deepEqual(r.driving27.sectors.map(x=>x.reason),s.driving27.sectors.map(x=>x.reason));assert.deepEqual(r.sectors.map(x=>x.passed),s.sectors.map(x=>x.passed));assert.equal(r.coins,s.coins);
 }
}if(id===1){assert.equal(s.reason,'damage');assert.equal(s.driving27.incident.kind,'impact');assert.ok(s.driving27.incident.speed>60);}else{assert.equal(s.state,'win');assert.equal(s.remaining(),s.boxes.length);assert.ok(s.driving27.sectors.some(x=>x.reason==='sway'));assert.equal(s.rating(),2);}}
assert.ok(fastClean,'smooth actual fast sector earns bonus without arbitrary speed cap');assert.ok(roughRestore,'rough sector saved before cargo loss');
// Observation must never alter body forces, transmission, cargo or fuel.
for(const id of [1,28,41]){const a=make(id),r=P.contractRoute(id),b=new P.Drive(id,[0,0,0],r.requires||'rover','standard',P.courses26.edition(id),{course:26,support:true,recoveryBonus:true});for(let n=0;n<2000&&a.state==='drive';n++){const c=input(a,P),other=input(b,P);assert.deepEqual(c,other);a.step(...c);b.step(...other);}assert.deepEqual(a.car.getPosition(),b.car.getPosition());assert.equal(a.condition,b.condition);assert.equal(a.fuel,b.fuel);assert.equal(a.remaining(),b.remaining());}
const live=make(1);for(let n=0;n<120;n++)live.step(.5,false);const d=C.capture(live,P);assert.ok(d);

'use strict';
function create(base){return function(s,P,choice='continue'){
 if(s.edition!==25)return base(s,P);
 if(s.ridge.waiting){if(choice==='cash')s.ridgeDeliver();else s.ridgeContinue();return [false,false];}
 const p=s.car.getPosition(),v=s.car.getLinearVelocity(),a=Math.atan2(Math.sin(s.car.getAngle()),Math.cos(s.car.getAngle())),w=s.car.getAngularVelocity();if(s.ridge.waiting)s.ridgeContinue();if(p.x>38&&p.x<47&&s.groundCount)return [.65,false];if(s.groundCount<2){const target=p.x>=47&&p.x<52?.25:s.groundCount?Math.atan((P.height(p.x+2,s.roadId)-P.height(p.x,s.roadId))/2):v.y<0?-.18:.12,d=target-a-w*.23;return d>.055?[true,false]:d<-.055?[false,true]:[false,false];}const grade=(P.height(p.x+3,s.roadId)-P.height(p.x,s.roadId))/3;let target=s.ridge.load===3?6.5:5.5;if(p.x>38&&p.x<47)target=7.5;if(grade<-.16)target=3.0;if(v.x>target+.25)return [false,true];if(v.x>target-.2)return [false,false];return [Math.min(1,.45+(target-v.x)*.25+Math.max(0,grade)*.5),false];
};}

'use strict';
function create(base){return function(s,P,path='detour'){
 if(!s.course26)return base(s,P,path);
 if(s.forkWaiting)s.choosePath(path);
 const x=s.car.getPosition().x,v=s.car.getLinearVelocity(),a=Math.atan2(Math.sin(s.car.getAngle()),Math.cos(s.car.getAngle())),w=s.car.getAngularVelocity();
 const grade=(P.height(x+3,s.roadId)-P.height(x,s.roadId))/3;
 const hazard=s.route.hazards.find(h=>x>h.from-8&&x<h.to+2),bridge=P.bridges[s.roadId],gap=P.gaps[s.roadId],ford=(s.route.fords||[]).find(f=>x>f.from-5&&x<f.to+4),rocks=(s.route.rocks||[]).some(r=>x>r.x-8&&x<r.x+10);
 const low=!(gap&&x>gap.from-19&&x<gap.to+7)&&(!!ford||!!bridge&&x>bridge.from-8&&x<bridge.to+5||rocks||grade>.19||P.surfaceAt(x,s.roadId).type==='mud'||!!hazard&&['c','d'].includes(hazard.type)&&x<hazard.peak+3);
 if(s.groundCount&&Math.abs(v.x)<3.3&&s.shiftCooldown<=0&&low!==s.lowGear)s.setLowGear(low);
 if(s.edition===21&&s.path==='ridge'&&hazard?.type==='c'&&x>hazard.from+5&&x<hazard.peak+5&&s.lowGear&&Math.abs(v.x)<.45&&s.winchTime<=0)s.useWinch();
 if(s.edition===23){if(!s.tow.attached){if(s.canToggleTow())s.useWinch();else return [false,true];}if(s.canDeliverRecovery()){s.useWinch();return [false,true];}}
 if(s.groundCount===0){const target=v.y<0?-.10:.10,d=target-a-w*.22;return d>.045?[true,false]:d<-.045?[false,true]:[false,false];}
 let target=s.edition===23?2.4:s.edition===21?3:s.edition===22?3.3:s.fluidCapacity?2.9:s.route.cargo==='logs'?3.5:s.route.kind==='careful'?3.6:5.4;
 let drop=0;for(let n=2;n<=10;n+=2)drop=Math.min(drop,(P.height(x+n+2,s.roadId)-P.height(x+n,s.roadId))/2);
 if(drop<-.45)target=Math.min(target,s.edition===23?1.3:s.dispatchTerms?2.05:1.65);else if(drop<-.16||grade<-.12)target=Math.min(target,s.edition===23?1.6:2.4);
 if(low)target=Math.min(target,s.dispatchTerms?3.3:3.0);if(rocks)target=Math.min(target,1.7);if(bridge&&x>bridge.from-7&&x<bridge.to+6)target=Math.min(target,1.3);if(ford)target=1.5;
 const sector=s.sectors.find(h=>!h.done&&x>h.from-8&&x<h.to+1);if(sector)target=Math.min(target,2.7);
 const gate=s.nextGate(),lift=s.nextLiftBridge(),stop=s.nextStop(),service=s.nextService(),tasks=[...(gate?[{...gate,task:'gate'}]:[]),...(lift?[{...lift,x:lift.control,task:'lift'}]:[]),...(stop?[{...stop,task:'stop'}]:[]),...(service&&(service.required||service.x>=x-3)&&(service.required||s.condition<65||s.fuel<25||s.temperature>101)?[{...service,task:'service'}]:[])].sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);
 const task=tasks[0];if(task&&x>task.x-13){if(x>=task.x-2.2)return [false,true];target=Math.min(target,Math.max(.7,(task.x-x-1.8)*.40));}
 if(s.edition===23){const delta=s.route.length-s.recovery.body.getPosition().x;if(delta<16)target=Math.min(target,Math.max(.45,delta*.18));if(delta<1.3)return [false,true];}
 if(gap&&x>gap.from-15&&x<gap.from-.15)target=6.8+(s.delivered&&s.delivered()>0?.4:0);
 if(gap&&x>=gap.from-.15&&x<gap.to+3){if(s.groundCount&&x<gap.from+s.vehicle.base)return [true,false];const d=.12-a-w*.2;return d>0?[true,false]:[false,true];}
 if((s.route.kind==='eco'||s.route.length>450||s.fuel<22)&&!hazard&&!task&&Math.abs(grade)<.15&&drop>-.15)return [v.x<2.6,v.x>4.8];
 if(v.x>target+.16)return [false,true];if(v.x>target-.12)return [false,false];
 return [Math.max(.25,Math.min(1,.4+(target-v.x)*.35+Math.max(0,grade)*.9)),false];
};}
const base=require('./driving-input25.cjs'),driver=create(base);driver.browserSource='('+create.toString()+')('+base.browserSource+')';module.exports=driver;
